from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class UserProfile(models.Model):
    ROLE_CHOICES = [
        ('superadmin',  _('Super Administrator')),
        ('tenant_admin', _('Tenant Admin (krovna firma)')),
        ('company_admin', _('Admin tvrtke')),
        ('project_manager', _('Voditelj projekta')),
        ('worker',      _('Radnik')),
        ('investor',    _('Investitor')),
        ('partner',     _('Partner / Klijent')),
    ]
    user = models.OneToOneField(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='profile')
    role = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=ROLE_CHOICES,
                            default='worker', verbose_name=_('Uloga'))
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'))
    profile_image = models.ImageField(upload_to='profiles/', blank=True,
                                      verbose_name=_('Profilna slika'))
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Telefon'))
    department = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Odjel'))
    totp_secret = models.CharField(max_length=32, blank=True, verbose_name='TOTP Secret')
    totp_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='2FA aktiviran')
    google_id = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    microsoft_id = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    preferred_language = models.CharField(
        max_length=5, default='hr',
        choices=[('hr', 'Hrvatski'), ('de', 'Deutsch'), ('en', 'English')],
        verbose_name=_('Jezik sučelja')
    )
    can_upload_photos = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Može uploadati slike'))
    can_view_financials = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Vidi financije'))
    can_approve_timesheets = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Odobrava radne sate'))
    # Web Push subscription (VAPID)
    push_subscription   = models.TextField(blank=True, default='',
                                            verbose_name='Push subscription JSON')
    must_change_password = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_("Mora promijeniti lozinku"))

    two_factor_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    two_factor_secret = models.CharField(max_length=32, blank=True)
    branch       = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.CompanyBranch',
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='users',
        verbose_name=_('Podružnica'),
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    last_login_ip = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'core_userprofile'
        verbose_name = _('Korisnički profil')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Korisnički profili')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.get_full_name() or self.user.username} [{self.get_role_display()}]"

    @property
    def is_superadmin(self):
        return self.role == 'superadmin' or self.user.is_superuser

    @property
    def is_tenant_admin(self):
        return self.role == 'tenant_admin'

    def get_visible_companies(self):
        """Vrati firme koje ovaj korisnik smije vidjeti.
        superadmin (is_superuser) = SVE
        tenant_admin = umbrella children + grandchildren (rekurzivno)
        ostali = samo vlastita firma
        """
        from companies.models import Company
        if self.user.is_superuser and self.role == 'superadmin':
            return Company.objects.all()
        if self.role == 'tenant_admin' and self.company:
            # Rekurzivno: vlastita + children + grandchildren (max 3 razine)
            ids = set()
            ids.add(self.company.pk)
            # Level 1: direct children
            level1 = set(Company.objects.filter(umbrella_parent=self.company).values_list('pk', flat=True))
            ids.update(level1)
            # Level 2: grandchildren
            if level1:
                level2 = set(Company.objects.filter(umbrella_parent__in=level1).values_list('pk', flat=True))
                ids.update(level2)
                # Level 3: great-grandchildren
                if level2:
                    level3 = set(Company.objects.filter(umbrella_parent__in=level2).values_list('pk', flat=True))
                    ids.update(level3)
            return Company.objects.filter(pk__in=ids)
        if self.company:
            return Company.objects.filter(pk=self.company.pk)
        return Company.objects.none()

    @property
    def is_company_admin(self):
        return self.role in ('superadmin', 'company_admin') or self.user.is_staff

    @property
    def is_manager(self):
        return self.role in ('superadmin', 'company_admin', 'project_manager')

    @property
    def dashboard_url(self):
        URLS = {
            'superadmin': '/superadmin/',
            'company_admin': '/dashboard/company/',
            'project_manager': '/dashboard/manager/',
            'worker': '/dashboard/worker/',
            'investor': '/dashboard/investor/',
            'partner': '/dashboard/partner/',
        }
        return URLS.get(self.role, '/')




class ImpersonationLog(models.Model):
    """Audit trail for every superadmin impersonation session."""
    superadmin  = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='impersonation_sessions',
        verbose_name='Superadmin'
    )
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='impersonation_logs', verbose_name='Tvrtka'
    )
    started_at    = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name='Početak')
    ended_at      = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Kraj')
    ip_address    = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='IP adresa')
    actions_count = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Broj akcija')

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-started_at']
        verbose_name = 'Log impersonacije'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Logovi impersonacija'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.superadmin.username} → {self.company.name} @ {self.started_at:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M}"

    @property
    def duration_display(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        end = self.ended_at or timezone.now()
        secs = int((end - self.started_at).total_seconds())
        h, m, s = secs // 3600, (secs % 3600) // 60, secs % 60
        return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"



class CompanyMembership(models.Model):
    """Links a user to a company with a specific role.
    Enables one user to belong to multiple companies."""
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='company_memberships')
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='memberships'
    )
    role = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=UserProfile.ROLE_CHOICES, default='worker')
    is_default = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    joined_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('user', 'company')
        db_table = 'accounts_companymembership'
        verbose_name = 'Membership'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Memberships'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} @ {self.company.name} [{self.get_role_display()}]"


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# DeviceToken — Mobilni barcode login (mobitel = čip kartica)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

import uuid as _uuid
import hashlib as _hashlib


class DeviceToken(models.Model):
    """
    Token za login bez lozinke — korisnik sprema barcode u mobitel,
    skenira ga na POS terminalu kamerom → instant prijava.
    Ako POS radi na istom mobitelu → auto-login bez skeniranja.
    """
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='device_tokens', verbose_name=_('Korisnik'),
    )
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='device_tokens', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    token = models.UUIDField(default=_uuid.uuid4, unique=True, editable=False)
    # Barcode data: LC-DEV-{short_hash} — kratko za skeniranje
    barcode_data = models.CharField(
        max_length=30, unique=True, editable=False,
        verbose_name=_('Barcode podatak'),
    )
    device_name = models.CharField(
        max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Naziv uređaja'),
        help_text=_('npr. "Moj iPhone", "Samsung tablet blagajna"'),
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivan'))
    # Auto POS login: ako je True, browser s ovim tokenom u localStorage
    # automatski se logira bez skeniranja
    auto_pos_login = models.BooleanField(
        default=True, verbose_name=_('Auto POS login'),
        help_text=_('Automatski prijavi na POS bez skeniranja barkoda'),
    )
    # Opcionalno: cashier ID za direktan cashier-switch nakon logina
    cashier = models.ForeignKey(
        'pos.POSCashier', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('POS blagajnik'),
        help_text=_('Ako je postavljeno, odmah aktivira ovog blagajnika'),
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    last_used_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    last_ip = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Device token')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Device tokeni')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return '%s — %s (%s)' % (
            self.user.get_full_name() or self.user.username,
            self.device_name or 'Uređaj',
            'aktivan' if self.is_active else 'deaktiviran',
        )

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.barcode_data:
            # Generiraj kratki barcode: LC-DEV-XXXXXXXX
            short = _hashlib.sha256(self.token.bytes).hexdigest()[:8].upper()
            self.barcode_data = 'LC-DEV-%s' % short
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

=== ./work_orders/models.py ===
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from core.managers import CompanyManager


class WorkOrder(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft',       _('Nacrt')),
        ('in_progress', _('U tijeku')),
        ('completed',   _('Završen')),
        ('invoiced',    _('Fakturiran')),
        ('cancelled',   _('Otkazan')),
    ]
    PRIORITY_CHOICES = [
        ('low',    _('Nisko')),
        ('normal', _('Normalno')),
        ('high',   _('Visoko')),
        ('urgent', _('Hitno')),
    ]

    company         = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects         = CompanyManager()

    order_number    = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Broj naloga'))
    date            = models.DateField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('Datum'))
    status          = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft', verbose_name=_('Status'))
    client          = models.ForeignKey(
        'partners.Partner', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='work_orders', verbose_name=_('Klijent'),
    )
    assigned_to     = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='assigned_work_orders', verbose_name=_('Dodijeljen'),
    )
    description     = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis radova'))
    notes           = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomene'))
    priority        = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=PRIORITY_CHOICES, default='normal', verbose_name=_('Prioritet'))
    estimated_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Procijenjeni sati'))
    actual_hours    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Stvarni sati'))
    scheduled_date  = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Planirani datum'))
    scheduled_time  = models.TimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Planirano vrijeme'))
    completed_date  = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Datum završetka'))
    deadline        = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Rok'))
    # Totals (recalculated on save)
    total_labor     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Ukupno rad'))
    total_materials = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Ukupno materijal'))
    discount        = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Popust (%)'))
    total_net       = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Neto'))
    total_vat       = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('PDV'))
    total_gross     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Bruto'))
    invoice         = models.ForeignKey(
        'invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='work_orders', verbose_name=_('Račun'),
    )
    created_by      = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='work_orders_created', verbose_name=_('Kreirao'),
    )
    created_at      = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at      = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Radni nalog')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Radni nalozi')
        ordering            = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.order_number or 'RN-???'} — {self.client or 'Bez klijenta'}"

    def generate_order_number(self):
        import datetime
        year = datetime.date.today().year
        last = WorkOrder.objects.filter(
            company=self.company,
            order_number__startswith=f'RN-{year}-',
        ).order_by('-order_number').first()
        if last and last.order_number:
            try:
                seq = int(last.order_number.split('-')[-1]) + 1
            except ValueError:
                seq = 1
        else:
            seq = 1
        return f'RN-{year}-{seq:04d}'

    def recalculate_totals(self):
        from decimal import Decimal
        labor = Decimal('0')
        materials = Decimal('0')
        total_net = Decimal('0')
        total_vat = Decimal('0')
        for item in self.items.all():
            net = item.total_net
            vat = net * item.tax_rate / Decimal('100')
            total_net += net
            total_vat += vat
            if item.item_type == 'labor':
                labor += net
            else:
                materials += net
        disc_factor = (Decimal('100') - self.discount) / Decimal('100')
        self.total_labor     = (labor     * disc_factor).quantize(Decimal('0.01'))
        self.total_materials = (materials * disc_factor).quantize(Decimal('0.01'))
        self.total_net       = (total_net * disc_factor).quantize(Decimal('0.01'))
        self.total_vat       = (total_vat * disc_factor).quantize(Decimal('0.01'))
        self.total_gross     = (self.total_net + self.total_vat).quantize(Decimal('0.01'))
        self.save(update_fields=['total_labor', 'total_materials', 'total_net', 'total_vat', 'total_gross'])

    def complete(self):
        import datetime
        self.status = 'completed'
        self.completed_date = datetime.datetime.now()
        self.save(update_fields=['status', 'completed_date'])

    @property
    def status_color(self):
        return {
            'draft':       '#64748b',
            'in_progress': '#3b82f6',
            'completed':   '#22c55e',
            'invoiced':    '#c9a84c',
            'cancelled':   '#ef4444',
        }.get(self.status, '#64748b')

    @property
    def priority_color(self):
        return {
            'low':    '#64748b',
            'normal': '#94a3b8',
            'high':   '#f59e0b',
            'urgent': '#ef4444',
        }.get(self.priority, '#94a3b8')


class WorkOrderItem(models.Model):
    ITEM_TYPES = [
        ('labor',    _('Rad / Usluga')),
        ('material', _('Materijal')),
    ]

    work_order      = models.ForeignKey(WorkOrder, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                         related_name='items', verbose_name=_('Radni nalog'))
    item_type       = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ITEM_TYPES, default='labor',
                                        verbose_name=_('Tip stavke'))
    article         = models.ForeignKey(
        'inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Artikl iz zalihe'),
    )
    description     = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Opis'))
    quantity        = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=1, verbose_name=_('Količina'))
    unit            = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='sat', verbose_name=_('JMJ'))
    unit_price      = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Cijena/jed.'))
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Popust (%)'))
    tax_rate        = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25, verbose_name=_('PDV (%)'))
    total_net       = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Neto'))
    total_gross     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Bruto'))
    bom_deducted    = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('BOM razduženo'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Stavka radnog naloga')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke radnog naloga')
        ordering            = ['item_type', 'id']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.description} × {self.quantity} {self.unit}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        from decimal import Decimal
        net = self.quantity * self.unit_price * (Decimal('100') - self.discount_percent) / Decimal('100')
        vat = net * self.tax_rate / Decimal('100')
        self.total_net   = net.quantize(Decimal('0.01'))
        self.total_gross = (net + vat).quantize(Decimal('0.01'))
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)


class VehicleCard(models.Model):
    """Karton vozila — za autoservise"""
    company             = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),

    objects             = CompanyManager()

        'partners.Partner', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='vehicle_cards', verbose_name=_('Vlasnik'),
    )
    make                = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Marka'))
    model               = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Model'))
    year                = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Godište'))
    registration_plate  = models.CharField(max_length=20, verbose_name=_('Registracija'))
    vin                 = models.CharField(max_length=17, blank=True, verbose_name=_('VIN broj'))
    engine_type         = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Motor'))
    mileage             = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Kilometraža'))
    color               = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Boja'))
    notes               = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomene'))
    created_at          = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at          = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Karton vozila')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Kartoni vozila')
        ordering            = ['client__name', 'make', 'model']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.make} {self.model} ({self.registration_plate})"


class ClientCard(models.Model):
    """Karton klijenta — za optičare, frizere, kozmetičare"""
    CARD_TYPES = [
        ('optics',    _('Optičar — dioptrija')),
        ('hair',      _('Frizer / Kozmetičar')),
        ('cosmetics', _('Kozmetika')),
        ('general',   _('Opći karton')),
    ]

    company      = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects      = CompanyManager()
    client       = models.ForeignKey(
        'partners.Partner', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='client_cards', verbose_name=_('Klijent'),
    )
    card_type    = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CARD_TYPES, default='general', verbose_name=_('Vrsta kartona'))

    # Optičarski podaci (dioptrija)
    sphere_right   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Sfero D (sph)'))
    cylinder_right = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Cilindar D (cyl)'))
    axis_right     = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Os D (axis)'))
    sphere_left    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Sfero L (sph)'))
    cylinder_left  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Cilindar L (cyl)'))
    axis_left      = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Os L (axis)'))
    pd             = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=1, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('PD (mm)'))
    add_power      = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ADD'))

    # Frizerski / kozmetičarski podaci
    hair_type    = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tip kose'))
    allergies    = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Alergije'))

    # Opće
    gdpr_consent = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('GDPR suglasnost'))
    last_visit   = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Zadnji posjet'))
    notes        = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomene'))
    created_at   = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at   = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Karton klijenta')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Kartoni klijenata')
        unique_together     = [('company', 'client', 'card_type')]
        ordering            = ['client__name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.client.name} — {self.get_card_type_display()}"

=== ./propter/models.py ===
"""
Propter models -- Faza A + B

Faza A: BillingPeriod, Otpremnica, OtpremnicaItem
Faza B: PropterTenant, PropterOperator
"""
import uuid
from decimal import Decimal
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone


# =============================================================================
# FAZA A -- BillingPeriod + Otpremnica
# =============================================================================

class BillingPeriod(models.Model):
    """Obracunski period (mjesec) per firma.
    Skuplja otpremnice kroz cijeli mjesec.
    Na kraju perioda aggregate_to_eracun() kreira jedan zbirni e-Racun.
    """

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        OPEN      = "open",     "Otvoren"
        CLOSING   = "closing",  "Zatvara se"
        CLOSED    = "closed",   "Zatvoren"
        INVOICED  = "invoiced", "Fakturiran"

    company = models.ForeignKey(
        "companies.Company", on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="billing_periods"
    )
    year  = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()  # 1-12

    status      = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.OPEN)
    opened_at   = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    closed_at   = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    invoiced_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Zbirni e-Racun kreiran na kraju perioda (FK na eracun.ERacun)
    zbirni_eracun_id     = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, db_index=True)
    zbirni_invoice_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)

    # Snapshot iznosa pri zatvaranju
    total_net        = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_tax        = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_with_tax   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    otpremnica_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("company", "year", "month")
        ordering = ["-year", "-month"]
        verbose_name = "Obracunski period"
        verbose_name_plural = "Obracunski periodi"

    def __str__(self):
        return "{} {}/{:02d} ({})".format(
            self.company.name, self.year, self.month, self.get_status_display()
        )

    @classmethod
    def get_or_create_current(cls, company):
        now = timezone.now()
        period, _ = cls.objects.get_or_create(
            company=company, year=now.year, month=now.month,
            defaults={"status": cls.Status.OPEN}
        )
        return period


class Otpremnica(models.Model):
    """Otpremnica (delivery note).
    Izvor: POS zatvaranje smjene, rucni unos, Synesis sync, API.
    Agregira se na kraju perioda u jedan zbirni e-Racun.
    """

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        DRAFT      = "draft",      "Nacrt"
        ISSUED     = "issued",     "Izdana"
        AGGREGATED = "aggregated", "Ukljucena u zbirni racun"
        CANCELLED  = "cancelled",  "Stornirana"

    class Source(models.TextChoices):
        POS     = "pos",     "POS blagajna"
        MANUAL  = "manual",  "Rucni unos"
        SYNESIS = "synesis", "Synesis"
        API     = "api",     "API"
        INVOICE = "invoice", "Faktura"

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)

    company = models.ForeignKey(
        "companies.Company", on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="otpremnice"
    )
    billing_period = models.ForeignKey(
        BillingPeriod, on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        related_name="otpremnice", null=True, blank=True
    )

    # Veze na izvorne dokumente (opcionalno -- loose coupling)
    pos_receipt_id = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, db_index=True)
    invoice_id     = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, db_index=True)

    number        = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name="Broj otpremnice")
    issue_date    = models.DateField()
    delivery_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)

    recipient_name    = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    recipient_oib     = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    recipient_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True)

    subtotal   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    tax_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total      = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    source = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Source.choices, default=Source.MANUAL)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.DRAFT)
    note   = models.TextField(blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        "auth.User", on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True
    )

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-issue_date", "-created_at"]
        verbose_name = "Otpremnica"
        verbose_name_plural = "Otpremnice"
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["company", "status"]),
            models.Index(fields=["company", "billing_period"]),
            models.Index(fields=["issue_date"]),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return "OTP-{} -> {} ({} EUR)".format(self.number, self.recipient_name, self.total)

    def save(self, **kwargs):
        if not self.billing_period_id and self.company_id:
            self.billing_period = BillingPeriod.get_or_create_current(self.company)
        if not self.number:
            now = timezone.now()
            count = Otpremnica.objects.filter(
                company=self.company,
                issue_date__year=now.year
            ).count() + 1
            self.number = "{}-{:04d}".format(now.year, count)
        super().save(**kwargs)


class OtpremnicaItem(models.Model):
    """Stavka otpremnice (EN 16931 kompatibilna)."""

    UNIT_CHOICES = [
        ("EA", "Komad"), ("HUR", "Sat"), ("KGM", "Kilogram"),
        ("MTR", "Metar"), ("LTR", "Litra"), ("MTK", "m2"),
        ("DAY", "Dan"), ("MON", "Mjesec"), ("SET", "Set"),
    ]

    otpremnica  = models.ForeignKey(Otpremnica, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="items")
    line_number = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=300)
    quantity    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=3, default=1)
    unit        = models.CharField(max_length=5, choices=UNIT_CHOICES, default="EA")
    unit_price  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=4)
    tax_rate    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25)
    line_total  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["line_number"]
        verbose_name = "Stavka otpremnice"

    def __str__(self):
        return "#{}: {} x{} = {}".format(
            self.line_number, self.description, self.quantity, self.line_total
        )

# FAZA B -- PropterTenant (SaaS multi-tenant)
# =============================================================================

class PropterConfig(models.Model):
    """SaaS tenant za Propter modul.

    Tier bookkeeper: vodi N klijenata, max pretplata.
    Tier client: vlastita firma, manja pretplata.

    Arhitektura certifikata (zakonski):
        - Svaki klijent ima vlastiti FINA certifikat
        - KS Limit/operator nije registrirana pristupna tocka
        - Kad zakon dopusti -- operator moze biti pristupna tocka
    """

    class Tier(models.TextChoices):
        CLIENT      = "client",      "Komitent (direktni)"
        BOOKKEEPER  = "bookkeeper",  "Knjigovodstveni servis"
        OPERATOR    = "operator",    "Operator/Hub (buducnost)"

    class CertStatus(models.TextChoices):
        NONE    = "none",    "Nema certifikata"
        PENDING = "pending", "Ceka certifikat"
        ACTIVE  = "active",  "Aktivan"
        EXPIRED = "expired", "Istekao"

    company = models.OneToOneField(
        "companies.Company", on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="propter_config"
    )

    tier            = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=Tier.choices, default=Tier.CLIENT)
    operator_name   = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True,
                                       help_text="Npr. KS Limit d.o.o.")
    max_clients     = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1,
                                                  help_text="Za bookkeepere: max klijenata")

    # FINA certifikat status
    cert_status      = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=CertStatus.choices,
                                        default=CertStatus.NONE)
    cert_valid_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Moduli ukljuceni u pretplatu
    modules_enabled = models.JSONField(default=list,
                                       help_text="Slugovi modula: eracun, payroll, pos, ...")

    # Pretplata
    monthly_fee  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    billing_day  = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=1)  # dan u mj. naplate
    is_active    = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    trial_until  = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Eco/marketing
    eco_badge_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    public_eco_page   = models.BooleanField(default=False,
                                            help_text="Javna eko stranica za klijenta")

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Propter tenant"
        verbose_name_plural = "Propter tenanti"

    def __str__(self):
        return "{} [{}] {}/mj".format(
            self.company.name, self.get_tier_display(), self.monthly_fee
        )

    @property
    def is_trial(self):
        return self.trial_until and self.trial_until >= timezone.now().date()

    @property
    def cert_is_valid(self):
        if self.cert_status != self.CertStatus.ACTIVE:
            return False
        if self.cert_valid_until and self.cert_valid_until < timezone.now().date():
            return False
        return True


# =============================================================================
# FAZA C -- EkoStats (marketing differentiator)
# =============================================================================

class EkoStats(models.Model):
    """Eko statistika po firmi i mjesecu.
    Automatski se izracunava pri zatvaranju BillingPeriod-a.

    Marketing: 'bez papira', CO2 usteda, ESG reporting.
    Badge razine: bronze 50+, silver 200+, gold 500+, platinum 1000+ e-racuna/mj
    """

    class Badge(models.TextChoices):
        NONE     = "",          "Bez bedza"
        BRONZE   = "bronze",   "Bronze (50+)"
        SILVER   = "silver",   "Silver (200+)"
        GOLD     = "gold",     "Gold (500+)"
        PLATINUM = "platinum", "Platinum (1000+)"

    company        = models.ForeignKey(
        "companies.Company", on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="eko_stats"
    )
    billing_period = models.OneToOneField(
        BillingPeriod, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="eko_stats", null=True, blank=True
    )
    year  = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()

    # Brojaci
    eracuni_sent     = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    otpremnice_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)

    # Eko izracun (IPCC standard: 5g CO2 po listu A4)
    paper_saved_sheets  = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    co2_saved_grams     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    trees_equivalent    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=4, default=0)

    # Kumulativ za godinu
    paper_saved_ytd  = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    co2_saved_ytd    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    badge_level = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Badge.choices, blank=True, default="")

    calculated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ("company", "year", "month")
        ordering = ["-year", "-month"]
        verbose_name = "Eko statistika"
        verbose_name_plural = "Eko statistike"

    def __str__(self):
        return "{} {}/{:02d} -- {}g CO2 usteda [{}]".format(
            self.company.name, self.year, self.month,
            self.co2_saved_grams, self.badge_level or "bez bedza"
        )

    @classmethod
    def calculate(cls, billing_period):
        """Izracunaj i spremi eko statistiku za period."""
        from decimal import Decimal

        count = billing_period.otpremnica_count or 0

        # CO2: 5g po listu, prosjecno 2 lista po dokumentu (original + kopija)
        sheets = count * 2
        co2 = Decimal(str(sheets * 5))  # grama
        # 1 stablo apsorbira ~21700g CO2/god
        trees = co2 / Decimal("21700") if co2 > 0 else Decimal("0")

        # Badge logika
        if count >= 1000:
            badge = cls.Badge.PLATINUM
        elif count >= 500:
            badge = cls.Badge.GOLD
        elif count >= 200:
            badge = cls.Badge.SILVER
        elif count >= 50:
            badge = cls.Badge.BRONZE
        else:
            badge = ""

        # YTD -- zbroj prethodnih mjeseci iste godine
        ytd_qs = cls.objects.filter(
            company=billing_period.company,
            year=billing_period.year,
            month__lt=billing_period.month
        )
        ytd_sheets = sum(e.paper_saved_sheets for e in ytd_qs) + sheets
        ytd_co2 = sum(e.co2_saved_grams for e in ytd_qs) + co2

        eko, _ = cls.objects.update_or_create(
            company=billing_period.company,
            year=billing_period.year,
            month=billing_period.month,
            defaults={
                "billing_period": billing_period,
                "eracuni_sent": count,
                "otpremnice_count": count,
                "paper_saved_sheets": sheets,
                "co2_saved_grams": co2,
                "trees_equivalent": trees.quantize(Decimal("0.0001")),
                "paper_saved_ytd": ytd_sheets,
                "co2_saved_ytd": ytd_co2,
                "badge_level": badge,
            }
        )
        return eko

=== ./payroll/models.py ===
"""
payroll/models.py — Payroll modul

Arhitektura:
  PayrollRun  → jedan obračun za (company, month, year)
  Payslip     → jedan listić po radniku, agregira TimeEntry podatke
  PayrollJournalLink → veza s konto JournalEntry (audit trail)

Integracija:
  - Podatke vuče iz worktime.TimeEntry (već ima regular/overtime/night hours)
  - EmployeeProfile (hourly_rate, overtime_rate, bank_account IBAN)
  - Pain001Generator iz finance.bank_parsers (SALA plaćanja)
  - konto.services.book_payroll() (TEM-PLACA temeljnica)
  - Company.country (HR/DE) određuje porezni engine
"""

from decimal import Decimal
from django.db import models, transaction
from django.conf import settings
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from core.managers import CompanyManager


class PayrollRun(models.Model):
    """
    Jedan obračunski ciklus: (company, year, month).
    Nakon finalizacije, nema izmjena (GoBD/audit).
    """
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('DRAFT',     _('Nacrt')),
        ('LOCKED',    _('Zaključan — obračun u toku')),
        ('FINALIZED', _('Finaliziran — isplata odobrena')),
        ('PAID',      _('Isplaćeno — pain.001 generiran')),
    ]

    company   = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                   related_name='payroll_runs')
    year      = models.IntegerField(verbose_name=_('Godina'))
    month     = models.IntegerField(verbose_name=_('Mjesec'))
    status    = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='DRAFT')

    # Ukupni agregirani iznosi (popunjava se pri finalizaciji)
    total_gross  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_net    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_tax    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    # pain.001 export
    pain001_generated_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    pain001_batch_id     = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)

    # Audit
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                    null=True, blank=True, related_name='payroll_runs_created')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    finalized_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    objects = CompanyManager()

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('company', 'year', 'month')
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']
        verbose_name = _('Obračun plaća')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Obračuni plaća')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.company.name} — {self.month:02d}/{self.year}"

    @property
    def period_label(self):
        import calendar
        return f"{calendar.month_name[self.month]} {self.year}"

    def lock(self):
        """
        Zaključava obračun za izmjene dok traje izračun.
        Koristi select_for_update() — atomarno.
        """
        with transaction.atomic():
            run = PayrollRun.objects.select_for_update().get(pk=self.pk)
            if run.status not in ('DRAFT',):
                raise ValueError(f"Ne može se zaključati: status je {run.status}")
            run.status = 'LOCKED'
            run.save(update_fields=['status'])
            self.status = 'LOCKED'

    def unlock_to_draft(self):
        """Vraća u DRAFT (samo ako nije FINALIZED/PAID)."""
        if self.status in ('FINALIZED', 'PAID'):
            raise ValueError("Finalizirani obračun ne može se otvoriti.")
        self.status = 'DRAFT'
        self.save(update_fields=['status'])


class Payslip(models.Model):
    """
    Obračunski listić za jednog radnika.

    Satnica (hourly_rate) se kopira u trenutku obračuna —
    naknadna izmjena EmployeeProfile ne smije retroaktivno
    mijenjati stare obračune (GoBD §146 Abs.4).
    """
    payroll_run = models.ForeignKey(PayrollRun, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                     related_name='payslips')
    user        = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
                                     on_delete=models.PROTECT,
                                     related_name='payslips')

    # Sati agregirati iz TimeEntry (APPROVED) za period
    regular_hours  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    overtime_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    night_hours    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_hours    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    # Satnice u trenutku obračuna (snapshot — ne mijenja se!)
    hourly_rate_snapshot   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    overtime_rate_snapshot = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    # Iznosi
    gross_amount         = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                                verbose_name=_('Bruto'))
    # HR porezni engine
    contributions_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                                verbose_name=_('Doprinosi iz'))
    tax_base             = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                                verbose_name=_('Porezna osnovica'))
    tax_amount           = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                                verbose_name=_('Porez + prirez'))
    net_amount           = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                                verbose_name=_('Neto'))

    # Isplata
    employee_iban    = models.CharField(max_length=34, blank=True,
                                         verbose_name=_('IBAN radnika'))
    is_paid          = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    payment_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)

    # DATEV export flag (DE kompanije)
    datev_exported   = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    # ArbZG/ZOR upozorenja iz perioda
    compliance_notes = models.TextField(blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('payroll_run', 'user')
        ordering = ['user__last_name', 'user__first_name']
        verbose_name = _('Obračunski listić')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Obračunski listići')

    def __str__(self):
        return (f"{self.user.get_full_name() or self.user.username} — "
                f"{self.payroll_run}")

    @property
    def employee_name(self):
        return self.user.get_full_name() or self.user.username

=== ./permissions/models.py ===
"""
🔐 RBAC Permission System
Granular role-based access control
"""
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.core.cache import cache
from django.db import models
from django.db.models.signals import post_delete, post_save
from django.dispatch import receiver


class Permission(models.Model):
    """Granular permissions across all modules"""

    MODULE_CHOICES = [
        ('users', '👥 Users'),
        ('companies', '🏢 Companies'),
        ('partners', '🤝 Partners'),
        ('invoices', '📄 Invoices'),
        ('documents', '📎 Documents'),
        ('finance', '💰 Finance'),
        ('worktime', '⏱️ Work Time'),
        ('projects', '📊 Projects'),
        ('reports', '📈 Reports'),
        ('calculator', '🧮 Calculator'),
        ('system', '⚙️ System'),
    ]

    ACTION_CHOICES = [
        ('view', '👁️ View'),
        ('create', '➕ Create'),
        ('edit', '✏️ Edit'),
        ('delete', '🗑️ Delete'),
        ('approve', '✅ Approve'),
        ('export', '📤 Export'),
        ('import', '📥 Import'),
        ('manage', '⚙️ Manage'),
    ]

    module = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=MODULE_CHOICES, db_index=True)
    action = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=ACTION_CHOICES, db_index=True)
    codename = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True, db_index=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)

    # Hierarchical permissions
    requires = models.ManyToManyField('self', blank=True, symmetrical=False,
                                     related_name='unlocks',
                                     help_text="Required permissions")

    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'permissions'
        ordering = ['module', 'action']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['module', 'action']),
            models.Index(fields=['codename']),
        ]
        unique_together = [['module', 'action']]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_module_display()}: {self.get_action_display()}"

    def get_full_codename(self):
        """Return module.action format"""
        return f"{self.module}.{self.action}"
class Role(models.Model):
    """User roles with permission sets"""

    ROLE_TYPES = [
        ('superadmin', '👑 Super Admin'),
        ('company_admin', '🏢 Company Admin'),
        ('accountant', '💼 Accountant'),
        ('manager', '📊 Manager'),
        ('worker', '👷 Worker'),
        ('partner', '🤝 Partner'),
        ('investor', '💰 Investor'),
        ('guest', '👁️ Guest'),
    ]

    role_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=ROLE_TYPES, unique=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    permissions = models.ManyToManyField(Permission, related_name='roles', blank=True)

    # Role hierarchy
    parent_role = models.ForeignKey('self', null=True, blank=True,
                                   on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                   related_name='child_roles')

    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'roles'
        ordering = ['role_type']

    def __str__(self):
        return self.get_role_type_display()

    def get_all_permissions(self):
        """Get permissions including inherited from parent"""
        perms = set(self.permissions.filter(is_active=True).values_list('codename', flat=True))

        # Inherit from parent
        if self.parent_role:
            perms.update(self.parent_role.get_all_permissions())

        return list(perms)

    def clear_cache(self):
        """Clear cached permissions for this role"""
        cache_key = f"role_perms_{self.role_type}"
        cache.delete(cache_key)
class UserRole(models.Model):
    """User-Role assignment with scope"""

    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='user_roles')
    role = models.ForeignKey(Role, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='user_assignments')

    # Scope limitations
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', null=True, blank=True,
                               on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                               help_text="Limit role to specific company")

    # Time-based access
    valid_from = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    valid_until = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    is_primary = models.BooleanField(default=True,
                                     help_text="Primary role for user")

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, null=True, blank=True,
                                  on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                  related_name='assigned_roles')

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'user_roles'
        ordering = ['-is_primary', 'role']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['user', 'role']),
            models.Index(fields=['company']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} - {self.role.name}"

    def is_valid(self):
        """Check if role assignment is currently valid"""
        from django.utils import timezone
        now = timezone.now()

        if self.valid_from and now < self.valid_from:
            return False
        if self.valid_until and now > self.valid_until:
            return False

        return True
class PermissionOverride(models.Model):
    """Override specific permissions for users"""

    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='permission_overrides')
    permission = models.ForeignKey(Permission, on_delete=models.CASCADE)

    # Grant or revoke
    grant = models.BooleanField(default=True, help_text="True=Grant, False=Revoke")

    # Scope
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', null=True, blank=True,
                               on_delete=models.CASCADE)

    reason = models.TextField(help_text="Why this override exists")

    valid_until = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True,
                                  related_name='granted_overrides')

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'permission_overrides'
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['user', 'permission']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        action = "GRANT" if self.grant else "REVOKE"
        return f"{action}: {self.user.username} - {self.permission.codename}"
# ============================================================================
# SIGNALS - Clear cache on changes
# ============================================================================
@receiver([post_save, post_delete], sender=Role)
def clear_role_cache(sender, instance, **kwargs):
    """Clear role cache when permissions change"""
    instance.clear_cache()
@receiver([post_save, post_delete], sender=UserRole)
def clear_user_permission_cache(sender, instance, **kwargs):
    """Clear user permission cache"""
    cache_key = f"user_perms_{instance.user.id}"
    cache.delete(cache_key)

=== ./aml/models.py ===
"""
AML — Anti-Money Laundering / Dubinska analiza klijenta.
Zakon o sprjecavanju pranja novca i financiranja terorizma (NN 108/17).
"""
from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings


class AMLRiskCategory(models.Model):
    """Kategorija rizika."""
    RISK_LEVELS = [
        ('low', 'Nizak'),
        ('medium', 'Srednji'),
        ('high', 'Visok'),
        ('very_high', 'Vrlo visok'),
    ]
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    risk_level = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=RISK_LEVELS)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['name']
        verbose_name = 'Kategorija rizika'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Kategorije rizika'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.name} ({self.get_risk_level_display()})'


class AMLClientProfile(models.Model):
    """Profil dubinske analize klijenta."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', 'Nacrt'),
        ('active', 'Aktivno'),
        ('review_needed', 'Potreban pregled'),
        ('expired', 'Isteklo'),
        ('archived', 'Arhivirano'),
    ]
    CLIENT_TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('fizicka', 'Fizicka osoba'),
        ('pravna', 'Pravna osoba'),
    ]
    RISK_CHOICES = [
        ('low', 'Nizak'),
        ('medium', 'Srednji'),
        ('high', 'Visok'),
        ('very_high', 'Vrlo visok'),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                 related_name='aml_profiles')
    assessed_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                     null=True, related_name='aml_assessments')
    assessment_date = models.DateField()
    next_review_date = models.DateField()
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft')

    # Identifikacija
    client_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CLIENT_TYPE_CHOICES, default='pravna')
    legal_form = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True,
        help_text='d.o.o., j.d.o.o., obrt, udruga, itd.')
    ownership_structure = models.TextField(blank=True,
        verbose_name='Vlasnička struktura')
    beneficial_owners = models.TextField(blank=True,
        verbose_name='Krajnji korisnici (stvarni vlasnici)')

    # Rizik
    risk_categories = models.ManyToManyField(AMLRiskCategory, blank=True)
    overall_risk = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=RISK_CHOICES, default='low')
    risk_score = models.IntegerField(default=0,
        help_text='0-100, automatski izracunat ili rucni')

    # Provjere
    id_verified = models.BooleanField(default=False,
        verbose_name='Osobna iskaznica provjerena')
    registry_checked = models.BooleanField(default=False,
        verbose_name='Sudski registar provjeren')
    pep_check = models.BooleanField(default=False,
        verbose_name='PEP provjera (politicki eksponirane osobe)')
    sanctions_check = models.BooleanField(default=False,
        verbose_name='Lista sankcija provjerena')
    adverse_media = models.BooleanField(default=False,
        verbose_name='Negativni medijski clanci provjereni')

    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-assessment_date']
        verbose_name = 'AML profil klijenta'
        verbose_name_plural = 'AML profili klijenata'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'AML: {self.company.name} ({self.get_overall_risk_display()})'

    @property
    def is_expired(self):
        from datetime import date
        return self.next_review_date < date.today()

    @property
    def checks_completed(self):
        checks = [self.id_verified, self.registry_checked, self.pep_check,
                   self.sanctions_check, self.adverse_media]
        return sum(checks), len(checks)


class AMLTransaction(models.Model):
    """Sumnjiva transakcija."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('detected', 'Detektirano'),
        ('under_review', 'U pregledu'),
        ('reported', 'Prijavljeno AMLA uredu'),
        ('cleared', 'Razrijeseno'),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                 related_name='aml_transactions')
    client_profile = models.ForeignKey(AMLClientProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                        related_name='transactions', null=True, blank=True)
    reported_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True)
    transaction_date = models.DateField()
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2)
    description = models.TextField()
    suspicion_reason = models.TextField(verbose_name='Razlog sumnje')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='detected')
    reported_to_amla = models.BooleanField(default=False,
        verbose_name='Prijavljeno Uredu za sprecavanje pranja novca')
    report_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    reference_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True,
        verbose_name='AMLA referenca')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-transaction_date']
        verbose_name = 'Sumnjiva transakcija'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Sumnjive transakcije'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'AML TX: {self.amount} EUR ({self.get_status_display()})'


class AMLDocument(models.Model):
    """Prilog uz AML profil."""
    DOC_TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('osobna', 'Osobna iskaznica'),
        ('izvadak_sudski', 'Izvadak iz sudskog registra'),
        ('izvadak_oib', 'Izvadak iz OIB sustava'),
        ('potvrda_adrese', 'Potvrda adrese'),

    profile = models.ForeignKey(AMLClientProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                 related_name='documents')

    uploaded_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True)
    uploaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-uploaded_at']
        verbose_name = 'AML dokument'
        verbose_name_plural = 'AML dokumenti'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.get_document_type_display()} — {self.profile.company.name}'

=== ./procurement/models.py ===
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.utils import timezone
from core.managers import CompanyManager

class PurchaseOrder(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', _('Nacrt')), ('sent', _('Poslano')), ('confirmed', _('Potvrdjeno')),
        ('partial', _('Djelomicno isporuceno')), ('received', _('Isporuceno')),
        ('invoiced', _('Fakturirano')), ('cancelled', _('Otkazano')),
    ]
    PRIORITY_CHOICES = [
        ('low', _('Nizak')), ('normal', _('Normalan')),
        ('high', _('Visok')), ('urgent', _('Hitno')),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    objects = CompanyManager()
    po_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True)
    supplier = models.ForeignKey('partners.Partner', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='purchase_orders')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft')
    priority = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=PRIORITY_CHOICES, default='normal')
    order_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.now)
    expected_delivery = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    actual_delivery = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    subtotal = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    tax_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, default='')
    internal_note = models.TextField(blank=True, default='')
    matched_invoice = models.ForeignKey('invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='matched_po')
    match_status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=[
        ('unmatched', 'Neupareno'), ('matched', 'Upareno'),
        ('mismatch', 'Nepodudaranje'), ('approved', 'Odobreno'),
    ], default='unmatched')
    match_diff_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='po_created')
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='po_approved')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Narudzbenica'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Narudzbenice'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.po_number} - {self.supplier or 'N/A'}"

    def generate_po_number(self):
        year = timezone.now().year
        last = PurchaseOrder.objects.filter(po_number__startswith=f'PO-{year}-').order_by('-po_number').first()
        seq = 1
        if last:
            try: seq = int(last.po_number.split('-')[-1]) + 1
            except Exception: pass
        return f'PO-{year}-{seq:04d}'

    def recalculate(self):
        from django.db.models import Sum
        agg = self.items.aggregate(sub=Sum('line_total'), tax=Sum('line_tax'))
        self.subtotal = agg['sub'] or 0
        self.tax_amount = agg['tax'] or 0
        self.total = self.subtotal + self.tax_amount
        self.save(update_fields=['subtotal', 'tax_amount', 'total', 'updated_at'])


class PurchaseOrderItem(models.Model):
    purchase_order = models.ForeignKey(PurchaseOrder, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='items')
    inventory_item = models.ForeignKey('inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=500)
    sku = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default='')
    quantity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=3)
    unit = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='kom')
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=4)
    tax_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25)
    line_total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    line_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    quantity_received = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=3, default=0)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Stavka narudzbenice'

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.line_total = self.quantity * self.unit_price
        self.line_tax = self.line_total * (self.tax_rate / __import__("decimal").Decimal("100"))
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)


class SupplierPriceList(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    objects = CompanyManager()
    supplier = models.ForeignKey('partners.Partner', on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='price_lists')
    inventory_item = models.ForeignKey('inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    agreed_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=4)
    valid_from = models.DateField(default=timezone.now)
    valid_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    min_quantity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=3, default=1)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Cjenik dobavljaca'
        unique_together = ['supplier', 'inventory_item', 'valid_from']

import secrets
from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


def _token():
    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)


class SynesisConfig(models.Model):
    company = models.OneToOneField(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='synesis_config', verbose_name='Tvrtka',
    )
    api_token = models.CharField(max_length=64, unique=True, default=_token, verbose_name='API Token')
    storage_db_path = models.CharField(
        max_length=500, blank=True,
        default=r'C:\Synesis\FIRMA\Storage.mdb',
        verbose_name='Putanja do Storage.mdb',
        help_text=r'Npr. C:\Synesis\KONJIK2025\Storage.mdb',
    )
    journal_db_path = models.CharField(
        max_length=500, blank=True,
        default=r'C:\Synesis\FIRMA\Journal.mdb',
        verbose_name='Putanja do Journal.mdb',
    )
    pos_db_path = models.CharField(
        max_length=500, blank=True, default='',
        verbose_name='Putanja do SynesisPOS baze (kasa)',
        help_text=r'Npr. C:\Synesis\POS\SynesisPOS.mdb',
    )
    pos_db_password = models.CharField(
        max_length=100, blank=True, default='',
        verbose_name='SynesisPOS lozinka baze',
    )
    pos_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default='Kasa')
    last_sync_pos = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    pos_receipts_total = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    pos_revenue_today = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    pos_revenue_week = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    pos_revenue_month = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    last_sync_partners = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    last_sync_items = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    partners_total = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    items_total = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Synesis konfiguracija'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Synesis: {self.company.name}'

    def regenerate_token(self):
        self.api_token = _token()
        self.save(update_fields=['api_token'])
        return self.api_token

    invoices_total = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('config', 'year')
        ordering = ['-year']
        verbose_name = 'Synesis godišnja konfiguracija'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.config.company.name} {self.year}'

from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class ProjectLead(models.Model):

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        NOVI_UPIT          = 'NOVI_UPIT',          _('Novi upit')
        INTERNA_PROCJENA   = 'INTERNA_PROCJENA',   _('Interna procjena')
        NA_BURZI           = 'NA_BURZI',           _('Na burzi')
        PREUZETO           = 'PREUZETO',           _('Preuzeto')
        RIJESENO           = 'RIJESENO',           _('Riješeno')

    class Category(models.TextChoices):
        KUPAONICA  = 'kupaonica',  _('Kupaonica')
        PLOCICE    = 'plocice',    _('Pločice')
        VODOINSTAL = 'vodoinstal', _('Vodoinstalacije')
        ELEKTRIKA  = 'elektrika',  _('Elektrika')
        GRIJANJE   = 'grijanje',   _('Grijanje')
        RENOVACIJA = 'renovacija', _('Renovacija')
        GRADNJA    = 'gradnja',    _('Gradnja')
        FASADA     = 'fasada',     _('Fasada')
        OSTALO     = 'ostalo',     _('Ostalo')

    company       = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                       related_name='project_leads', null=True, blank=True)
    title         = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description   = models.TextField()
    category      = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=Category.choices, default=Category.OSTALO)
    location      = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    budget_from   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)
    budget_to     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)
    desired_start = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    contact_name  = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    contact_phone = models.CharField(max_length=30)
    contact_email = models.EmailField(blank=True)
    status        = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Status.choices,
                                      default=Status.NOVI_UPIT, db_index=True)
    assigned_to   = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
                                       related_name='assigned_leads')
    internal_notes= models.TextField(blank=True)
    is_public     = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    taken_by      = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                       null=True, blank=True, related_name='taken_leads')
    taken_by_user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
                                       related_name='taken_leads')
    taken_at      = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_by    = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
                                       related_name='created_leads')
    created_at    = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at    = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = 'Projektni upit'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Projektni upiti'
        ordering            = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.title} – {self.get_status_display()}'

    @property
    def budget_display(self):
        def _fmt(val):
            return f'{val:,.0f}'.replace(',', '.')
        if self.budget_from and self.budget_to:
            return f'€{_fmt(self.budget_from)} – €{_fmt(self.budget_to)}'
        elif self.budget_from:
            return f'od €{_fmt(self.budget_from)}'
        return '–'


class LeadActivity(models.Model):
    lead       = models.ForeignKey(ProjectLead, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='activities')
    user       = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True)
    action     = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    old_status = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    new_status = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    note       = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.lead.title} – {self.action}'

import uuid

from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Sum
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _

from companies.models import Company
from core.managers import CompanyManager


class Partner(models.Model):
    TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('client',   _('Klijent (Kupac)')),
        ('supplier', _('Dobavljač')),
        ('both',     _('Partner (Oboje)')),
        ('employee', _('Zaposlenik')),
    ]

    class Country(models.TextChoices):
        HR = 'HR', _('Hrvatska')
        DE = 'DE', _('Njemačka')
        AT = 'AT', _('Austrija')
        SI = 'SI', _('Slovenija')
        BA = 'BA', _('Bosna i Hercegovina')
        RS = 'RS', _('Srbija')
        IT = 'IT', _('Italija')
        HU = 'HU', _('Mađarska')
        OTHER = 'OTHER', _('Ostalo')

    company = models.ForeignKey(Company, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='partners',
                                 verbose_name=_('Vlasnik podataka'))
    objects = CompanyManager()

    # Osnovni podaci
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv / Ime'))
    oib  = models.CharField(max_length=13, blank=True, verbose_name=_('OIB / Steuer-Nr.'))

    # Lokacija
    address  = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Ulica i broj'))
    city     = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Grad'))
    zip_code = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Poštanski broj'))
    country  = models.CharField(
        max_length=10,
        choices=Country.choices,
        default=Country.HR,
        verbose_name=_('Država'),
    )

    # Kontakt
    email = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name='Email')
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Telefon'))
    web   = models.URLField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Web stranica'))

    # Kontakt osoba
    contact_person = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kontakt osoba'))
    contact_phone  = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Telefon kontakt osobe'))
    contact_email  = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Email kontakt osobe'))

    # Bankovni podaci
    iban  = models.CharField(max_length=34, blank=True, verbose_name='IBAN')
    swift = models.CharField(max_length=11, blank=True, verbose_name='SWIFT / BIC')

    # PDV
    is_vat_registered = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('U sustavu PDV-a'))
    vat_id = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('PDV ID / VAT ID'),
        help_text=_('EU PDV identifikacijski broj (npr. HR12345678901)'),
    )

    # Komercijalni uvjeti
    partner_type   = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=TYPE_CHOICES, default='client',
                                       verbose_name=_('Tip partnera'))
    payment_term   = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Rok plaćanja (dana)'))
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0.00,
                                            verbose_name=_('Rabat (%)'))
    credit_limit   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                          verbose_name=_('Kreditni limit'))

    # Sistem
    notes     = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Interne napomene'))
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivan'))
    portal_token = models.UUIDField(unique=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)

    # AUTOMATIZACIJA (ne dira se ručno)
    cached_balance = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0.00,
                                          verbose_name=_('Saldo'))

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    nkd_code = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.NKDCode',
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name='NKD djelatnost',
    )

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('company', 'oib')
        verbose_name = _('Partner')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Partneri')
        ordering = ['name']


    @property
    def is_complete(self):
        return bool(self.name and self.oib and self.address and self.city and self.email)

    @property
    def missing_fields(self):
        missing = []
        if not self.oib: missing.append("OIB")
        if not self.address: missing.append("Adresa")
        if not self.city: missing.append("Grad")
        if not self.email: missing.append("Email")
        if not self.phone: missing.append("Telefon")
        return missing
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.city or 'N/A'})"

    def update_balance(self):
        inflow  = self.transactions.filter(direction='income').aggregate(Sum('amount'))['amount__sum'] or 0
        outflow = self.transactions.filter(direction='expense').aggregate(Sum('amount'))['amount__sum'] or 0
        self.cached_balance = inflow - outflow
        self.save(update_fields=['cached_balance'])

=== ./konto/models.py ===
from decimal import Decimal
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.core.validators import MinValueValidator
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 1. KONTNI PLAN (Chart of Accounts)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
class AccountType(models.TextChoices):
    ASSET = 'asset', _('Imovina')           # Klasa 0-3
    LIABILITY = 'liability', _('Obveze')     # Klasa 4
    EQUITY = 'equity', _('Kapital')          # Klasa 9
    INCOME = 'income', _('Prihodi')          # Klasa 7
    EXPENSE = 'expense', _('Rashodi')        # Klasa 4-6


class KontoAccount(models.Model):
    """Konto iz kontnog plana (npr. 1000 - Žiro račun, 1200 - Potraživanja od kupaca)"""
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                related_name='konto_accounts')
    code = models.CharField(max_length=10, verbose_name=_('Šifra konta'),
        help_text='npr. 1000, 1200, 2200, 4000, 7000')
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv konta'))
    account_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=AccountType.choices,
                                     verbose_name=_('Vrsta konta'))
    parent = models.ForeignKey('self', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
                               related_name='children', verbose_name=_('Nadređeni konto'))
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['code']
        unique_together = ['company', 'code']
        verbose_name = _('Konto')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Kontni plan')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.code} - {self.name}"

    @property
    def balance(self):
        """Trenutni saldo konta"""
        from django.db.models import Sum
        debits = self.ledgerline_entries.filter(side='debit').aggregate(
            t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        credits = self.ledgerline_entries.filter(side='credit').aggregate(
            t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        # Asset i Expense rastu s dugovne strane
        if self.account_type in ('asset', 'expense'):
            return debits - credits
        # Liability, Equity, Income rastu s potražne strane
        return credits - debits


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 2. ŽIRO RAČUNI (Bank Accounts)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
class BankAccount(models.Model):
    """Žiro račun firme u banci"""
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                related_name='bank_accounts')
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv'),
        help_text='npr. Glavno Poslovanje - PBZ')
    iban = models.CharField(max_length=34, verbose_name='IBAN')
    bank_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('Banka'))
    swift = models.CharField(max_length=11, blank=True, verbose_name='SWIFT/BIC')
    currency = models.CharField(max_length=3, default='EUR', verbose_name=_('Valuta'))

    # Veza na kontni plan
    konto_account = models.ForeignKey(KontoAccount, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Konto'),
        help_text='npr. 1000 - Žiro račun u banci')

    opening_balance = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Početni saldo'))
    current_balance = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Trenutni saldo'))
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    is_primary = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Primarni račun'))

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-is_primary', 'name']
        verbose_name = _('Žiro račun')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Žiro računi')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.iban[-8:]})"

    def recalculate_balance(self):
        """Preračunaj saldo iz svih transakcija"""
        from django.db.models import Sum
        inflows = self.transactions.filter(
            transaction_type='inflow'
        ).aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        outflows = self.transactions.filter(
            transaction_type='outflow'
        ).aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        self.current_balance = self.opening_balance + inflows - outflows
        self.save(update_fields=['current_balance', 'updated_at'])
