# 3. BANKOVNE TRANSAKCIJE (izvod)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
class BankTransaction(models.Model):
    """Jedna stavka bankovnog izvoda"""

    TRANSACTION_TYPES = [
        ('inflow', _('Uplata (priljev)')),
        ('outflow', _('Isplata (odljev)')),
    ]

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('unmatched', _('Nepariran')),
        ('matched', _('Pariran')),
        ('ignored', _('Ignoriran')),
    ]

    bank_account = models.ForeignKey(BankAccount, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                     null=True, blank=True, related_name='transactions')
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE)

    # Podaci iz izvoda
    transaction_date = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Datum transakcije'))
    value_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Datum valute'))
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Iznos'))
    transaction_type = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=TRANSACTION_TYPES)

    # Detalji
    counterparty_name = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Platitelj/Primatelj'))
    counterparty_iban = models.CharField(max_length=34, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('IBAN platitelja/primatelja'))
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis plaćanja'))
    reference_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Poziv na broj'))

    # Pariranje
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='unmatched')
    matched_invoice = models.ForeignKey('invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='bank_transactions',
        verbose_name=_('Pariran račun'))

    # Knjiženje
    is_booked = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Proknjiženo'))

    # Import metadata
    import_batch = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True,
        help_text='ID importa za grupiranje')
    external_id = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True,
        help_text='ID iz banke za dedup')

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-transaction_date', '-id']
        verbose_name = _('Bankovna transakcija')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Bankovne transakcije')
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['company', '-transaction_date']),
            models.Index(fields=['status', 'company']),
            models.Index(fields=['reference_number']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        sign = '+' if self.transaction_type == 'inflow' else '-'
        return f"{self.transaction_date} {sign}{self.amount}\u20ac {self.counterparty_name[:30]}"


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 4. KNJIŽENJE (Ledger Entries) - Dvostavno
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
class JournalEntry(models.Model):
    """Temeljnica - grupa knjiženja koja mora biti u balansu (duguje = potražuje)"""
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                related_name='journal_entries')
    entry_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Broj temeljnice'))
    date = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Datum knjiženja'))
    description = models.CharField(max_length=300, verbose_name=_('Opis'))

    # Izvor knjiženja
    SOURCE_TYPES = [
        ('invoice', _('Račun')),
        ('bank_statement', _('Bankovni izvod')),
        ('manual', _('Ručno knjiženje')),
        ('payroll', _('Obračun plaća')),
        ('pos', _('POS promet')),
        ('correction', _('Ispravak')),
        ('depreciation', _('Amortizacija')),
    ]
    source_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, choices=SOURCE_TYPES)
    source_invoice = models.ForeignKey('invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='journal_entries')
    source_transaction = models.ForeignKey(BankTransaction, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='journal_entries')

    is_posted = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Proknjiženo'))
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                    null=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-date', '-id']
        verbose_name = _('Temeljnica')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Temeljnice')
        unique_together = [['company', 'entry_number']]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.entry_number} - {self.description}"

    def clean(self):
        from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
        # Period lock check
        if self.date and self.company_id:
            if AccountingPeriod.is_period_locked(self.company, self.date.year, self.date.month):
                if not self.pk or JournalEntry.objects.filter(pk=self.pk, date=self.date).exists() is False:
                    raise ValidationError(
                        f"Razdoblje {self.date.month:02d}/{self.date.year} je zaključano. Knjiženje nije moguće."
                    )
        # Skip balance check for new entries (no lines yet)
        if self.pk and self.lines.exists():
            from django.db.models import Sum
            debits = self.lines.filter(side="debit").aggregate(t=Sum("amount"))["t"] or Decimal("0")
            credits = self.lines.filter(side="credit").aggregate(t=Sum("amount"))["t"] or Decimal("0")
            if debits != credits:
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"Temeljnica nije uravnotežena: duguje={debits}, potražuje={credits}, razlika={abs(debits - credits)}"
                )

    @property
    def is_balanced(self):
        """Provjera: duguje == potražuje"""
        from django.db.models import Sum
        debits = self.lines.filter(side='debit').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        credits = self.lines.filter(side='credit').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        return debits == credits

    @property
    def total_debit(self):
        from django.db.models import Sum
        return self.lines.filter(side='debit').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')

    @property
    def total_credit(self):
        from django.db.models import Sum
        return self.lines.filter(side='credit').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')


class LedgerLine(models.Model):
    """Jedan red knjiženja - dugovna ili potražna strana"""
    journal_entry = models.ForeignKey(JournalEntry, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                      related_name='lines')
    account = models.ForeignKey(KontoAccount, on_delete=models.PROTECT,
                                related_name='ledgerline_entries')
    side = models.CharField(max_length=6, choices=[
        ('debit', _('Duguje')),
        ('credit', _('Potražuje')),
    ])
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, validators=[MinValueValidator(Decimal('0.01'))])
    integrity_hash = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, default='',
        verbose_name=_('Integrity Hash'), help_text='SHA-256 chain za GoBD audit')
    description = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Stavka knjiženja')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke knjiženja')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.account.code} {'D' if self.side == 'debit' else 'P'} {self.amount}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        import hashlib
        if not self.integrity_hash:
            data = f"{self.journal_entry_id}|{self.account_id}|{self.side}|{self.amount}"
            self.integrity_hash = hashlib.sha256(data.encode()).hexdigest()
        self.full_clean()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 5. POREZNO RAZDOBLJE (Tax Period / USt-Voranmeldung)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

class TaxPeriod(models.Model):
    PERIOD_TYPES = [
        ('monthly', _('Miesięczny / Monatlich')),
        ('quarterly', _('Kvartalni / Quartal')),
        ('yearly', _('Godišnji / Jährlich')),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                related_name='tax_periods')
    period_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PERIOD_TYPES, default='monthly')
    year = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(null=True, blank=True)   # 1-12
    quarter = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(null=True, blank=True) # 1-4

    # Output VAT (izlazni PDV / Umsatzsteuer)
    total_output_vat = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Izlazni PDV'))
    # Input VAT (pretporez / Vorsteuer)
    total_input_vat = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Pretporez'))
    # Payable = output - input
    vat_payable = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('PDV za uplatu'))
    # Revenue and expenses in period
    total_revenue = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Prihodi'))
    total_expenses = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Rashodi'))

    filing_deadline = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Rok prijave'))
    is_filed = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Prijavljeno'))
    filed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-year', '-month', '-quarter']
        unique_together = ['company', 'period_type', 'year', 'month', 'quarter']
        verbose_name = _('Porezno razdoblje')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Porezna razdoblja')

    def __str__(self):
        if self.period_type == 'monthly' and self.month:
            return f"{self.year}-{self.month:02d} ({self.company})"
        if self.period_type == 'quarterly' and self.quarter:
            return f"{self.year} Q{self.quarter} ({self.company})"
        return f"{self.year} ({self.company})"

    @property
    def period_label(self):
        if self.period_type == 'monthly' and self.month:
            import calendar
            return f"{calendar.month_name[self.month]} {self.year}"
        if self.period_type == 'quarterly' and self.quarter:
            return f"Q{self.quarter}/{self.year}"
        return str(self.year)


class TaxLineDetail(models.Model):
    """Per-rate VAT breakdown within a TaxPeriod."""
    period = models.ForeignKey(TaxPeriod, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                               related_name='line_details')
    tax_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2,
        verbose_name=_('Stopa PDV-a (%)'))
    revenue_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Porezna osnovica'))
    output_vat = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Izlazni PDV'))

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-tax_rate']
        unique_together = ['period', 'tax_rate']
        verbose_name = _('Stavka PDV-a')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke PDV-a')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.period} @ {self.tax_rate}%"


class AccountingPeriod(models.Model):
    """Accounting period lock - prevents posting to closed periods."""
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField()
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    is_locked = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    locked_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    locked_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='locked_periods')
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = [['company', 'year', 'month']]
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']
        verbose_name = 'Obračunsko razdoblje'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Obračunska razdoblja'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.month:02d}/{self.year} ({'zaključano' if self.is_locked else 'otvoreno'})"

    def lock(self, user=None):
        from django.utils import timezone
        self.is_locked = True
        self.locked_at = timezone.now()
        self.locked_by = user
        self.save(update_fields=['is_locked', 'locked_at', 'locked_by'])

    def unlock(self, user=None):
        self.is_locked = False
        self.locked_at = None
        self.locked_by = None
        self.save(update_fields=['is_locked', 'locked_at', 'locked_by'])

    @classmethod
    def is_period_locked(cls, company, year, month):
        try:
            period = cls.objects.get(company=company, year=year, month=month)
            return period.is_locked
        except cls.DoesNotExist:
            return False

=== ./payments/models.py ===
from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
import uuid


class PaymentConfig(models.Model):
    """Company-level payment gateway configuration."""
    company = models.OneToOneField(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='payment_config', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    # Stripe
    stripe_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Stripe aktivan'))
    stripe_public_key = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Stripe Publishable Key'))
    stripe_secret_key = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Stripe Secret Key'))
    stripe_webhook_secret = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Stripe Webhook Secret'))

    # BTCPay
    btcpay_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('BTCPay aktivan'))
    btcpay_url = models.URLField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('BTCPay Server URL'))
    btcpay_store_id = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('BTCPay Store ID'))
    btcpay_api_key = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('BTCPay API Key'))

    # PayPal
    paypal_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('PayPal aktivan'))
    paypal_client_id = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('PayPal Client ID'))
    paypal_secret = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('PayPal Secret'))
    paypal_mode = models.CharField(max_length=10, default='live', choices=[('sandbox','Sandbox'),('live','Live')], verbose_name=_('PayPal Mode'))

    # General
    default_currency = models.CharField(max_length=3, default='EUR', verbose_name=_('Valuta'))
    auto_capture = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Automatski naplati'))

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Konfiguracija placanja')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Konfiguracije placanja')

    def __str__(self):
        methods = []
        if self.stripe_enabled:
            methods.append('Stripe')
        if self.btcpay_enabled:
            methods.append('BTCPay')
        if self.paypal_enabled:
            methods.append('PayPal')
        return f'{self.company.name} - {", ".join(methods) or "Samo gotovina"}'


class Payment(models.Model):
    """Universal payment record - used by any module."""
    METHOD_CHOICES = [
        ('cash', _('Gotovina')),
        ('stripe', _('Kartica (Stripe)')),
        ('btcpay', _('Kriptovaluta (BTCPay)')),
        ('paypal', _('PayPal')),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Na cekanju')),
        ('processing', _('U obradi')),
        ('completed', _('Zavrseno')),
        ('failed', _('Neuspjesno')),
        ('refunded', _('Povrat')),
        ('cancelled', _('Otkazano')),
    ]
    SOURCE_CHOICES = [
        ('gastro_qr', _('Gastro QR Narudzba')),
        ('pos', _('POS Blagajna')),
        ('webshop', _('Webshop')),
        ('invoice', _('Faktura')),
        ('rental', _('Najam / Hostel')),
        ('booking', _('Rezervacija')),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='payments', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )

    # What is being paid for
    source = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=SOURCE_CHOICES, verbose_name=_('Izvor'))
    source_id = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ID izvora'),
                                  help_text=_('PK narudzbe, fakture, itd.'))

    # Payment details
    method = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=METHOD_CHOICES, verbose_name=_('Nacin placanja'))
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending', verbose_name=_('Status'))
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Iznos'))
    currency = models.CharField(max_length=3, default='EUR')
    description = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis'))

    # External gateway references
    external_id = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Vanjski ID'),
                                    help_text=_('Stripe PaymentIntent ID ili BTCPay Invoice ID'))
    external_url = models.URLField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Vanjski URL'),
                                    help_text=_('Stripe checkout URL ili BTCPay invoice URL'))
    gateway_data = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Gateway podaci'))

    # Customer info
    customer_email = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Email kupca'))
    customer_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Ime kupca'))

    # Crypto-specific
    crypto_currency = models.CharField(max_length=10, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kripto valuta'),
                                        help_text=_('BTC, ETH, LTC, itd.'))
    crypto_amount = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kripto iznos'))
    crypto_address = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Crypto adresa'))

    # Timestamps
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    completed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Placanje')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Placanja')
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['company', 'status']),
            models.Index(fields=['source', 'source_id']),
            models.Index(fields=['external_id']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.get_method_display()} {self.amount} {self.currency} - {self.get_status_display()}'

    @property
    def is_completed(self):
        return self.status == 'completed'

    @property
    def is_pending(self):
        return self.status in ('pending', 'processing')

class Appointment(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Tvrtka')
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('scheduled', 'Zakazano'),
        ('confirmed', 'Potvrđeno'),
        ('completed', 'Završeno'),
        ('cancelled', 'Otkazano'),
        ('no_show',   'Nije došao'),
    ]
    TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('service', 'Kratka usluga'),
        ('rental',  'Dugoročni najam'),
        ('event',   'Događaj'),
        ('meeting', 'Obični sastanak'),
    ]
    title            = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name='Naziv')
    description      = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='Opis')
    start_time       = models.DateTimeField(verbose_name='Početak')
    end_time         = models.DateTimeField(verbose_name='Kraj')
    location         = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name='Lokacija')
    is_online        = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Online')
    meeting_link     = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name='Link za sastanak')
    status           = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='scheduled', verbose_name='Status')
    client           = models.ForeignKey('partners.Partner', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='appointments', verbose_name='Klijent')
    employee         = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='appointments', verbose_name='Zaposlenik')
    related_project  = models.ForeignKey('projects.Project', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='appointments', verbose_name='Projekt')
    service          = models.ForeignKey('BookingService', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Odabrana usluga')
    resource         = models.ForeignKey('BookingResource', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Zauzeti resurs')
    actor_resource   = models.ForeignKey('BookingResource', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='actor_appointments', verbose_name='Dodijeljeni izvrsitelj')
    appointment_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TYPE_CHOICES, default='meeting', verbose_name='Tip termina')
    is_all_day       = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Cjelodnevni/Višednevni najam')
    color            = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='#3b82f6', verbose_name='Boja u kalendaru')
    total_price      = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Ukupna cijena')
    payment_method   = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='invoice', verbose_name='Način plaćanja')
    whatsapp_number  = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name='WhatsApp')
    whatsapp_reminder_sent = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    # Baking intake
    token_number = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Limeni broj')
    weight_kg = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name='Tezina (kg)')
    should_salt = models.BooleanField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Soliti')
    intake_notes = models.TextField(blank=True, default='', verbose_name='Napomena prijema')
    intake_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Zaprimljeno')
    intake_by = models.ForeignKey('auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='intakes', verbose_name='Zaprimio')
    ready_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Gotovo')
    picked_up_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Preuzeto')
    email_reminder_sent    = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    reminder_sent_at       = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at       = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at       = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'appointments_appointment'
        verbose_name = 'Termin'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Termini'
        ordering = ['-start_time']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} - {self.start_time.strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M')}"

class BookingService(models.Model):
    SLOT_DURATIONS = [
        (15, '15 min'), (20, '20 min'), (30, '30 min'),
        (45, '45 min'), (60, '1 sat'), (90, '1h 30min'), (120, '2 sata'),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey("companies.Company", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="booking_services", null=True, blank=True)
    name                  = models.CharField(max_length=150, verbose_name='Naziv usluge')
    description           = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='Opis usluge')
    duration              = models.DurationField(verbose_name='Trajanje usluge (HH:MM:SS)')
    price                 = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name='Cijena (EUR)')
    resource_type         = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=BookingResource.RESOURCE_TYPES, default='ljudstvo', verbose_name='Vezano za tip resursa')
    is_active             = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Aktivno za javnost')
    branch_type           = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=BRANCH_TYPES, default='general', verbose_name='Tip djelatnosti')
    slot_duration_minutes = models.PositiveIntegerField(choices=SLOT_DURATIONS, default=15, verbose_name='Korak termina (min)')
    color                 = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='#10b981', verbose_name='Boja usluge')
    icon                  = models.CharField(max_length=60, default='fa-calendar-check', verbose_name='Font Awesome ikona')
    requires_actor    = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Zahtijeva izvrsitelja (radnika)')
    requires_resource = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Zahtijeva fizicki resurs')
    requires_dossier      = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Potreban karton klijenta')
    total_price           = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)
    payment_method        = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='invoice')
    whatsapp_number       = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Usluga'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Usluge'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.duration})"

    @property
    def duration_minutes(self):
        return int(self.duration.total_seconds() // 60)

    @property
    def branch_icon(self):
        return BRANCH_ICONS.get(self.branch_type, 'fa-calendar-check')


class WorkingHours(models.Model):
    DAYS_OF_WEEK = [
        (0, 'Ponedjeljak'), (1, 'Utorak'), (2, 'Srijeda'),
        (3, 'Četvrtak'), (4, 'Petak'), (5, 'Subota'), (6, 'Nedjelja')
    ]
    resource       = models.ForeignKey(BookingResource, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='working_hours', verbose_name='Resurs')
    day_of_week    = models.IntegerField(choices=DAYS_OF_WEEK, verbose_name='Dan u tjednu')
    start_time     = models.TimeField(verbose_name='Početak rada')
    end_time       = models.TimeField(verbose_name='Kraj rada')
    is_day_off     = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Neradni dan')
    total_price    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='invoice')
    whatsapp_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Radno vrijeme'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Radna vremena'
        unique_together = ('resource', 'day_of_week')
        ordering = ['resource', 'day_of_week']

    def __str__(self):
        if self.is_day_off:
            return f"{self.resource.name} - {self.get_day_of_week_display()} (Slobodno)"
        return f"{self.resource.name} - {self.get_day_of_week_display()} ({self.start_time.strftime('%H:%M')} - {self.end_time.strftime('%H:%M')})"

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Unos u karton'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Unosi u karton'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_entry_type_display()}: {self.title}"

    @property
    def entry_icon(self):
        return self.ENTRY_ICONS.get(self.entry_type, 'fa-file')

    @property
    def entry_color(self):
        return self.ENTRY_COLORS.get(self.entry_type, '#6b7280')

from django.db import models


class Product(models.Model):
    """
    Polymorphic Product — single model for ALL domains.
    FOOD: HACCP expiry, packaging enforcement, kalo
    OPTICS: medical attributes (SPH, CYL), no HACCP
    EQUIPMENT: standard stock, no special rules
    SERVICE: no stock tracking
    """
    CATEGORY_CHOICES = [
        ('FOOD', 'Hrana / Grosmarkt'),
        ('OPTICS', 'Optika / Medicinski'),
        ('EQUIPMENT', 'Oprema / Alat'),
        ('SERVICE', 'Usluga'),
        ('OTHER', 'Ostalo'),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='webshop_products',
        verbose_name='Tvrtka',
    )
    sku = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True, verbose_name='SKU')
    name = models.CharField(max_length=300, verbose_name='Naziv')
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='Opis')
    category = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=CATEGORY_CHOICES, default='OTHER',
        verbose_name='Domenski tip',
    )
    price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name='Cijena')
    stock = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Zaliha (legacy)')
    available_qty = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Dostupna kolicina')
    is_rental = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Za iznajmljivanje')
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Aktivno')
    image = models.ImageField(upload_to='products/', blank=True, verbose_name='Slika')
    inventory_item = models.OneToOneField(
        'inventory.InventoryItem',
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='webshop_product',
        verbose_name='Artikl iz zalihe',
    )

    # Domain-specific JSONB attributes
    # FOOD: {"kalo_percent": 2.0}
    # OPTICS: {"requires_sph": true, "requires_cyl": false, "requires_ax": false}
    domain_attrs = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name='Domenski atributi')

    # FOOD-specific
    expiry_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Rok trajanja')
    packaging_unit = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name='Ambalaza')
    packaging_capacity_kg = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Kapacitet ambalaze (kg)',
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'webshop_product'
        verbose_name = 'Proizvod'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Proizvodi'
        ordering = ['name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.category}] {self.name} ({self.sku})"

    @property
    def is_expired(self):
        if not self.expiry_date:
            return False
        from datetime import date
        return self.expiry_date < date.today()

    @property
    def is_food(self):
        return self.category == 'FOOD'

    @property
    def is_optics(self):
        return self.category == 'OPTICS'

from decimal import Decimal

class WorkGroup(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                null=True, blank=True, related_name='work_groups')
    objects = CompanyManager()

    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    manager = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                null=True, blank=True, related_name='managed_groups')
    members = models.ManyToManyField(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, blank=True,
                                     related_name='work_groups')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    class Meta:

class Project(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('ACTIVE', 'Aktivan'), ('PAUSED', 'Pauziran'),
        ('COMPLETED', 'Završen'), ('ARCHIVED', 'Arhiviran'),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                null=True, blank=True, related_name='worktime_projects')
    objects = CompanyManager()

    code = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    client_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='ACTIVE')
    hourly_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal('25.00'))
    is_billable = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    budget_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True,
                                        help_text='Budžet u satima')
    color = models.CharField(max_length=7, default='#3b82f6', help_text='HEX boja za prikaz')
    work_group = models.ForeignKey(WorkGroup, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                    null=True, blank=True, related_name='projects')
    members = models.ManyToManyField(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, blank=True,
                                      related_name='assigned_projects')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.code}] {self.name}"

    @property
    def hours_used(self):
        return self.time_entries.aggregate(s=models.Sum('duration'))['s'] or Decimal('0')

    @property
    def budget_percentage(self):
        if not self.budget_hours or self.budget_hours == 0:
            return 0
        return min(100, round(float(self.hours_used) / float(self.budget_hours) * 100, 1))

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['name']

class TimeEntry(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('DRAFT', 'Skica'), ('PENDING', 'Na čekanju'),
        ('APPROVED', 'Odobreno'), ('REJECTED', 'Odbijeno'),
    ]
    TASK_TYPES = [
        ('DEVELOPMENT', 'Razvoj'), ('DESIGN', 'Dizajn'),
        ('MEETING', 'Sastanak'), ('SUPPORT', 'Podrška'),
        ('ADMIN', 'Administracija'), ('TESTING', 'Testiranje'),
        ('PLANNING', 'Planiranje'), ('TRAINING', 'Edukacija'),
        ('TRAVEL', 'Putovanje'), ('OTHER', 'Ostalo'),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                null=True, blank=True, related_name='time_entries')
    objects = CompanyManager()

    user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                              related_name='time_entries')
    project = models.ForeignKey(Project, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='time_entries')
    date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    start_time = models.TimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    end_time = models.TimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    duration = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2,
                                    validators=[MinValueValidator(Decimal('0.01'))])
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    task_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TASK_TYPES, default='DEVELOPMENT')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='PENDING')
    billing_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal('0'))
    is_billed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_overtime = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    invoice = models.ForeignKey('invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                 null=True, blank=True, related_name='time_entries')
    project_lead = models.ForeignKey('burza.ProjectLead', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                      null=True, blank=True, related_name='time_entries',
                                      verbose_name='Burza projekt')
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                     null=True, blank=True, related_name='approved_entries')
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    rejection_reason = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='Razlog odbijanja')
    tags = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, help_text='Tagovi odvojeni zarezom')
    from_timer = models.BooleanField(default=False, help_text='Kreirano putem štoperice')

    # ── Zakonski podaci (HR Pravilnik NN 55/24 + DE ArbZG/ArbSchG) ──────────
    ABSENCE_TYPES = [
        ('',          '—'),
        ('ANNUAL',    'Godišnji odmor / Jahresurlaub'),
        ('SICK',      'Bolovanje / Krankheit'),
        ('PAID',      'Plaćeni dopust / Bezahlter Sonderurlaub'),
        ('UNPAID',    'Neplaćeni dopust / Unbezahlter Urlaub'),
        ('HOLIDAY',   'Blagdan / Feiertag'),
        ('PATERNITY', 'Očinski dopust / Vaterschaftsurlaub'),
        ('CARE',      'Dopust za njegu / Pflegeurlaub'),
        ('MILITARY',  'Vojna obveza / Wehrdienst'),
        ('ELECTION',  'Izborna odsutnost / Wahlkampf'),
        ('STRIKE',    'Štrajk / Streik'),
        ('LOCKOUT',   'Isključenje / Aussperrung'),
        ('UNJUSTIFIED','Neopravdani izostanak / Unentschuldigtes Fehlen'),
        ('STOPPAGE',  'Zastoj/krivnja poslodavca / Betriebsstörung'),
        ('OTHER',     'Ostalo / Sonstiges'),
    ]
    absence_type   = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ABSENCE_TYPES, blank=True, default='',
                                       verbose_name='Vrsta odsutnosti / Abwesenheitsart')
    is_night_work  = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Noćni rad / Nachtarbeit')
    is_holiday_work= models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Rad na blagdan / Feiertagsarbeit')
    # ── Payroll izračun (auto-popunjava save()) ──────────────────
    regular_hours  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal('0'),
                                         verbose_name='Redovni sati / Reguläre Stunden')
    overtime_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal('0'),
                                         verbose_name='Prekovremeni / Überstunden')
    night_hours    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal('0'),
                                         verbose_name='Noćni sati / Nachtstunden')
    compliance_note = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default='',
                                        verbose_name='Zakonsko upozorenje / Compliance')

    break_minutes  = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0,
                                                       verbose_name='Pauza (min) / Pause (Min)')
    daily_rest_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=4, decimal_places=1, null=True, blank=True,
                                            verbose_name='Dnevni odmor (h) / Ruhezeit (h)')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)


    def calculate_payroll(self):
        """Auto-split hours into regular/overtime/night. Efficient interval math."""
        import datetime as _dt
        dur = self.duration or Decimal('0')

        # Regular vs overtime (threshold from EmployeeProfile or 8h default)
        try:
            emp = self.user.employeeprofile
            daily_limit = emp.daily_hours or Decimal('8')
            base_rate = emp.hourly_rate or Decimal('20')
            ot_rate = emp.overtime_rate or Decimal('30')
        except Exception:
            daily_limit = Decimal('8')
            base_rate = Decimal('20')
            ot_rate = Decimal('30')

        if dur > daily_limit:
            self.regular_hours = daily_limit
            self.overtime_hours = dur - daily_limit
            self.is_overtime = True
        else:
            self.regular_hours = dur
            self.overtime_hours = Decimal('0')

        # Night hours — interval overlap math (22:00-06:00), no minute-loop
        night = Decimal('0')
        if self.start_time and self.end_time:
            dummy = _dt.date(2000, 1, 1)
            s = _dt.datetime.combine(dummy, self.start_time)
            e = _dt.datetime.combine(dummy, self.end_time)
            if e <= s:
                e += _dt.timedelta(days=1)
            total_minutes = (e - s).total_seconds() / 60

            # Night windows: 00:00-06:00 (day 0), 22:00-24:00 (day 0), 00:00-06:00 (day 1)
            night_ranges = [
                (_dt.datetime(2000, 1, 1, 0, 0), _dt.datetime(2000, 1, 1, 6, 0)),
                (_dt.datetime(2000, 1, 1, 22, 0), _dt.datetime(2000, 1, 2, 0, 0)),
                (_dt.datetime(2000, 1, 2, 0, 0), _dt.datetime(2000, 1, 2, 6, 0)),
            ]
            night_mins = 0
            for ns, ne in night_ranges:
                overlap_start = max(s, ns)
                overlap_end = min(e, ne)
                if overlap_start < overlap_end:
                    night_mins += (overlap_end - overlap_start).total_seconds() / 60
            night = Decimal(str(round(night_mins / 60, 2)))

        self.night_hours = night
        if night > 0:
            self.is_night_work = True

        # Billing amount
        night_bonus = base_rate * Decimal('0.25')
        self.billing_amount = (
            self.regular_hours * base_rate
            + self.overtime_hours * ot_rate
            + self.night_hours * night_bonus
        )

        # Compliance checks (ArbZG §3 / ZOR čl.65)
        warnings = []
        if dur > Decimal('10'):
            warnings.append('ArbZG §3: >10h rada')
        if dur > Decimal('12'):
            warnings.append('KRITIČNO: >12h rada')
        if self.break_minutes < 30 and dur >= Decimal('6'):
            warnings.append('ArbZG §4: pauza <30min za 6+h')
        if self.break_minutes < 45 and dur >= Decimal('9'):
            warnings.append('ArbZG §4: pauza <45min za 9+h')
        self.compliance_note = '; '.join(warnings)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.duration and self.duration > 0 and not self.absence_type:
            self.calculate_payroll()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} | {self.project.code} | {self.date} | {self.duration}h"

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-date', '-start_time']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['user', 'date']),
            models.Index(fields=['project', 'date']),
            models.Index(fields=['status']),
        ]


class ActiveTimer(models.Model):
    """Aktivna štoperica - jedan korisnik može imati samo jedan aktivan timer"""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('RUNNING', 'Aktivan'),
        ('PAUSED', 'Pauziran'),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                null=True, blank=True, related_name='active_timers')
    objects = CompanyManager()

    user = models.OneToOneField(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                 related_name='active_timer')
    project = models.ForeignKey(Project, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    project_lead = models.ForeignKey('burza.ProjectLead', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                      null=True, blank=True, related_name='active_timers',
                                      verbose_name='Burza projekt')
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    task_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TimeEntry.TASK_TYPES, default='DEVELOPMENT')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='RUNNING')
    started_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    paused_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    accumulated_seconds = models.IntegerField(default=0,
        help_text='Akumulirane sekunde iz prethodnih pauza')
    tags = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    @property
    def elapsed_seconds(self):
        if self.status == 'PAUSED':
            return self.accumulated_seconds
        now = timezone.now()
        running = int((now - self.started_at).total_seconds())
        return self.accumulated_seconds + running

    @property
    def elapsed_display(self):
        total = self.elapsed_seconds
        hours = total // 3600
        minutes = (total % 3600) // 60
        seconds = total % 60
        return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{seconds:02d}"

    @property
    def elapsed_hours(self):
        return Decimal(str(round(self.elapsed_seconds / 3600, 2)))

    def pause(self):
        if self.status == 'RUNNING':
            now = timezone.now()
            running = int((now - self.started_at).total_seconds())
            self.accumulated_seconds += running
            self.paused_at = now
            self.status = 'PAUSED'
            self.save()

    def resume(self):
        if self.status == 'PAUSED':
            self.started_at = timezone.now()
            self.paused_at = None
            self.status = 'RUNNING'
            self.save()

    def stop_and_save(self):
        duration = self.elapsed_hours
        if duration < Decimal('0.01'):
            duration = Decimal('0.01')
        now = timezone.now()
        entry_date = self.created_at.date()
        start_time = self.created_at.time()
        end_time = now.time()
        try:
            rate = self.user.employeeprofile.hourly_rate
        except Exception:
            rate = self.project.hourly_rate
        billing = duration * rate if self.project.is_billable else Decimal('0')
        entry = TimeEntry.objects.create(
            company=self.company,
            user=self.user, project=self.project, date=entry_date,
            start_time=start_time, end_time=end_time, duration=duration,
            description=self.description, task_type=self.task_type,
            status='PENDING', billing_amount=billing,
            from_timer=True, tags=self.tags,
            project_lead=self.project_lead,
        )
        self.delete()
        return entry

    def discard(self):
        self.delete()

    def __str__(self):
        return f"⏱ {self.user.username} | {self.project.code} | {self.elapsed_display}"

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Aktivna štoperica'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Aktivne štoperice'

# HYBRID TIME TRACKING — Clock In/Out + Geofence + ArbZG

class TimeLog(models.Model):
    """Physical clock-in/clock-out log with GPS verification."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('OPEN', 'Aktivna smjena'),
        ('CLOSED', 'Zatvorena'),
        ('FLAGGED', 'Zastavica'),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                null=True, blank=True, related_name='time_logs')
    user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                             related_name='time_logs')
    start_time = models.DateTimeField()
    end_time = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    date = models.DateField(db_index=True)

    # GPS at clock-in/out
    clock_in_lat = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    clock_in_lon = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    clock_out_lat = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    clock_out_lon = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    clock_in_distance = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True,
        help_text='Udaljenost od firme pri prijavi (m)')
    clock_out_distance = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True,
        help_text='Udaljenost od firme pri odjavi (m)')

    # Calculated fields
    gross_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='Bruto sati (bez odbitka)')
    break_minutes = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0,
        verbose_name='ArbZG pauza (min)')
    payable_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='Neto sati za placu')

    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='OPEN')
    is_audited = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    audit_flag = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True,
        help_text='Revizorska zastavica')
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'worktime_timelog'
        ordering = ['-date', '-start_time']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['user', 'date']),
            models.Index(fields=['status']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.username} | {self.date} | {self.gross_hours}h"

    def calculate_arbzg_break(self):
        """
        ArbZG section 4 — mandatory break deduction:
        - 6-9 hours work: 30 min break
        - >9 hours work: 45 min break
        - <=6 hours: no mandatory break
        """
        if self.gross_hours <= 6:
            return 0
        elif self.gross_hours <= 9:
            return 30
        else:
            return 45

    def close_shift(self):
        """Close shift, calculate ArbZG break, compute payable hours."""
        if not self.end_time or not self.start_time:
            return
        delta = self.end_time - self.start_time
        self.gross_hours = Decimal(str(round(delta.total_seconds() / 3600, 2)))
        self.break_minutes = self.calculate_arbzg_break()
        break_hours = Decimal(str(self.break_minutes)) / Decimal('60')
        self.payable_hours = self.gross_hours - break_hours
        if self.payable_hours < 0:
            self.payable_hours = Decimal('0')
        self.status = 'CLOSED'
        self.save()


class ActivityEvent(models.Model):
    """Physical proof-of-work events from IoT/scanners/app actions."""
    ACTION_CHOICES = [
        ('GENESIS_SCAN', 'Genesis QR sken'),
        ('PALLET_SCAN', 'Sken palete'),

                                null=True, blank=True, related_name='activity_events')

                             related_name='activity_events')

class TravelOrder(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("draft", "Nacrt"),
        ("approved", "Odobren"),
        ("completed", "Završen"),
        ("cancelled", "Otkazan"),
    ]
    TRANSPORT_CHOICES = [
        ("car_personal", "Osobni automobil"),
        ("car_company", "Službeni automobil"),
        ("bus", "Autobus"),
        ("train", "Vlak"),
        ("plane", "Avion"),
        ("other", "Ostalo"),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey("companies.Company", on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                null=True, blank=True, related_name="travel_orders")
    objects = CompanyManager()
    employee = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                 related_name="travel_orders")
    order_number = models.CharField(max_length=50)  # PN-2026-0001

    destination = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    purpose = models.TextField()

    departure_date = models.DateTimeField()
    return_date = models.DateTimeField()

    transport_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=TRANSPORT_CHOICES, default="car_personal")

    # Costs
    distance_km = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=1, default=0)
    per_diem_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal("26.55"))
    per_diem_days = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=1, default=0)
    per_diem_total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    mileage_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=Decimal("0.40"))
    mileage_total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    other_costs = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="draft")
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                    null=True, blank=True, related_name="approved_travel_orders")
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-departure_date"]
        unique_together = [["company", "order_number"]]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.order_number} - {self.destination}"

    def calculate_costs(self):
        self.per_diem_total = self.per_diem_days * self.per_diem_rate
        self.mileage_total = self.distance_km * self.mileage_rate
        self.total_cost = self.per_diem_total + self.mileage_total + self.other_costs

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.calculate_costs()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @staticmethod
    def next_order_number(company):
        year = timezone.now().year
        last = TravelOrder.objects.filter(
            company=company,
            order_number__startswith=f"PN-{year}-"
        ).order_by("-order_number").first()
        if last:
            try:
                num = int(last.order_number.split("-")[-1]) + 1
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                num = 1
        else:
            num = 1
        return f"PN-{year}-{num:04d}"

=== ./tracking/models.py ===
import uuid
from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.contenttypes.fields import GenericForeignKey
from django.contrib.contenttypes.models import ContentType


class TrackingStatus(models.Model):
    TRACKING_TYPES = [
        ('food_order', 'Narudzba hrane'),
        ('delivery', 'Dostava'),
        ('intervention', 'Hitna intervencija'),
        ('work_order', 'Radni nalog'),
        ('expiry', 'Istek roka'),
        ('shipment', 'Otpremnica / Isporuka'),
    ]
    tracking_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=TRACKING_TYPES)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    slug = models.SlugField(max_length=100)
    order = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    icon = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True)
    color = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='#c9a84c')

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['tracking_type', 'order']
        unique_together = ['tracking_type', 'slug']
        verbose_name = 'Status pracenja'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Statusi pracenja'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.get_tracking_type_display()} / {self.name}'


class TrackedItem(models.Model):
    content_type = models.ForeignKey(
        ContentType, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
    )
    object_id = models.CharField(max_length=64)
    content_object = GenericForeignKey('content_type', 'object_id')

    tracking_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=TrackingStatus.TRACKING_TYPES)
    current_status = models.ForeignKey(
        TrackingStatus, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='tracked_items',
    )
    latitude = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=6, null=True, blank=True)
    longitude = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=6, null=True, blank=True)
    public_token = models.CharField(max_length=32, unique=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Praceni objekt'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Praceni objekti'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.get_tracking_type_display()} #{self.pk}'

    # Logistics fields (used for shipment tracking type)
    driver_name   = models.CharField('Vozač', max_length=100, blank=True)
    vehicle_plate = models.CharField('Registracija', max_length=20, blank=True)
    tracking_url  = models.URLField('Vanjski tracking link', blank=True)
    DELIVERY_METHOD_CHOICES = [
        ('internal', 'Vlastito vozilo'),
        ('bus', 'Autobusna linija'),
        ('taxi', 'Taxi / brza dostava'),
        ('crowd', 'Crowd-shipping'),
        ('courier', 'Kurirska sluzba'),
        ('pickup', 'Osobno preuzimanje'),
    ]
    delivery_method = models.CharField(
        'Nacin dostave', max_length=20,
        choices=DELIVERY_METHOD_CHOICES, blank=True,
    )

    dispatched_at = models.DateTimeField('Polazak', null=True, blank=True)
    delivered_at  = models.DateTimeField('Isporuka', null=True, blank=True)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.public_token:
            self.public_token = uuid.uuid4().hex[:32]
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def update_status(self, new_status, user=None, note=''):
        self.current_status = new_status
        self.save(update_fields=['current_status', 'updated_at'])
        return TrackingEvent.objects.create(
            tracked_item=self,
            status=new_status,
            user=user,
            note=note,
        )


class TrackingEvent(models.Model):
    tracked_item = models.ForeignKey(
        TrackedItem, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='events',
    )
    status = models.ForeignKey(TrackingStatus, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
    )
    note = models.TextField(blank=True)
    latitude = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=6, null=True, blank=True)
    longitude = models.DecimalField(max_digits=9, decimal_places=6, null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Dogadaj pracenja'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Dogadaji pracenja'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.tracked_item} -> {self.status.name}'

"""
POS Kasa — Point of Sale module
Podržava HR fiskalizaciju, bar kod skeniranje i integraciju s invoicing app.
"""
import uuid
from decimal import Decimal

from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _

from core.managers import CompanyManager


class POSTerminal(models.Model):
    """
    Fizička blagajna / kasa.
    Jedna tvrtka može imati više kasa (npr. Kasa 1, Kasa 2).
    Svaka kasa je vezana uz BusinessLocation radi fiskalizacije.
    """
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='pos_terminals',
        verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects = CompanyManager()
    business_location = models.ForeignKey(
        'invoicing.BusinessLocation', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='pos_terminals',
        verbose_name=_('Poslovni prostor (za fiskalizaciju)'),
    )
    name          = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('Naziv kase'), help_text='Npr. Kasa 1, Šank, Blagajna')
    terminal_code = models.CharField(max_length=20, verbose_name=_('Kod terminala'), help_text='Jedinstveni kod npr. KS001')
    description   = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis / Lokacija'))
    is_active     = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivna'))
    branch        = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.CompanyBranch',
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='pos_terminals',
        verbose_name='Podružnica',
    )
    pos_type      = models.CharField(
        max_length=10,
        choices=[('quick', 'Brza blagajna'), ('retail', 'Maloprodaja'), ('restaurant', 'Restoran'), ('field', 'Terenska prodaja'), ('service', 'Usluge'), ('reception', 'Recepcija')],
        default='retail',
        verbose_name=_('Tip POS sučelja'),
    )
    created_at    = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('POS terminal (kasa)')
        verbose_name_plural = _('POS terminali (kase)')
        unique_together     = [('company', 'terminal_code')]
        ordering            = ['company', 'name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.name} ({self.terminal_code})'


class POSSession(models.Model):
    """
    Smjena / dnevno otvaranje blagajne.
    Svaka smjena se otvara i zatvara — obavezno za fiskalizaciju (Z-izvješće).
    """
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        OPEN   = 'open',   _('Otvorena')
        CLOSED = 'closed', _('Zatvorena')
        Z_DONE = 'z_done', _('Z-izvješće generirano')

    terminal      = models.ForeignKey(POSTerminal, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                       related_name='sessions', verbose_name=_('Terminal'))
    operator      = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                       null=True, related_name='pos_sessions', verbose_name=_('Operater'))
    session_number = models.PositiveIntegerField(verbose_name=_('Broj smjene'))

    opened_at     = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('Otvorena u'))
    closed_at     = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Zatvorena u'))

    opening_cash  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                         verbose_name=_('Gotovina pri otvaranju (€)'))
    closing_cash  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                         verbose_name=_('Prebrojana gotovina pri zatvaranju (€)'))

    status        = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Status.choices,
                                      default=Status.OPEN, verbose_name=_('Status'))
    z_report_number = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Broj Z-izvješća'))
    notes         = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomene'))

    # Terenska prodaja
    is_field_session  = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Terenska smjena'))
    vehicle_plate     = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Reg. oznaka vozila'))
    route_name        = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Ruta'))
    loaded_items_json = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Utovareni artikli'))
    returned_items_json = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Vraceni artikli'))

    # Multi-operator: blagajnici koji su radili u ovoj smjeni
    active_cashiers = models.ManyToManyField(
        'pos.POSCashier', blank=True,
        related_name='sessions_worked',
        verbose_name=_('Blagajnici u smjeni'),
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('POS smjena')
        verbose_name_plural = _('POS smjene')
        unique_together     = [('terminal', 'session_number')]
        ordering            = ['-opened_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Smjena #{self.session_number} – {self.terminal} ({self.get_status_display()})'

    @property
    def total_sales(self):
        return self.receipts.filter(
            status=POSReceipt.Status.PAID
        ).aggregate(models.Sum('total'))['total__sum'] or Decimal('0.00')

    @property
    def total_cash_sales(self):
        return self.receipts.filter(
            status=POSReceipt.Status.PAID,
            payment_lines__method=POSPaymentLine.Method.CASH,
        ).aggregate(models.Sum('payment_lines__amount'))['payment_lines__amount__sum'] or Decimal('0.00')

    @property
    def total_deposits_amount(self):
        return sum(d.amount for d in self.cash_deposits.filter(deposit_type='deposit'))

    @property
    def total_withdrawals_amount(self):
        return sum(d.amount for d in self.cash_deposits.filter(deposit_type='withdrawal'))

    @property
    def expected_closing_cash(self):
        return self.opening_cash + self.total_cash_sales + self.total_deposits_amount - self.total_withdrawals_amount

    @property
    def cash_difference(self):
        return self.closing_cash - self.expected_closing_cash


class POSReceipt(models.Model):
    """
    Jedan račun / bon na blagajni.
    Automatski kreira Invoice (+ fiskalizira ga za HR).
    """
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        OPEN      = 'open',      _('Otvoreni bon')
        PAID      = 'paid',      _('Plaćen')
        CANCELLED = 'cancelled', _('Storniran / Otkazan')
        REFUNDED  = 'refunded',  _('Povrat')

    uuid           = models.UUIDField(default=uuid.uuid4, unique=True, editable=False)
    session        = models.ForeignKey(POSSession, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                        related_name='receipts', verbose_name=_('Smjena'))
    receipt_number = models.PositiveIntegerField(verbose_name=_('Broj bona'))

    # Veza na Invoice (kreira se automatski pri plaćanju + fiskalizira)
    invoice        = models.OneToOneField(
        'invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='pos_receipt',
        verbose_name=_('Fiskalni račun'),
    )

    # Iznosi (denormalizirani za brzinu)
    subtotal   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Iznos bez PDV'))
    tax_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('PDV'))
    total      = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Ukupno'))
    discount   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Popust'))

    status     = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Status.choices,
                                   default=Status.OPEN, verbose_name=_('Status'))
    table_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Stol / Zona'),
                                     help_text=_('Za ugostiteljstvo: broj stola'))
    note       = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomena na bonu'))

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    paid_at        = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Plaćen u'))
    cancel_reason  = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Razlog storniranja'))

    # Multi-cashier: tko je izdao ovaj racun (PIN switch unutar smjene)
    cashier        = models.ForeignKey(
        'pos.POSCashier', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='receipts',
        verbose_name=_('Blagajnik'),
    )

    # Fiskalizacija (FINA CIS)
    zki            = models.CharField(max_length=32, null=True, blank=True,
                                       verbose_name='ZKI (Zaštitni kod izdavatelja)')
    jir            = models.CharField(max_length=40, null=True, blank=True,
                                       verbose_name='JIR (Jedinstveni identifikator računa)')
    fiscal_datetime = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True,
                                            verbose_name='Datum/vrijeme fiskalizacije')

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('POS bon / račun')
        verbose_name_plural = _('POS bonovi / računi')
        unique_together     = [('session', 'receipt_number')]
        ordering            = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Bon #{self.receipt_number} – {self.total} EUR ({self.get_status_display()})'

    def recalculate(self):
        """Reizračunaj iznose iz stavki."""
        items = self.items.all()
        self.subtotal = sum(
            i.quantity * i.unit_price * (1 - i.discount_percent / 100)
            for i in items
        )
        self.tax_amount = sum(
            i.quantity * i.unit_price * (1 - i.discount_percent / 100) * (i.tax_rate / 100)
            for i in items
        )
        self.total = self.subtotal + self.tax_amount - self.discount
        return self


class POSReceiptItem(models.Model):
    """
    Stavka na bonu — artikl skeniran barkodom ili dodan ručno.
    """
    receipt        = models.ForeignKey(POSReceipt, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                        related_name='items', verbose_name=_('Bon'))
    # Direktna veza na artikl iz zalihe (via barcode scan)
    inventory_item = models.ForeignKey(
        'inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='pos_items',
        verbose_name=_('Artikl (zaliha)'),
    )
    barcode        = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Barkod'),
                                       help_text=_('Uneseno skeniranjem'))
    description    = models.CharField(max_length=255, verbose_name=_('Naziv artikla'))
    quantity       = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=3, default=1, verbose_name=_('Količina'))
    unit           = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='kom', verbose_name=_('Jed.mjera'))
    unit_price     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Cijena bez PDV'))
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Popust %'))
    tax_rate       = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25, verbose_name=_('PDV %'))
    tax_rate_obj   = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.TaxRate', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('PDV stopa'),
    )

    # ── DSFinV-K flags (auto-set from InventoryItem ili rucno) ────────────
    is_pfand_return = models.BooleanField(
        default=False,
        verbose_name=_("Povrat Pfanda"),
        help_text=_("Negativan iznos — DSFinV-K GV_TYP=Pfandrueckzahlung"),
    )
    is_tip = models.BooleanField(
        default=False,
        verbose_name=_("Napojnica"),
        help_text=_("DSFinV-K GV_TYP=TrinkgeldAN ili TrinkgeldAG"),
    )
    is_tip_employer = models.BooleanField(
        default=False,
        verbose_name=_("Napojnica vlasnika (AG)"),
    )

    total_price    = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                          verbose_name=_('Ukupno s PDV'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Stavka bona')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke bona')

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.description} x{self.quantity} = {self.total_price}'

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.tax_rate_obj_id:
            self.tax_rate = self.tax_rate_obj.rate
        if self.inventory_item_id and self.inventory_item:
            if not self.description:
                self.description = self.inventory_item.name
            if not self.barcode:
                self.barcode = self.inventory_item.barcode
        net = Decimal(str(self.quantity)) * Decimal(str(self.unit_price)) * (
            1 - Decimal(str(self.discount_percent)) / 100
        )
        self.total_price = round(net * (1 + Decimal(str(self.tax_rate)) / 100), 2)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)


class POSPaymentLine(models.Model):
    """
    Kako je plaćen bon — podržava split plaćanja (npr. 20€ gotovina + 30€ kartica).
    """
    class Method(models.TextChoices):
        CASH       = 'CASH',       _('Gotovina')
        CARD       = 'CARD',       _('Kartica')
        MOBILE     = 'MOBILE',     _('Mobilno plaćanje (mPOS)')
        VOUCHER    = 'VOUCHER',    _('Bon / Voucher')
        ACCOUNT    = 'ACCOUNT',    _('Na račun (klijent)')
        CRYPTO     = 'CRYPTO',     _('Kriptovalute')
        REVOLUT    = 'REVOLUT',    _('Revolut')
        AIRCASH    = 'AIRCASH',    _('Aircash')

    receipt        = models.ForeignKey(POSReceipt, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                        related_name='payment_lines', verbose_name=_('Bon'))
    method         = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Method.choices, verbose_name=_('Način plaćanja'))
    amount         = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Iznos'))
    change_given   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                          verbose_name=_('Vraćeno kusur'))
    card_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True,
                                       verbose_name=_('Referenca kartice / autorizacijski kod'))
    created_at     = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Linija plaćanja')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Linije plaćanja')

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.get_method_display()} {self.amount} EUR'

class POSCashier(models.Model):
    """Blagajnik s PIN kodom i dozvolama."""
    company      = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='pos_cashiers', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    name         = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('Ime blagajnika'))
    pin_hash     = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, verbose_name=_('PIN hash'))
    can_void     = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Može stornirati'))
    can_discount = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Može davati popust'))
    max_discount_percent = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Max popust (%)'),
    )
    is_active    = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivan'))
    user         = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='pos_cashier_profiles',
        verbose_name=_('Django korisnik'),
        help_text=_('Povezani korisnik za evidenciju radnog vremena'),
    )
    clock_in_at  = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Prijava u'))
    created_at   = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('POS blagajnik')
        verbose_name_plural = _('POS blagajnici')
        ordering            = ['name']

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    def set_pin(self, pin_str):
        import hashlib, secrets
        salt = secrets.token_hex(4)  # 8 hex chars
        dk = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac('sha256', pin_str.strip().encode(), salt.encode(), 150_000)
        # Format: p$8salt$24hash = ~36 chars (fits varchar 64)
        self.pin_hash = 'p$%s$%s' % (salt, dk.hex()[:24])

    def check_pin(self, pin_str):
        import hashlib
        pin_str = pin_str.strip()
        if not self.pin_hash:
            return False
        # Backward compat: stari SHA256 hash (64 hex chars, no $)
        if '$' not in self.pin_hash:
            if self.pin_hash == hashlib.sha256(pin_str.encode()).hexdigest():
                # Auto-upgrade to PBKDF2
                self.set_pin(pin_str)
                self.save(update_fields=['pin_hash'])
                return True
            return False
        # Novi PBKDF2 format: p$salt$hash
        parts = self.pin_hash.split('$')
        if len(parts) != 3:
            return False
        _, salt, stored_hash = parts
        dk = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac('sha256', pin_str.encode(), salt.encode(), 150_000)
        return dk.hex()[:24] == stored_hash

    )

        max_length=10, choices=TABLE_TYPES, default='table',

        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):

            self.tracking_token = uuid.uuid4().hex[:32]
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

class GuestLoyalty(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='guest_loyalties', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects = CompanyManager()
    program = models.ForeignKey(LoyaltyProgram, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='entries')
    guest_email = models.EmailField(verbose_name=_('Email gosta'))
    guest_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    stamps = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Pecati'))
    rewards_claimed = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Nagrade iskoristene'))
    total_spent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    last_visit = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('company', 'program', 'guest_email')
        verbose_name = _('Lojalnost gosta')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Lojalnosti gostiju')

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.guest_email} - {self.program.name} ({self.stamps}/{self.program.stamps_required})'

    @property
    def has_reward(self):
        return self.stamps >= self.program.stamps_required

    @property
    def stamps_remaining(self):
        return max(0, self.program.stamps_required - self.stamps)


# ======================================================================
# WAITER SHIFTS & HEARTBEAT
# ======================================================================

class WaiterShift(models.Model):
    """Tracks waiter shifts with heartbeat pings every 5 min."""
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='waiter_shifts', verbose_name=_('Konobar'),
    )
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='waiter_shifts', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects = CompanyManager()
    started_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('Pocetak smjene'))
    ended_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kraj smjene'))
    last_heartbeat = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('Zadnji ping'))
    heartbeat_count = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Broj pingova'))
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivna'))
    total_orders_claimed = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    total_orders_delivered = models.IntegerField(default=0)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Smjena konobara')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Smjene konobara')
        ordering = ['-started_at']

    def __str__(self):
        name = self.user.get_full_name() or self.user.username
        return f'{name} - {self.started_at:%d.%m. %H:%M}'

    @property
    def duration_minutes(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        end = self.ended_at or timezone.now()
        return int((end - self.started_at).total_seconds() / 60)

    @property
    def duration_display(self):
        mins = self.duration_minutes
        h, m = divmod(mins, 60)
        return f'{h}h {m:02d}min'

    @property
    def is_heartbeat_stale(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        if not self.is_active:
            return False
        return (timezone.now() - self.last_heartbeat).total_seconds() > 600  # 10 min = 2 missed


# EXPIRY TRACKING — Rokovi trajanja artikala
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class ExpiryBatch(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='expiry_batches', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects = CompanyManager()
    inventory_item = models.ForeignKey(
        'inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='expiry_batches', verbose_name=_('Artikl'),
    )
    batch_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Broj sarze'))
    quantity = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Kolicina'),
    )
    expiry_date = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Datum isteka'))
    alert_days_before = models.PositiveIntegerField(
        default=3, verbose_name=_('Upozorenje dana prije'),
    )
    is_removed = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Uklonjeno'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Sarzni rok')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Sarzni rokovi')
        ordering = ['expiry_date']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.inventory_item.name} - {self.batch_number} (exp: {self.expiry_date})'

    @property
    def days_until_expiry(self):
        from datetime import date
        return (self.expiry_date - date.today()).days

    @property
    def is_expired(self):
        return self.days_until_expiry < 0

    @property
    def is_warning(self):
        d = self.days_until_expiry
        return 0 <= d <= self.alert_days_before

# VAUČERI / BONOVI
