class Voucher(models.Model):
    """Vaučer / Bon s određenom vrijednosti koji se koristi kao metoda plaćanja."""
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='vouchers', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects = CompanyManager()
    code            = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, verbose_name=_('Kod vaučera'))
    original_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Izvorna vrijednost (€)'))
    remaining       = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Preostalo (€)'))
    expiry_date     = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Rok valjanosti'))
    is_active       = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivan'))
    notes           = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomena'))
    created_by      = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='created_vouchers', verbose_name=_('Kreirao'),
    )
    created_at      = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Vaučer')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Vaučeri')
        ordering            = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.code} — {self.remaining}/{self.original_amount} EUR'

    @property
    def is_expired(self):
        if not self.expiry_date:
            return False
        from datetime import date
        return self.expiry_date < date.today()

    @property
    def is_usable(self):
        return self.is_active and not self.is_expired and self.remaining > 0

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# POSDailyReport — Trajno spremanje dnevnih utržaka
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class POSDailyReport(models.Model):
    """
    Agregirani dnevni izvještaj blagajne.
    Auto-generira se na zatvaranju sesije.
    Sprema sve ukupne iznose umjesto on-the-fly izračuna.
    """
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='pos_daily_reports', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    terminal = models.ForeignKey(
        POSTerminal, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='daily_reports', verbose_name=_('Terminal'),
    )
    session = models.OneToOneField(
        POSSession, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='daily_report', verbose_name=_('Smjena'),
    )
    date = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Datum'))
    # Ukupni iznosi
    total_revenue = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Ukupan promet'),
    )
    cash_total = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Gotovina'),
    )
    card_total = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Kartica'),
    )
    mobile_total = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Mobilno plaćanje'),
    )
    voucher_total = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Vaučeri'),
    )
    account_total = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Na račun'),
    )
    # Brojevi
    receipt_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Broj računa'))
    cancelled_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Stornirani'))
    # Prosjek
    average_ticket = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Prosječan račun'),
    )
    # Gotovina
    opening_cash = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Početna gotovina'),
    )
    closing_cash = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Završna gotovina'),
    )
    cash_difference = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Razlika gotovine'),
    )
    # Detaljni breakdowni
    tax_breakdown = models.JSONField(
        default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name=_('PDV razrada'),
        help_text=_('{"25.00": {"base": 100, "tax": 25, "total": 125}, ...}'),
    )
    cashier_breakdown = models.JSONField(
        default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Po blagajnicima'),
        help_text=_('{"Ana": {"count": 10, "total": 500}, ...}'),
    )
    # Z-report PDF
    z_report_pdf = models.FileField(
        upload_to='pos/zreports/%Y/%m/', blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Z-report PDF'),
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Dnevni izvještaj POS')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Dnevni izvještaji POS')
        ordering = ['-date', '-created_at']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['company', 'date']),
            models.Index(fields=['terminal', 'date']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return '%s — %s — %s EUR' % (self.date, self.terminal.name, self.total_revenue)

    @classmethod
    def generate_from_session(cls, session):
        """Generiraj dnevni izvještaj iz zatvorene sesije."""
        from decimal import Decimal
        from django.db.models import Sum, Count, Q

        receipts = session.receipts.filter(status='paid')
        cancelled = session.receipts.filter(status='cancelled').count()

        # Payment breakdown
        from pos.models import POSPaymentLine
        payment_qs = POSPaymentLine.objects.filter(receipt__session=session, receipt__status='paid')
        cash = payment_qs.filter(method='CASH').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        card = payment_qs.filter(method='CARD').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        mobile = payment_qs.filter(method='MOBILE').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        voucher = payment_qs.filter(method='VOUCHER').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        account = payment_qs.filter(method='ACCOUNT').aggregate(t=Sum('amount'))['t'] or Decimal('0')
        total = cash + card + mobile + voucher + account
        count = receipts.count()

        # Tax breakdown
        from pos.models import POSReceiptItem
        tax_items = POSReceiptItem.objects.filter(receipt__session=session, receipt__status='paid')
        tax_bd = {}
        for item in tax_items:
            rate_key = str(item.tax_rate)
            if rate_key not in tax_bd:
                tax_bd[rate_key] = {'base': 0, 'tax': 0, 'total': 0}
            net = float(item.total_price / (1 + item.tax_rate / 100)) if item.tax_rate else float(item.total_price)
            tax_amount = float(item.total_price) - net
            tax_bd[rate_key]['base'] = round(tax_bd[rate_key]['base'] + net, 2)
            tax_bd[rate_key]['tax'] = round(tax_bd[rate_key]['tax'] + tax_amount, 2)
            tax_bd[rate_key]['total'] = round(tax_bd[rate_key]['total'] + float(item.total_price), 2)

        # Cashier breakdown
        cashier_bd = {}
        for r in receipts.select_related('cashier'):
            name = r.cashier.name if r.cashier else r.note or 'Nepoznat'
            if name not in cashier_bd:
                cashier_bd[name] = {'count': 0, 'total': 0}
            cashier_bd[name]['count'] += 1
            cashier_bd[name]['total'] = round(cashier_bd[name]['total'] + float(r.total), 2)

        report, created = cls.objects.update_or_create(
            session=session,
            defaults={
                'company': session.terminal.company,
                'terminal': session.terminal,
                'date': (session.closed_at or session.opened_at).date(),
                'total_revenue': total,
                'cash_total': cash,
                'card_total': card,
                'mobile_total': mobile,
                'voucher_total': voucher,
                'account_total': account,
                'receipt_count': count,
                'cancelled_count': cancelled,
                'average_ticket': round(total / count, 2) if count else Decimal('0'),
                'opening_cash': session.opening_cash,
                'closing_cash': session.closing_cash or Decimal('0'),
                'cash_difference': (session.closing_cash or Decimal('0')) - session.expected_closing_cash,
                'tax_breakdown': tax_bd,
                'cashier_breakdown': cashier_bd,
            },
        )
        return report

"""
Virtualni Ured — Klijentski dashboard za skeniranu postu i dokumente.
App: virtual_office/

from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings


class VirtualMailbox(models.Model):
    """Per-company virtual office konfiguracija."""
    company = models.OneToOneField('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                    related_name='virtual_mailbox')
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    address_line = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True,
        help_text='Adresa virtualnog ureda (npr. Kappa centar, Cepin)')
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Virtualni pretinac'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Virtualni pretinci'

    def __str__(self):

class IncomingMail(models.Model):
    """Pristigla posta — skenirana i pohranjena."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('received', 'Zaprimljeno'),
        ('scanned', 'Skenirano'),
        ('forwarded', 'Proslijedjeno'),
        ('archived', 'Arhivirano'),
    ]
    MAIL_TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('letter', 'Pismo'),
        ('invoice', 'Racun'),
        ('notice', 'Obavijest'),
        ('tax', 'Porezna uprava'),
        ('court', 'Sud / Odvjetnik'),
        ('bank', 'Banka'),
        ('insurance', 'Osiguranje'),
        ('package', 'Paket'),
        ('other', 'Ostalo'),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                 related_name='incoming_mail')
    received_date = models.DateField()
    sender = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True,
        help_text='Posiljatelj (ako je vidljivo na omotnici)')
    mail_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=MAIL_TYPE_CHOICES, default='letter')
    subject = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True,
        help_text='Kratak opis sadrzaja')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='received')

    # Skenirani dokument
    scanned_file = models.FileField(upload_to='virtual_office/scans/%Y/%m/', blank=True)
    scan_pages = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)

    # Tko je obradio
    processed_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                      null=True, blank=True, related_name='processed_mail')
    processed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Klijent je pregledao?
    client_viewed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    client_viewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-received_date', '-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Pristigla posta'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Pristigla posta'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.get_mail_type_display()}: {self.sender or "Nepoznat"} ({self.received_date})'

        ('sms', 'SMS'),
        ('push', 'Push obavijest'),
    ])

    is_read = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Obavijest o posti'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Obavijesti o posti'

"""
traceability/models.py - Farm-to-Fork traceability

Svaki proizvod ima lanac događaja od berbe do stola.
Gost u restoranu skenira QR → vidi cijeli put proizvoda.
"""
import uuid

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class ProductTrace(models.Model):
    """Kompletna traceability za jedan proizvod u jednoj narudžbi."""
    # Veze
    product = models.ForeignKey(
        'marketplace.MarketplaceProduct', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='traces', verbose_name=_('Proizvod')
    )
    order_item = models.ForeignKey(
        'marketplace.MarketplaceOrderItem', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='traces',
        verbose_name=_('Stavka narudžbe')
    )

    # Proizvođač
    producer_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        verbose_name=_('Proizvođač (OPG)')
    )
    producer_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv OPG-a'))
    producer_region = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Regija'))
    producer_story = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Priča o OPG-u'))
    producer_photo = models.ImageField(upload_to='traceability/producers/', blank=True, null=True)

    # Proizvodnja
    harvest_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Datum berbe'))
    production_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Datum proizvodnje'))
    lot_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Broj šarže'))
    is_organic = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Ekološki'))
    certifications = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Certifikati'))

    # Transport
    cold_chain_maintained = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Hladni lanac održan'))
    temp_log = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Temp log'))
    pickup_datetime = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    delivery_datetime = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    transport_duration_hours = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=1, null=True, blank=True)

    # QR
    qr_code = models.UUIDField(default=uuid.uuid4, unique=True, verbose_name=_('QR kod'))
    trace_url = models.URLField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Javni URL'))

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Trag proizvoda')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Tragovi proizvoda')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Trace: {self.product.name} ← {self.producer_name}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.trace_url:
            self.trace_url = f"https://limit-connect.com/trace/{self.qr_code}/"
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)


class TraceEvent(models.Model):
    """Pojedinačni događaj u lancu traceability-ja."""
    EVENT_TYPES = [
        ('harvested', _('Ubrano / Proizvedeno')),
        ('stored', _('Uskladišteno')),
        ('ordered', _('Naručeno')),
        ('picked_up', _('Preuzeto od OPG-a')),
        ('in_transit', _('U transportu')),
        ('temp_check', _('Provjera temperature')),
        ('delivered', _('Dostavljeno restoranu')),
        ('received', _('Zaprimljeno')),
        ('prepared', _('Pripremljeno u kuhinji')),
        ('served', _('Servirano gostu')),
    ]

    trace = models.ForeignKey(
        ProductTrace, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='events', verbose_name=_('Trag')
    )
    event_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=EVENT_TYPES)
    timestamp = models.DateTimeField()

    location_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    latitude = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=7, null=True, blank=True)
    longitude = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=7, null=True, blank=True)
    temperature = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=1, null=True, blank=True)

    performed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Izvršio')
    )
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    photo = models.ImageField(upload_to='traceability/events/', blank=True, null=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Događaj traga')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Događaji traga')
        ordering = ['trace', 'timestamp']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_event_type_display()} @ {self.timestamp:%d.%m. %H:%M}"

from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from core.managers import CompanyManager
import uuid
import random
import string


class Property(models.Model):
    """A rental property unit (room, apartment, house)."""
    TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('room', _('Soba')),
        ('apartment', _('Apartman')),
        ('house', _('Kuca')),
        ('suite', _('Suite')),
        ('dorm', _('Dorm / Spavaonica')),
        ('office', _('Ured')),
        ('parking', _('Parking')),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('available', _('Slobodna')),
        ('occupied', _('Zauzeta')),
        ('cleaning', _('Ciscenje')),
        ('maintenance', _('Odrzavanje')),
        ('blocked', _('Blokirana')),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='rental_properties', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects = CompanyManager()

    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('Naziv'))
    type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TYPE_CHOICES, default='room', verbose_name=_('Tip'))
    floor = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kat / Zona'))
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis'))
    capacity = models.IntegerField(default=2, verbose_name=_('Kapacitet (osoba)'))
    beds = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kreveti'),
                             help_text=_('npr. 1x bracni, 2x single'))

    # Pricing
    price_per_night = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                           verbose_name=_('Cijena po noci (EUR)'))
    price_weekend = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True,
                                         verbose_name=_('Cijena vikend'))
    price_weekly = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True,
                                        verbose_name=_('Cijena tjedna'))
    price_monthly = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True,
                                         verbose_name=_('Cijena mjesecna'))
    tourist_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=1,
                                       verbose_name=_('Boravisna pristojba po osobi/noci'))
    cleaning_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                        verbose_name=_('Naknada za ciscenje'))
    min_nights = models.IntegerField(default=1, verbose_name=_('Min. noci'))

    # Smart Lock / Access
    smart_lock_id = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Smart Lock ID'))
    smart_lock_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tip brave'),
                                        help_text=_('nuki, ttlock, igloohome, yale'))
    wifi_name = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('WiFi naziv'))
    wifi_password = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('WiFi lozinka'))

    # QR / Access
    qr_code = models.CharField(max_length=32, unique=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('QR kod'))

    # Status
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='available',
                                verbose_name=_('Status'))
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivna'))
    location = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Lokacija / Adresa'))
    photo_url = models.URLField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Foto URL'))

    # Amenities
    amenities = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Pogodnosti'),
                                  help_text=_('["wifi","parking","ac","tv","kitchen"]'))

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Nekretnina')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Nekretnine')
        ordering = ['floor', 'name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.name} ({self.get_type_display()})'

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.qr_code:
            self.qr_code = uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @property
    def is_available(self):
        return self.status == 'available' and self.is_active

class Booking(models.Model):
    """Guest booking / reservation."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Na cekanju')),
        ('confirmed', _('Potvrdena')),
        ('checked_in', _('Prijavljen')),
        ('checked_out', _('Odjavljen')),
        ('cancelled', _('Otkazana')),
        ('no_show', _('No-show')),
    ]
    SOURCE_CHOICES = [
        ('direct', _('Direktno')),
        ('website', _('Web stranica')),
        ('qr', _('QR kod')),
        ('booking_com', _('Booking.com')),
        ('airbnb', _('Airbnb')),
        ('phone', _('Telefon')),
        ('walkin', _('Walk-in')),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='rental_bookings', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    objects = CompanyManager()

    # Reference
    booking_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Broj rezervacije'))
    rental_property = models.ForeignKey(Property, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='bookings',
                                  verbose_name=_('Nekretnina'))

    # Guest info
    guest_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('Ime gosta'))
    guest_email = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Email'))
    guest_phone = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Telefon'))
    guest_country = models.CharField(max_length=3, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Drzava'),
                                      help_text=_('ISO 3166 kod (HR, DE, AT...)'))
    guest_id_number = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Broj dokumenta'))
    guest_count = models.IntegerField(default=1, verbose_name=_('Broj gostiju'))

    # Dates
    check_in = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Check-in'))
    check_out = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Check-out'))
    actual_check_in = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Stvarni check-in'))
    actual_check_out = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Stvarni check-out'))

    # Pricing
    price_per_night = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Cijena/noc'))
    total_nights = models.IntegerField(default=1, verbose_name=_('Broj noci'))
    subtotal = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Podnos'))
    tourist_tax_total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0,
                                             verbose_name=_('Boravisna pristojba'))
    cleaning_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Ukupno'))

    # Access
    access_code = models.CharField(max_length=8, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Pristupni kod'))
    access_qr = models.CharField(max_length=64, unique=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('QR pristup'))
    access_valid_from = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    access_valid_until = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Status
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending',
                                verbose_name=_('Status'))
    source = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=SOURCE_CHOICES, default='direct',
                                verbose_name=_('Izvor'))
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomene'))
    is_paid = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Placeno'))

    # Payment link
    payment_id = models.UUIDField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Payment ID'))

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Rezervacija')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Rezervacije')
        ordering = ['-check_in']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.booking_number} - {self.guest_name} ({self.check_in} to {self.check_out})'

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.booking_number:
            prefix = self.company.name[:2].upper() if self.company else 'RH'
            num = Booking.objects.filter(company=self.company).count() + 1
            self.booking_number = f'{prefix}-{num:05d}'
        if not self.access_code:
            self.access_code = ''.join(random.choices(string.digits, k=6))
        if not self.access_qr:
            self.access_qr = uuid.uuid4().hex
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def calculate_price(self):
        """Calculate total price including tax and fees."""
        from datetime import timedelta
        nights = (self.check_out - self.check_in).days
        if nights < 1:
            nights = 1
        self.total_nights = nights
        self.subtotal = self.price_per_night * nights
        self.tourist_tax_total = self.rental_property.tourist_tax * self.guest_count * nights
        self.cleaning_fee = self.rental_property.cleaning_fee
        self.total_price = self.subtotal + self.tourist_tax_total + self.cleaning_fee
        return self.total_price

    @property

    def is_active_stay(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        today = timezone.now().date()
        return self.status == 'checked_in' and self.check_in <= today <= self.check_out


class HousekeepingLog(models.Model):
    """Cleaning/maintenance log for properties."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Za ciscenje')),
        ('in_progress', _('U tijeku')),
        ('done', _('Zavrseno')),
        ('inspected', _('Pregledano')),
    ]
    rental_property = models.ForeignKey(Property, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='housekeeping_logs')
    booking = models.ForeignKey(Booking, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    assigned_to = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Zaduzena osoba'),
    )
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    started_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    completed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Ciscenje')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.rental_property.name} - {self.get_status_display()}'

class InsurancePolicy(models.Model):
    TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('health', 'Zdravstveno'),
        ('life', 'Zivotno'),
        ('property', 'Imovinska'),
        ('vehicle', 'Vozila'),
        ('liability', 'Odgovornost'),
        ('other', 'Ostalo'),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('active', 'Aktivna'),
        ('expired', 'Istekla'),
        ('cancelled', 'Otkazana'),
        ('pending', 'Na cekanju'),
    ]
    policy_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True, verbose_name='Broj police')
    insurance_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TYPE_CHOICES,
                                       default='other', verbose_name='Vrsta osiguranja')
    insurer = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name='Osiguravatelj')
    partner = models.ForeignKey('partners.Partner', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                 null=True, blank=True, related_name='insurance_policies',
                                 verbose_name='Osiguranik')
    start_date = models.DateField(verbose_name='Pocetak')
    end_date = models.DateField(verbose_name='Kraj')
    premium = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name='Premija')
    coverage_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2,
                                           default=0, verbose_name='Iznos pokrica')

                               default='active', verbose_name='Status')

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'insurance_policy'
        verbose_name = 'Polica osiguranja'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Police osiguranja'
        ordering = ['-start_date']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.policy_number} - {self.insurer} ({self.insurance_type})"

                                 verbose_name=_('PDF datoteka'))

    # Meta

                                    related_name='created_invoices')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'invoicing_invoice'
        verbose_name = _('Račun')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Računi')
        ordering = ['-date_issued']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.number} \u2013 {self.client_name} ({self.total} {self.currency})"

    def clean(self):
        from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
        from konto.models import AccountingPeriod
        if self.date_issued and hasattr(self, 'company') and self.company_id:
            if AccountingPeriod.is_period_locked(self.company, self.date_issued.year, self.date_issued.month):
                raise ValidationError(f"Razdoblje {self.date_issued.month:02d}/{self.date_issued.year} je zaključano. Izdavanje računa nije moguće.")

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        """
        Zakonska zaštita:
        1. Fiskalizirani/zaključani račun se ne smije mijenjati (ZOR čl.9, Zakon o fiskalizaciji)
        2. deletion_protected_until se automatski postavlja na datum_izdavanja + 11 godina (ZOR čl.10)
        """
        from django.core.exceptions import PermissionDenied

        # ── Guard: zaključani račun ──────────────────────────────────────────
        if self.pk:
            _uf = kwargs.get('update_fields')
            # Polja koja se smiju mijenjati čak i na zaključanom računu
            _safe_fields = {
                'qr_code_base64', 'pdf_file', 'photo', 'updated_at',
                'is_locked', 'deletion_protected_until', 'archive_hash',
                'jir', 'zki', 'is_fiscalized', 'fiscal_datetime',
                'operator_oib', 'issuer_phone',
            }
            if self.is_locked and _uf is not None and not set(_uf).issubset(_safe_fields):
                raise PermissionDenied(
                    f"Račun {self.number} je zaključan (fiskaliziran/arhiviran) "
                    f"i ne može se mijenjati. Izdajte Storno račun."
                )

        # ── Auto: postavi deletion_protected_until = datum + 11 godina ──────
        if not self.deletion_protected_until and self.date_issued:
            try:
                issued = self.date_issued
                if hasattr(issued, 'date'):
                    issued = issued.date()
                self.deletion_protected_until = issued.replace(year=issued.year + 11)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # ── Auto: SHA-256 archive_hash za integritet dokumenta ──────────
        import hashlib
        hash_source = "|".join([
            str(self.number or ""),
            str(self.total or "0"),
            str(self.jir or ""),
            str(self.zki or ""),
            str(self.date_issued or ""),
            str(self.client_name or ""),
            str(self.issuer_oib or ""),
        ])
        self.archive_hash = hashlib.sha256(hash_source.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def recalculate(self):
        from decimal import Decimal
        items = self.items.all()
        self.subtotal = sum(i.total_price for i in items)
        # Calculate tax from per-item tax_rate (not flat tax_percent)
        self.tax_amount = sum(
            i.total_price * i.tax_rate / Decimal('100')
            for i in items
        )
        self.total = self.subtotal + self.tax_amount
        return self

    @property
    def is_hr(self):
        return self.country == 'HR'

    @property
    def is_de(self):
        return self.country == 'DE'

    @property
    def effective_iban(self):
        """Uvijek vrati IBAN izdavatelja bez obzira na koji je field pohranjen.
        issuer_iban je canonical; iban je legacy field za stare zapise."""
        return self.issuer_iban or self.iban or ''

    @property
    def effective_swift(self):
        """Uvijek vrati SWIFT izdavatelja (issuer_swift > swift > prazan string)."""
        return self.issuer_swift or self.swift or ''


class InvoiceItem(models.Model):
    invoice      = models.ForeignKey(Invoice, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                      related_name='items', verbose_name=_('Račun'))
    description  = models.TextField(verbose_name=_('Opis'), default='')
    quantity     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=1, verbose_name=_('Količina'))
    unit         = models.CharField(max_length=10, default='kom', verbose_name=_('Jed.mjera'))
    unit_price   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Jed.cijena'))
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Popust %'))
    total_price  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Ukupno'))
    gross_total  = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Ukupno s PDV'))
    tax_rate     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25, verbose_name=_('PDV %'))

    # Normalizirana veza na TaxRate model
    tax_rate_obj = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.TaxRate', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('PDV stopa'),
        help_text=_('Veza na poreznu stopu iz registra'),
    )

    # Veza na InventoryItem (opcionalno - za praćenje zaliha)
    inventory_item = models.ForeignKey(
        'inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='invoice_items',
        verbose_name=_('Artikl iz zalihe'),
    )

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'invoicing_invoiceitem'
        verbose_name = _('Stavka računa')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke računa')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.description} \xd7 {self.quantity} = {self.total_price}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # Sync tax_rate from tax_rate_obj if set
        if self.tax_rate_obj_id and self.tax_rate_obj:
            self.tax_rate = self.tax_rate_obj.rate
        self.total_price = round(
            Decimal(str(self.quantity)) * Decimal(str(self.unit_price)) * (1 - Decimal(str(self.discount_percent)) / Decimal('100')), 2
        )
        self.gross_total = round(self.total_price * (1 + Decimal(str(self.tax_rate)) / Decimal('100')), 2)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)




class InvoiceEvent(models.Model):
    """Audit trail — every action on an invoice is logged here for the Timeline."""
    EVENT_TYPES = [
        ('created', 'Dokument kreiran'),
        ('lookup', 'Lookup partnera'),
        ('vies_ok', 'VIES potvrda'),
        ('vies_fail', 'VIES negativan'),
        ('reverse_charge', 'Reverse Charge primijenjen'),
        ('item_added', 'Stavka dodana'),
        ('item_weighed', 'Stavka izvagana'),
        ('rollwagen_bound', 'Rollwagen vezan'),
        ('dispatched', 'Otpremljeno'),
        ('fiscalized', 'Fiskalizirano'),
        ('paid', 'Placeno'),
        ('storno_partial', 'Djelomicni storno'),
        ('storno_full', 'Potpuni storno'),
        ('sent_email', 'Poslano emailom'),
        ('sent_whatsapp', 'Poslano WhatsApp'),
        ('morphed', 'Dokument mutirao'),
        ('status_change', 'Promjena statusa'),
        ('edited', 'Izmjena podataka'),
        ('note', 'Biljeska'),
    ]
    invoice = models.ForeignKey(
        'Invoice', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='events',
    )
    event_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=EVENT_TYPES)
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    detail = models.TextField(blank=True)
    metadata = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    actor = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
    )
    # ── Audit: tko je editirao, s kojeg uređaja ─────────────────────────────
    # ZOR čl. 9 + GoBD §147 — dokazivost svake izmjene
    updated_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='invoice_events_updated',
        verbose_name='Izvršio izmjenu',
    )
    client_ip = models.GenericIPAddressField(
        null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name='IP adresa',
        help_text='IP adresa korisnika koji je izvršio akciju',
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'invoicing_invoice_event'
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Invoice Event'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.event_type}: {self.title}"

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            'id': self.pk,
            'event_type': self.event_type,
            'title': self.title,
            'detail': self.detail,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
            'actor': self.actor.get_full_name() if self.actor else '',
            'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat() if self.created_at else '',
            'time_display': self.created_at.strftime('%H:%M') if self.created_at else '',
            'date_display': self.created_at.strftime('%d.%m.%Y') if self.created_at else '',
        }


class DunningRule(models.Model):
    CHANNEL_CHOICES = [
        ('email',    'Email'),
        ('whatsapp', 'WhatsApp'),
        ('both',     'Email + WhatsApp'),
    ]
    TONE_CHOICES = [
        ('gentle', 'Blagi podsjetnik'),
        ('firm',   'Asertivan zahtjev'),
        ('legal',  'Formalna opomena'),
    ]

    company      = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                      related_name='dunning_rules')
    step         = models.PositiveIntegerField(verbose_name='Korak')
    days_overdue = models.PositiveIntegerField(verbose_name='Dana kasnjenja')
    channel      = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CHANNEL_CHOICES, default='email')
    tone         = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TONE_CHOICES, default='gentle')
    cc_admin     = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='CC company_admin')
    is_active    = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('company', 'step')
        ordering = ['company', 'step']
        verbose_name = 'Pravilo opomene'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Pravila opomena'

    def __str__(self):
        return (f"{self.company.name} | Korak {self.step}: "
                f"D+{self.days_overdue} [{self.channel}/{self.tone}]")



class DunningHistory(models.Model):
    """Audit log for every dunning reminder sent."""
    CHANNEL_CHOICES = [
        ("email", "Email"),
        ("whatsapp", "WhatsApp"),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("sent", "Poslano"),
        ("failed", "Neuspjelo"),
        ("bounced", "Odbijeno"),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey(
        "companies.Company", on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="dunning_history",
        verbose_name=_("Tvrtka"),
    )
    invoice = models.ForeignKey(
        Invoice, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="dunning_history",
        verbose_name=_("Racun"),
    )
    dunning_rule = models.ForeignKey(
        DunningRule, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name="history",
        verbose_name=_("Pravilo opomene"),
    )
    step = models.PositiveIntegerField(verbose_name=_("Korak"))
    channel_used = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CHANNEL_CHOICES, verbose_name=_("Kanal"))
    sent_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_("Poslano"))
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="sent", verbose_name=_("Status"))
    error_message = models.TextField(blank=True, default="", verbose_name=_("Greska"))
    email_subject = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="", verbose_name=_("Predmet"))
    email_body = models.TextField(blank=True, default="", verbose_name=_("Sadrzaj"))
    recipient_email = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, default="", verbose_name=_("Primatelj"))

    objects = CompanyManager()

    class Meta:
        db_table = "invoicing_dunninghistory"
        ordering = ["-sent_at"]
        verbose_name = _("Dunning povijest")
        verbose_name_plural = _("Dunning povijest")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Opomena #{self.step} za {self.invoice.number} ({self.status})"

"""PDV-S (Prijava PDV-a) — modeli."""
from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings


class PDVSReport(models.Model):
    """Generiran PDV-S obrazac za period."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', 'Nacrt'),
        ('final', 'Finalizirano'),
        ('submitted', 'Predano'),
        ('accepted', 'Prihvaceno'),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                 related_name='pdvs_reports')
    year = models.IntegerField()
    month = models.IntegerField()
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft')

    # I. PRIMLJENE ISPORUKE (input)
    i1_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='I.1 Tuzemne isporuke - osnovica')
    i1_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='I.1 Tuzemne isporuke - pretporez')
    i2_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='I.2 Stjecanje iz EU - osnovica')
    i2_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='I.2 Stjecanje iz EU - pretporez')
    i3_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='I.3 Uvoz - osnovica')
    i3_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='I.3 Uvoz - pretporez')

    # II. OBAVLJENE ISPORUKE (output)
    ii1_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.1 Oporezive 25% - osnovica')
    ii1_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.1 Oporezive 25% - PDV')
    ii2_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.2 Oporezive 13% - osnovica')
    ii2_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.2 Oporezive 13% - PDV')
    ii3_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.3 Oporezive 5% - osnovica')
    ii3_tax = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.3 Oporezive 5% - PDV')
    ii4_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.4 Oslobodene isporuke')
    ii5_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.5 Isporuke u EU')
    ii6_base = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='II.6 Izvoz (trece zemlje)')

    # III. PRETPOREZ (input VAT totals)
    iii_total_pretporez = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    iii_ispravak = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='III.4 Ispravak pretporeza')

    # IV. OBVEZA
    iv_pdv_isporuke = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='IV.1 Ukupni PDV na isporuke')
    iv_pretporez = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='IV.2 Ukupni pretporez')
    iv_razlika = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='IV.3 Razlika (+ uplata / - povrat)')

    # Meta
    generated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    generated_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
                                      null=True, blank=True)
    xml_file = models.FileField(upload_to='pdv_s/exports/', blank=True)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = [('company', 'year', 'month')]
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']
        verbose_name = 'PDV-S Obrazac'
        verbose_name_plural = 'PDV-S Obrasci'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'PDV-S {self.month:02d}/{self.year} — {self.company.name}'

=== ./assessment/models.py ===
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.db import models

from core.managers import CompanyManager


class FieldAssessment(models.Model):
    ASSESSMENT_TYPES = [
        ('bathroom', 'Kupaona'),
        ('optical', 'Optika'),
        ('mechanic', 'Auto mehaničar'),
        ('other', 'Ostalo'),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('DRAFT', 'Skica'),
        ('COMPLETED', 'Završeno'),
        ('OFFER_SENT', 'Ponuda poslana'),
        ('ACCEPTED', 'Prihvaćeno'),
        ('REJECTED', 'Odbijeno'),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='assessments'
    )
    assessment_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=ASSESSMENT_TYPES, default='bathroom',
        verbose_name='Tip procjene'
    )

    # Client info
    client_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name='Ime klijenta')
    client_phone = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name='Telefon')
    client_email = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name='E-mail')
    client_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True, verbose_name='Adresa objekta')

    # Staff
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='assessments_created', verbose_name='Majstor / inspektor'
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='DRAFT', verbose_name='Status'
    )

    # All form responses as JSON
    json_data = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name='Podaci formulara')

    # Expert notes (mirrored from step5 for easy access)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='Mišljenje stručnjaka')
    voice_note = models.FileField(
        upload_to='assessments/voice/', null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Glasovna bilješka'
    )

    # Pricing estimate
    calculated_price_from = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Cijena od (€)'
    )
    calculated_price_to = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Cijena do (€)'
    )

    # Links to other modules
    linked_offer = models.ForeignKey(
        'invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='source_assessments', verbose_name='Ponuda'
    )
    linked_project = models.ForeignKey(
        'burza.ProjectLead', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='assessments', verbose_name='Projekt (Burza)'
    )

    objects = CompanyManager()

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Terenska procjena'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Terenske procjene'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Nalaz #{self.pk} – {self.get_assessment_type_display()} – {self.client_name}"

    def get_absolute_url(self):
        from django.urls import reverse
        return reverse('assessment:detail', args=[self.pk])

    @property
    def step_data(self):
        data = dict(self.json_data or {})
        for i in range(1, 7):
            data.setdefault(f'step{i}', {})
        return data

    @property
    def completion_percent(self):
        filled = sum(1 for i in range(1, 7) if (self.json_data or {}).get(f'step{i}'))
        return int(filled / 6 * 100)

    @property
    def status_color(self):
        return {
            'DRAFT': '#94a3b8',
            'COMPLETED': '#10b981',
            'OFFER_SENT': '#3b82f6',
            'ACCEPTED': '#c9a84c',
            'REJECTED': '#ef4444',
        }.get(self.status, '#94a3b8')


class AssessmentPhoto(models.Model):
    STEP_CHOICES = [
        (1, 'Lokacija'), (2, 'Instalacije'), (3, 'Stanje/Dimenzije'),
        (4, 'Elementi'), (5, 'Zaključak'), (6, 'Stil'),
    ]
    assessment = models.ForeignKey(
        FieldAssessment, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='photos'
    )
    photo = models.ImageField(
        upload_to='assessments/photos/%Y/%m/', verbose_name='Fotografija'
    )
    caption = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name='Opis')
    step = models.IntegerField(default=3, choices=STEP_CHOICES, verbose_name='Korak')
    uploaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['step', 'uploaded_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Foto #{self.pk} – Nalaz #{self.assessment_id}"

=== ./fiscal/models.py ===
from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings


class FiscalProvider(models.TextChoices):
    HR_FINA = 'hr_fina', 'Hrvatska - FINA CIS'
    DE_TSE = 'de_tse', 'Deutschland - KassenSichV/TSE'
    NONE = 'none', 'Bez fiskalizacije'


class FiscalConfiguration(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='fiscal_configs'
    )
    branch = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.CompanyBranch', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True
    )
    provider = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=FiscalProvider.choices,
        default=FiscalProvider.NONE
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_test_mode = models.BooleanField(default=True, help_text='Koristi test okolinu')

    # HR FINA
    certificate_file = models.FileField(upload_to='fiscal/certs/', blank=True, help_text='.p12 certifikat od FINA')
    certificate_password = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    oib = models.CharField(max_length=11, blank=True, help_text='OIB obveznika')
    business_premise_id = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name='Oznaka poslovnog prostora')
    device_id = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name='Oznaka naplatnog uredaja')

    # DE TSE
    tse_api_key = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text='fiskaly API Key')
    tse_api_secret = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text='fiskaly API Secret')
    tse_tss_id = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text='TSS ID iz fiskaly dashboarda')
    tse_client_id = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Fiskalna konfiguracija'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Fiskalne konfiguracije'
        unique_together = ['company', 'branch']

    def __str__(self):
        branch_name = self.branch.name if self.branch else 'Svi'
        return f"{self.company} / {branch_name} - {self.get_provider_display()}"


class FiscalLog(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('success', 'Uspjesno'),
        ('error', 'Greska'),
        ('pending', 'Na cekanju'),
        ('retry', 'Ponovni pokusaj'),
    ]
    config = models.ForeignKey(FiscalConfiguration, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    receipt_type = models.CharField(max_length=50)
    receipt_id = models.PositiveIntegerField()
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES)
    zki = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, verbose_name='ZKI')
    jir = models.CharField(max_length=40, blank=True, verbose_name='JIR')
    tse_signature = models.TextField(blank=True)
    tse_transaction_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    tse_start_time = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    tse_end_time = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    request_xml = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text='Poslani zahtjev')
    response_xml = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text='Primljeni odgovor')
    error_message = models.TextField(blank=True)
    qr_code_data = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Fiskalni log'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Fiskalni logovi'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.receipt_type}#{self.receipt_id} - {self.status}"

=== ./registration/models.py ===
import uuid

from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class CompanyRegistration(models.Model):
    PENDING = 'pending'
    APPROVED = 'approved'
    REJECTED = 'rejected'
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (PENDING, _('Pending')),
        (APPROVED, _('Approved')),
        (REJECTED, _('Rejected')),
    ]

    BUSINESS_TYPES = [
        ('doo', 'd.o.o.'),
        ('jdoo', 'j.d.o.o.'),
        ('obrt', 'Obrt'),
        ('dd', 'd.d.'),
        ('sp', 's.p.'),
        ('eu', 'EU company'),
        ('other', 'Other'),
    ]

    PLAN_CHOICES = [
        ('micro', 'Micro – €4/mj'),
        ('starter', 'Starter – €9/mj'),
        ('professional', 'Professional – €29/mj'),
        ('enterprise', 'Enterprise – €79/mj'),
        ('distributor', 'Distributor – €149/mj'),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)

    # Company info
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    oib = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
    country = models.CharField(max_length=100, default='Croatia')
    business_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=BUSINESS_TYPES, default='doo')
    address = models.TextField()

    # Contact
    contact_name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    contact_email = models.EmailField()
    contact_phone = models.CharField(max_length=50)

    # Document
    document = models.FileField(upload_to='registrations/documents/', blank=True, null=True)

    # Plan selected during registration
    selected_plan = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PLAN_CHOICES, default='starter')

    # Status
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default=PENDING)
    rejection_reason = models.TextField(blank=True)
    reviewed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        related_name='reviewed_registrations'
    )

    # Timestamps
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Company Registration'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Company Registrations'

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} [{self.status}]"

    @property
    def is_pending(self):
        return self.status == self.PENDING

    @property
    def is_approved(self):
        return self.status == self.APPROVED

    @property
    def is_rejected(self):
        return self.status == self.REJECTED



class PricingPlan(models.Model):
    slug = models.SlugField(max_length=50, unique=True)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    name_de = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    name_en = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    tagline = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    tagline_de = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    price_monthly = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    price_yearly = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    currency = models.CharField(max_length=3, default="EUR")
    max_users = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=3)
    max_locations = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)
    max_invoices_per_month = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=50)
    max_articles = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=100)
    storage_mb = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=500)
    included_modules = models.JSONField(default=list, help_text="List of module slugs included")
    has_fiscalization = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    has_whatsapp = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    has_api_access = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    has_premium_support = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    has_custom_branding = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_popular = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    sort_order = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["sort_order"]
        verbose_name = "Pricing Plan"
        verbose_name_plural = "Pricing Plans"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.price_monthly} {self.currency}/mo)"

class CompanySubscription(models.Model):
    PLAN_CHOICES = [
        ('starter', 'Starter'),
        ('professional', 'Professional'),
        ('business', 'Business'),  # legacy
        ('enterprise', 'Enterprise'),
        ('micro', 'Micro'),
        ('distributor', 'Distributor'),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company',
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='subscriptions'
    )
    plan = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PLAN_CHOICES)
    valid_from = models.DateField()
    valid_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    monthly_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    pricing_plan = models.ForeignKey(
        PricingPlan, on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name="subscriptions",
        verbose_name="Pricing Plan",
    )
    trial_ends = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name="Trial End Date")
    billing_cycle = models.CharField(
        max_length=10,
        choices=[("monthly", "Monthly"), ("yearly", "Yearly")],
        default="monthly",
    )
    stripe_customer_id = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    stripe_subscription_id = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    modules_override = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True, help_text="Custom module list; overrides plan if set")
    promo_code = models.ForeignKey(
        'PromoCode', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Korišteni promo kod',
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Company Subscription'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Company Subscriptions'

    def __str__(self):
        status = 'active' if self.is_active else 'inactive'
        return f"{self.company.name} – {self.plan} ({status})"

    @property
    def is_trial(self):
        from datetime import date
        return self.trial_ends is not None and self.trial_ends >= date.today()

    @property
    def allowed_modules(self):
        if self.modules_override is not None:
            return set(self.modules_override)
        if self.pricing_plan_id and self.pricing_plan:
            return set(self.pricing_plan.included_modules or [])
        from registration.constants import PLAN_MODULES
        return PLAN_MODULES.get(self.plan, set())

    def has_module(self, module_name):
        return module_name in self.allowed_modules

class PromoCode(models.Model):
    """Promo / gutschein kod za popust ili besplatne module pri registraciji."""
    TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('discount_percent', 'Popust (%)'),
        ('discount_fixed', 'Popust (fiksni EUR)'),
        ('free_months', 'Besplatni mjeseci'),
        ('plan_upgrade', 'Upgrade plana'),
        ('full_access', 'Puni pristup (svi moduli)'),
    ]

    code              = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, verbose_name='Kod')
    name              = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name='Naziv/Opis')
    promo_type        = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TYPE_CHOICES, verbose_name='Tip')
    discount_percent  = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Popust %')
    discount_amount   = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name='Popust iznos (EUR)')
    free_months       = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Besplatni mjeseci')
    target_plan       = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name='Ciljani plan (za upgrade)')
    max_uses          = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Max korištenja (0=neograničeno)')
    times_used        = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Korišteno puta')
    valid_from        = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    valid_until       = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    is_active         = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    is_superadmin_code = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Superadmin kod (puni pristup)')
    created_at        = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Promo kod'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Promo kodovi'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.code} ({self.get_promo_type_display()})"

    def is_valid(self):
        if not self.is_active:
            return False
        if self.max_uses > 0 and self.times_used >= self.max_uses:
            return False
        from django.utils import timezone
        now = timezone.now()
        if self.valid_from and now < self.valid_from:
            return False
        if self.valid_until and now > self.valid_until:
            return False
        return True

    def apply_to_subscription(self, subscription):
        if not self.is_valid():
            return False, "Kod nije validan"
        if self.promo_type == 'discount_percent':
            original = subscription.monthly_fee
            from decimal import Decimal
            discount = original * Decimal(self.discount_percent) / Decimal('100')
            subscription.monthly_fee = max(Decimal('0'), original - discount)
            subscription.save(update_fields=['monthly_fee'])
        elif self.promo_type == 'discount_fixed':
            from decimal import Decimal
            subscription.monthly_fee = max(Decimal('0'), subscription.monthly_fee - self.discount_amount)
            subscription.save(update_fields=['monthly_fee'])
        elif self.promo_type == 'free_months':
            from datetime import timedelta

            from django.utils import timezone
            if subscription.trial_ends:
                subscription.trial_ends += timedelta(days=30 * self.free_months)
            else:
                subscription.trial_ends = timezone.now().date() + timedelta(days=30 * self.free_months)
            subscription.save(update_fields=['trial_ends'])
        elif self.promo_type == 'plan_upgrade' and self.target_plan:
            subscription.plan = self.target_plan
            try:
                new_plan = PricingPlan.objects.get(slug=self.target_plan)
                subscription.pricing_plan = new_plan
            except PricingPlan.DoesNotExist:
                pass
            subscription.save(update_fields=['plan', 'pricing_plan'])
        elif self.promo_type == 'full_access' or self.is_superadmin_code:
            subscription.plan = 'enterprise'
            try:
                ent_plan = PricingPlan.objects.get(slug='enterprise')
                subscription.pricing_plan = ent_plan
            except PricingPlan.DoesNotExist:
                pass
            from decimal import Decimal
            subscription.monthly_fee = Decimal('0')
            subscription.save(update_fields=['plan', 'pricing_plan', 'monthly_fee'])
        self.times_used += 1
        self.save(update_fields=['times_used'])
        return True, "Kod primijenjen"



class PropterTenant(models.Model):
    """SaaS tenant for Propter e-invoicing gateway."""
    TIER_CHOICES = [
        ('client', 'Direktni klijent (30-80 EUR/mj)'),
        ('bookkeeper', 'Knjigovodstveni servis (150-200 EUR/mj)'),
    ]
    CERT_STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('none', 'Nema certifikata'),
        ('pending', 'Zahtjev poslan'),
        ('active', 'Aktivan'),
        ('expiring', 'Istice uskoro'),
        ('expired', 'Istekao'),
    ]

    company = models.OneToOneField(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='propter_tenant'
    )
    operator_name = models.CharField(
        max_length=200, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Operator / knjigovodstveni servis',
        help_text='Npr. KS Limit d.o.o.'
    )
    tier = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TIER_CHOICES, default='client', verbose_name='Tip pretplate')
    max_companies = models.PositiveIntegerField(
        default=1, verbose_name='Max tvrtki',
        help_text='Za bookkeepere: koliko klijenata mogu voditi'
    )
    fina_cert_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=15, choices=CERT_STATUS_CHOICES,
        default='none', verbose_name='Status FINA certifikata'
    )
    modules_enabled = models.JSONField(default=list, verbose_name='Omoguceni moduli')
    monthly_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name='Mjesecna naknada (EUR)')
    billing_day = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1, verbose_name='Dan fakturiranja')
    next_billing = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Sljedece fakturiranje')
    eracuni_sent_total = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Ukupno poslanih e-racuna')
    eracuni_sent_month = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='e-Racuna ovaj mjesec')
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Aktivan')
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='Napomene')

    # Self-referential: bookkeeper manages client tenants
    managed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'self', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='managed_tenants',
        verbose_name='Upravljano od',
        help_text='Knjigovodstveni servis koji upravlja ovom tvrtkom'
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Propter korisnik'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Propter korisnici'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.company.name} ({self.get_tier_display()})'


# AUTO-SYNC: registration.PropterTenant --> propter.PropterConfig
from django.db.models.signals import post_save
from django.dispatch import receiver

@receiver(post_save, sender=PropterTenant, dispatch_uid='propter_config_sync')
def sync_propter_config(sender, instance, created, **kwargs):
    try:
        from propter.models import PropterConfig
        tier_map = {
            'client': 'client',
            'bookkeeper': 'bookkeeper',
            'operator': 'operator',
        }
        cert_map = {
            'none': 'none',
            'pending': 'pending',
            'active': 'active',
            'expired': 'expired',
        }
        PropterConfig.objects.update_or_create(
            company=instance.company,
            defaults={
                'tier': tier_map.get(instance.tier, 'client'),
                'cert_status': cert_map.get(instance.fina_cert_status, 'none'),
                'monthly_fee': instance.monthly_fee,
                'max_clients': instance.max_companies,
                'is_active': True,
            }
        )
    except Exception as e:
        import logging
        logging.getLogger('propter').warning('PropterConfig sync greska: %s', e)


class UmbrellaInviteCode(models.Model):
    """Invite code generated by umbrella company for child registration."""
    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    umbrella_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='invite_codes',
        verbose_name='Umbrella tvrtka',
    )
    code = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, verbose_name='Pozivni kod')
    plan_slug = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='starter', verbose_name='Plan za novu firmu')
    relation_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, default='client',
        choices=[
            ('subsidiary', 'Podružnica'),
            ('client', 'Klijent (knjigovodstvo)'),
            ('franchise', 'Franšiza'),
        ],
        verbose_name='Tip veze',
    )
    max_uses = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1, verbose_name='Max korištenja')
    times_used = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Korišteno puta')
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Kreirao',
    )
    modules_override = models.JSONField(
        null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Custom moduli (override plana)',
    )
    free_months = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1, verbose_name='Besplatnih mjeseci')
    note = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name='Napomena')
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    valid_until = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Umbrella pozivni kod'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Umbrella pozivni kodovi'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.code} ({self.umbrella_company.name})'

    def is_valid(self):
        if not self.is_active:
            return False
        if self.max_uses > 0 and self.times_used >= self.max_uses:
            return False
        if self.valid_until:
            from django.utils import timezone
            if timezone.now() > self.valid_until:
                return False
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def generate_code():
        import random, string
        return 'UC-' + ''.join(random.choices(string.ascii_uppercase + string.digits, k=8))
