"""e-Račun models — multi-tenant e-invoice gateway."""
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from core.managers import CompanyManager
import uuid


class ERacunConfig(models.Model):
    """Per-company e-Račun configuration."""
    company = models.OneToOneField(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='eracun_config'
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='e-Račun aktivan')

    # FINA certifikat
    cert_p12 = models.FileField(upload_to='eracun/certs/', blank=True, verbose_name='FINA P12 certifikat')
    cert_password = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name='Lozinka certifikata')
    cert_oib = models.CharField(max_length=11, blank=True, verbose_name='OIB iz certifikata')
    cert_valid_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Certifikat vrijedi do')

    # Poslovni prostor
    oznaka_prostora = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='P1', verbose_name='Oznaka posl. prostora')
    oznaka_uredjaja = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='1', verbose_name='Oznaka naplatnog uredjaja')

    # Postavke
    use_test_env = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Testno okruzenje (CIS test)')
    auto_send = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Automatski salji pri kreiranju')
    archive_days = models.IntegerField(default=4015, verbose_name='Cuvaj arhivu (dana)', help_text='Zakonski min 11 godina = 4015 dana')

    # API pristup za vanjske klijente
    api_key = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, unique=True, verbose_name='API kljuc')
    api_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='API pristup aktivan')
    api_rate_limit = models.IntegerField(default=100, verbose_name='Max zahtjeva/sat')

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'e-Racun konfiguracija'
        verbose_name_plural = 'e-Racun konfiguracije'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'e-Racun: {self.company.name}'

    def save(self, **kwargs):
        if not self.api_key:
            import secrets
            self.api_key = secrets.token_urlsafe(48)
        super().save(**kwargs)


class ERacun(models.Model):
    """Individual e-invoice record with full audit trail."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', 'Nacrt'),
        ('validating', 'Validacija'),
        ('valid', 'Validan'),
        ('invalid', 'Nevalidan'),
        ('signing', 'Potpisivanje'),
        ('sending', 'Slanje'),
        ('sent', 'Poslan'),
        ('accepted', 'Prihvacen'),
        ('rejected', 'Odbijen'),
        ('error', 'Greska'),
        ('archived', 'Arhiviran'),
    ]

    DOC_TYPES = [
        ('380', 'Racun (Commercial Invoice)'),
        ('381', 'Odobrenje (Credit Note)'),
        ('383', 'Terecenje (Debit Note)'),
        ('386', 'Predracun (Prepayment Invoice)'),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='eracuni')

    # Veza s LIMIT Connect fakturom (opcionalno - moze doci i preko API-ja)
    invoice = models.OneToOneField(
        'invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='eracun'
    )

    # Dokument
    doc_type = models.CharField(max_length=3, choices=DOC_TYPES, default='380')
    invoice_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name='Broj racuna')
    issue_date = models.DateField(verbose_name='Datum izdavanja')
    delivery_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Datum isporuke')
    due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Rok placanja')
    currency = models.CharField(max_length=3, default='EUR')

    # Izdavatelj
    issuer_name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    issuer_oib = models.CharField(max_length=11)
    issuer_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True)
    issuer_city = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    issuer_postal = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    issuer_country = models.CharField(max_length=2, default='HR')
    issuer_iban = models.CharField(max_length=34, blank=True)
    issuer_swift = models.CharField(max_length=11, blank=True)

    # Primatelj
    recipient_name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    recipient_oib = models.CharField(max_length=20)  # moze biti i VAT ID za EU
    recipient_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True)
    recipient_city = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    recipient_postal = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    recipient_country = models.CharField(max_length=2, default='HR')

    # Iznosi
    subtotal = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    tax_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    # Placanje
    payment_means_code = models.CharField(max_length=3, default='30', help_text='30=transfer, 48=card, 10=cash')
    payment_reference = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name='Poziv na broj')
    payment_model = models.CharField(max_length=4, blank=True, default='HR00', verbose_name='Model placanja')
    note = models.TextField(blank=True)

    # Fiskalizacija
    zki = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, verbose_name='ZKI')
    jir = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, verbose_name='JIR')

    # Status i rezultat
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft')
    error_message = models.TextField(blank=True)
    error_code = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    fina_uuid = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, verbose_name='FINA UUID')

    # Anomaly detection
    anomaly_score = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True)
    risk_level = models.CharField(max_length=10, blank=True)  # low/medium/high

    # XML arhiva
    xml_request = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='XML zahtjev')
    xml_response = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name='XML odgovor')

    # Timestamps
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    sent_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    accepted_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    archived_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True
    )

    objects = CompanyManager()

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'e-Racun'
        verbose_name_plural = 'e-Racuni'
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['company', 'status']),
            models.Index(fields=['invoice_number']),
            models.Index(fields=['issuer_oib']),
            models.Index(fields=['recipient_oib']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.invoice_number} -> {self.recipient_name} ({self.get_status_display()})'


class ERacunItem(models.Model):
    """Line item on an e-Racun - EN 16931 compliant."""
    UNIT_CODES = [
        ('EA', 'Komad (EA)'),
        ('HUR', 'Sat (HUR)'),
        ('KGM', 'Kilogram (KGM)'),
        ('MTR', 'Metar (MTR)'),
        ('LTR', 'Litra (LTR)'),
        ('MTK', 'Kvadratni metar (MTK)'),
        ('DAY', 'Dan (DAY)'),
        ('MON', 'Mjesec (MON)'),
        ('SET', 'Set (SET)'),
        ('XPK', 'Pakiranje (XPK)'),
    ]

    TAX_CATEGORY_CODES = [
        ('S', 'Standardna stopa'),
        ('Z', 'Nulta stopa'),
        ('E', 'Oslobodjeno'),
        ('AE', 'Obrnuto oporezivanje (Reverse charge)'),
        ('K', 'Intra-community'),
        ('G', 'Izvoz (neoporezivo)'),
        ('O', 'Izvan PDV sustava'),
    ]

    eracun = models.ForeignKey(ERacun, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='items')
    line_number = models.IntegerField(default=1)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=300)
    quantity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=3, default=1)
    unit_code = models.CharField(max_length=5, choices=UNIT_CODES, default='EA')
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=4)
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    line_total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)
    tax_category = models.CharField(max_length=3, choices=TAX_CATEGORY_CODES, default='S')
    tax_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25)

    # NKD / klasifikacija
    classification_code = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name='KPD/NKD sifra')

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['line_number']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'#{self.line_number}: {self.description}'


class ERacunTaxSummary(models.Model):
    """Tax breakdown per category/rate - EN 16931 BG-23."""
    eracun = models.ForeignKey(ERacun, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='tax_summaries')
    tax_category = models.CharField(max_length=3)  # S, Z, E, AE, K
    tax_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2)
    taxable_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)
    tax_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ['eracun', 'tax_category', 'tax_rate']


class ERacunLog(models.Model):
    """Audit trail for every e-Racun action."""
    eracun = models.ForeignKey(ERacun, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='logs')
    action = models.CharField(max_length=30)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15)
    message = models.TextField(blank=True)
    details = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    user = models.ForeignKey('auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.action}: {self.message[:50]}'

=== ./outreach/models.py ===
from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class Contact(models.Model):
    class ContactType(models.TextChoices):
        HAUSVERWALTUNG = "hausverwaltung", _("Hausverwaltung")
        MAKLER         = "makler",         _("Makler")
        ARCHITEKT      = "architekt",      _("Architekt")
        PRIVATPERSON   = "privatperson",   _("Privatperson")
        FIRMA          = "firma",          _("Firma")

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        NEU          = "neu",          _("Neu")
        KONTAKTIERT  = "kontaktiert",  _("Kontaktiert")
        GEANTWORTET  = "geantwortet",  _("Geantwortet")
        INTERESSIERT = "interessiert", _("Interessiert")
        KLIENT       = "klient",       _("Klient")
        ABGELEHNT    = "abgelehnt",    _("Abgelehnt")

    class Source(models.TextChoices):
        MANUAL      = "manual",      _("Manuell")
        CSV         = "csv",         _("CSV Import")
        GOOGLE_MAPS = "google_maps", _("Google Maps")

    company      = models.ForeignKey(
        "companies.Company", on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name="outreach_contacts",
        verbose_name=_("Tvrtka"))
    name         = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True,  verbose_name=_("Name"))
    firm_name    = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True,  verbose_name=_("Firmenname"))
    email        = models.EmailField(verbose_name=_("E-Mail"))
    phone        = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True,   verbose_name=_("Telefon"))
    address      = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True,  verbose_name=_("Adresse"))
    city         = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True,  verbose_name=_("Stadt"))
    plz          = models.CharField(max_length=10, blank=True,   verbose_name=_("PLZ"))
    contact_type = models.CharField(max_length=20,
                                     choices=ContactType.choices,
                                     default=ContactType.FIRMA,
                                     verbose_name=_("Typ"))
    status       = models.CharField(max_length=20,
                                     choices=Status.choices,
                                     default=Status.NEU,
                                     verbose_name=_("Status"))
    source       = models.CharField(max_length=20,
                                     choices=Source.choices,
                                     default=Source.MANUAL,
                                     verbose_name=_("Quelle"))
    notes        = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_("Notizen"))
    created_at   = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at   = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        verbose_name = _("Kontakt")
        verbose_name_plural = _("Kontakte")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.firm_name or self.name} <{self.email}>"

    @property
    def display_name(self):
        return self.firm_name or self.name or self.email


class Campaign(models.Model):
    name             = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_("Kampagnenname"))
    subject_template = models.CharField(max_length=300,
                                         verbose_name=_("Betreff-Vorlage"),
                                         help_text=_("Vars: {ime_firme}, {grad}, {tip_posla}"))
    body_template    = models.TextField(verbose_name=_("Inhalt-Vorlage"))
    send_per_day     = models.IntegerField(default=50, verbose_name=_("Senden pro Tag"))
    follow_up_days   = models.IntegerField(default=3,  verbose_name=_("Follow-up Tage"))
    active           = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_("Aktiv"))
    stat_sent        = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    stat_opened      = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    stat_replied     = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    stat_interested  = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    created_at       = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at       = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        verbose_name = _("Kampagne")
        verbose_name_plural = _("Kampagnen")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    def refresh_stats(self):
        cc = self.contacts.all()
        self.stat_sent    = cc.filter(status__in=["sent", "follow_up", "replied"]).count()
        self.stat_replied = cc.filter(status="replied").count()
        self.stat_interested = Contact.objects.filter(
            campaign_contacts__campaign=self, status="interessiert").count()
        self.save(update_fields=["stat_sent", "stat_replied", "stat_interested"])


class CampaignContact(models.Model):
    class CCStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING   = "pending",   _("Ausstehend")
        SENT      = "sent",      _("Gesendet")
        FOLLOW_UP = "follow_up", _("Follow-up")
        REPLIED   = "replied",   _("Geantwortet")

    campaign   = models.ForeignKey(Campaign, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                    related_name="contacts", verbose_name=_("Kampagne"))
    contact    = models.ForeignKey(Contact, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                    related_name="campaign_contacts", verbose_name=_("Kontakt"))
    status     = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CCStatus.choices,
                                   default=CCStatus.PENDING, verbose_name=_("Status"))
    sent_at    = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    replied_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    reply_text = models.TextField(blank=True)
    personalized_subject = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True)
    personalized_body    = models.TextField(blank=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = [("campaign", "contact")]
        verbose_name = _("Kampagnen-Kontakt")
        verbose_name_plural = _("Kampagnen-Kontakte")

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.campaign} -> {self.contact}"

=== ./companies/models.py ===
import uuid

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.utils.text import slugify
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class NKDCode(models.Model):
    """
    NKD 2007 (Nacionalna klasifikacija djelatnosti) — HR verzija NACE Rev.2
    Hijerarhija: Odjeljak (A) → Područje (47) → Razred (47.1) → Podrazred (47.11)
    """

    class Level(models.TextChoices):
        SECTION  = 'section',  'Odjeljak (A–U)'
        DIVISION = 'division', 'Područje (XX)'
        GROUP    = 'group',    'Razred (XX.X)'
        CLASS    = 'class',    'Podrazred (XX.XX)'

    code     = models.CharField(max_length=10, unique=True, db_index=True, verbose_name='Šifra NKD',
                                 help_text='Npr. A, 47, 47.1, 47.11')
    level    = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Level.choices, verbose_name='Razina')
    section  = models.CharField(max_length=2, db_index=True, verbose_name='Odjeljak (A–U)')
    name_hr  = models.CharField(max_length=500, verbose_name='Naziv (HR)')
    name_en  = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True, verbose_name='Naziv (EN)')
    parent   = models.ForeignKey('self', null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                  related_name='children', verbose_name='Nadređena kategorija')
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Aktivna')
    allow_negative_stock = models.BooleanField(
        default=True,
        verbose_name='Dozvoli negativne zalihe',
        help_text='Ako je uklju\u010deno, prodaja je mogu\u0107a i kad je stanje 0.',
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = 'NKD šifra'
        verbose_name_plural = 'NKD šifarnik'
        ordering            = ['code']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.code} \u2013 {self.name_hr}'


class Company(models.Model):

    class Country(models.TextChoices):
        HR    = 'HR',    _('Hrvatska')
        DE    = 'DE',    _('Njemačka')
        AT    = 'AT',    _('Austrija')
        SI    = 'SI',    _('Slovenija')
        BA    = 'BA',    _('Bosna i Hercegovina')
        RS    = 'RS',    _('Srbija')
        OTHER = 'OTHER', _('Ostalo')

    class BusinessType(models.TextChoices):
        DOO              = 'doo',              _('d.o.o. (Društvo s ograničenom odgovornošću)')
        JDOO             = 'jdoo',             _('j.d.o.o. (Jednostavno d.o.o.)')
        DD               = 'dd',               _('d.d. (Dioničko društvo)')
        OBRT             = 'obrt',             _('Obrt')
        GMBH             = 'gmbh',             _('GmbH')
        AG               = 'ag',               _('AG (Aktiengesellschaft)')
        EINZELUNTERNEHMEN = 'einzelunternehmen', _('Einzelunternehmen')
        OTHER            = 'other',            _('Ostalo')

    class Currency(models.TextChoices):
        EUR = 'EUR', 'EUR (Euro)'
        USD = 'USD', 'USD (US Dollar)'
        GBP = 'GBP', 'GBP (British Pound)'
        CHF = 'CHF', 'CHF (Swiss Franc)'
        BAM = 'BAM', 'BAM (Bosanska marka)'
        RSD = 'RSD', 'RSD (Srpski dinar)'

    class FiscalSystem(models.TextChoices):
        HR_FISCALIZATION = 'hr_fiscal', _('HR Fiskalizacija (CIS)')
        DE_GOBD          = 'de_gobd',   _('DE GoBD')
        AT_RKSV          = 'at_rksv',   _('AT RKSV')
        NONE             = 'none',      _('Bez fiskalizacije')

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    name     = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv tvrtke'))
    oib      = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, verbose_name='OIB / Steuernummer')
    address  = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Adresa'))
    city     = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Grad'))
    zip_code = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Poštanski broj'))
    iban     = models.CharField(max_length=34, blank=True, verbose_name='IBAN')
    swift    = models.CharField(max_length=11, blank=True, default='', verbose_name='BIC/SWIFT')

    # International fields
    country       = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Country.choices, default=Country.HR, verbose_name=_('Država'))
    business_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=BusinessType.choices, default=BusinessType.DOO, verbose_name=_('Tip poslovnog subjekta'))
    tax_number    = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Porezni broj'), help_text=_('Porezni identifikacijski broj (za strana poduzeća)'))
    vat_id        = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('PDV ID / VAT ID'), help_text=_('EU PDV identifikacijski broj (npr. HR12345678901)'))
    currency      = models.CharField(max_length=3, choices=Currency.choices, default=Currency.EUR, verbose_name=_('Valuta'))
    fiscal_system = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=FiscalSystem.choices, default=FiscalSystem.HR_FISCALIZATION, verbose_name=_('Fiskalni sustav'))

    # NKD djelatnost
    nkd_code = models.ForeignKey(
        NKDCode, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('NKD djelatnost'),
        help_text=_('Nacionalna klasifikacija djelatnosti (npr. 56.10 – Restorani)'),
    )

    # Konfiguracija
    is_vat_registered = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('U sustavu PDV-a'))
    logo = models.ImageField(upload_to='company_logos/', blank=True, null=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Telefon'))
    email = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('E-mail'))
    website_url = models.URLField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Web stranica'))
    # Grosmarkt partner fields
    GROSMARKT_ROLE_CHOICES = [
        ('logistik', 'Logistik-Hub'),
        ('gaertnerei', 'Gärtnerei / Frischware'),
        ('haendler', 'Händler / Großhandel'),
        ('gastro', 'Gastronomie'),
        ('sonstiges', 'Sonstiges'),
    ]
    grosmarkt_partner = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Grosmarkt Partner', help_text='Prikazati na grosmarkt-online.eu?')
    grosmarkt_role = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, choices=GROSMARKT_ROLE_CHOICES, verbose_name='Grosmarkt Rolle')
    grosmarkt_description = models.TextField(blank=True, max_length=300, verbose_name='Grosmarkt Beschreibung (DE)', help_text='Kratki opis za grosmarkt-online.eu kartice')
    grosmarkt_show_name = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Prikaži naziv firme javno', help_text='Ako False, prikazuje se samo rola (anonimizirano)')

    mobile = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Mobitel'))
    booking_slug  = models.SlugField(max_length=200, unique=True, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Booking Slug'))
    booking_theme = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=[('dark','Tamna'), ('light','Svijetla'), ('standard','Standard (Navy)')],
        default='dark',
        verbose_name=_('Booking tema'),
        help_text=_('Zadana tema za javnu booking stranicu ove kompanije'),
    )
    settings = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name=_("Postavke tvrtke"))

    # PDV / USt status
    PDV_STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('none',             'Nije u sustavu PDV-a'),
        ('obveznik',         'Obveznik PDV-a'),
        ('pausalic',         'Paušalac (HR §90)'),
        ('kleinunternehmer', 'Kleinunternehmer (DE §19 UStG)'),
    ]
    pdv_status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=PDV_STATUS_CHOICES, default='none',
        verbose_name='PDV status',
    )
    is_kleinunternehmer = models.BooleanField(
        default=False,
        verbose_name='Kleinunternehmer §19 UStG',
        help_text='DE: oslobođen PDV-a prema §19 UStG ako god.prihodi < 22.000 EUR',
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    # Geofence / GPS
    latitude = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='GPS Latitude')
    longitude = models.FloatField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='GPS Longitude')
    geofence_radius = models.PositiveIntegerField(
        default=200, verbose_name='Geofence radius (m)',
        help_text='Maksimalna udaljenost za clock-in (metara)')
    no_show_tolerance_minutes = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=15, verbose_name='Tolerancija kasnjenja (min)')

    is_platform_owner = models.BooleanField(
        default=False,
        verbose_name='Platform Owner',
        help_text='Firma vlasnika platforme – svi moduli otključani bez pretplate',
    )

    # Parent-Child (B2B2B Umbrella)
    umbrella_parent = models.ForeignKey(
        'self', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='umbrella_children',
        verbose_name='Nadredjena tvrtka (Umbrella)',
    )
    UMBRELLA_RELATION_CHOICES = [
        ('subsidiary', 'Podru\u017enica / Sestrinska firma'),
        ('client', 'Klijent (knjigovodstvo)'),
        ('franchise', 'Fran\u0161iza'),
    ]
    is_umbrella = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name='Umbrella tvrtka')
    umbrella_relation = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=UMBRELLA_RELATION_CHOICES,
        default='client', blank=True,
        verbose_name='Tip veze s umbrella',
    )
    umbrella_max_children = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name='Max podtvrtki')
    umbrella_monthly_fee = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name='Umbrella mj. naknada (EUR)',
    )

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.booking_slug and self.name:
            base_slug = slugify(self.name)
            slug = base_slug
            n = 1
            while Company.objects.filter(booking_slug=slug).exclude(pk=self.pk).exists():
                slug = f"{base_slug}-{n}"
                n += 1
            self.booking_slug = slug
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Tvrtka')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Tvrtke')

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.name} ({self.oib})'


class TaxRate(models.Model):

    class Country(models.TextChoices):
        HR = 'HR', 'Hrvatska'
        DE = 'DE', 'Njemačka'
        AT = 'AT', 'Austrija'
        SI = 'SI', 'Slovenija'

    country     = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Country.choices, verbose_name=_('Država'))
    name        = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('Naziv stope'))
    rate        = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Stopa (%)'))
    description = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis'))
    is_default  = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Zadana stopa'))
    is_active   = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivna'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Porezna stopa')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Porezne stope')
        ordering            = ['country', '-rate']
        unique_together     = [('country', 'rate')]

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.country} \u2013 {self.name} ({self.rate}%)'


class BusinessProfile(models.Model):
    """
    Industrijski profil tvrtke — kontrolira module, cjenik i workflow.

    Primjeri: frizer, mehaničar, građevina, optika, restoran, webshop...
    Vezan 1:1 na Company.
    """
    class ProfileType(models.TextChoices):
        FRIZER        = 'frizer',     _('Frizerski salon')
        MEHANIČAR     = 'mehaničar',  _('Auto mehaničar')
        GRAĐEVINA     = 'gradjevina', _('Građevina / Renovacija')
        OPTIKA        = 'optika',     _('Optika')
        RESTORAN      = 'restoran',   _('Restoran / Caffe bar')
        WEBSHOP       = 'webshop',    _('Online trgovina')
        CONSULTING    = 'consulting', _('Konzalting / IT usluge')
        CUSTOM        = 'custom',     _('Prilagođeno')

    company = models.OneToOneField(
        'Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='business_profile',
        verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    profile_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=ProfileType.choices,
        default=ProfileType.CUSTOM,
        verbose_name=_('Tip profila'),
    )

    # ── Vidljivost modula (nav) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    module_invoicing    = models.BooleanField(default=True,  verbose_name=_('Fakturiranje'))
    module_pos          = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('POS kasa'))
    module_inventory    = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Zalihe'))
    module_appointments = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Termini'))
    module_projects     = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Projekti'))
    module_worktime     = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Radni sati'))
    module_finance      = models.BooleanField(default=True,  verbose_name=_('Financije'))
    module_partners     = models.BooleanField(default=True,  verbose_name=_('Partneri'))
    module_documents    = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Dokumenti'))
    module_webshop      = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Webshop'))
    module_rental       = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Najam'))
    module_fitness      = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Fitness'))

    # ── Workflow tip ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    class WorkflowType(models.TextChoices):
        INVOICE_ONLY  = 'invoice',     _('Samo fakturiranje')
        APPOINTMENT   = 'appointment', _('Termini + fakturiranje')
        WORK_ORDER    = 'work_order',  _('Radni nalozi')
        PROJECT       = 'project',     _('Projektni rad')
        POS           = 'pos',         _('POS kasa (maloprodaja)')

    workflow_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=WorkflowType.choices,
        default=WorkflowType.INVOICE_ONLY,
        verbose_name=_('Tip workflowa'),
    )

    # ── Prilagođeni nazivi dokumenata ─────────────────────────────────────────
    offer_label    = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='Ponuda',    verbose_name=_('Naziv ponude'))
    invoice_label  = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='Račun',     verbose_name=_('Naziv računa'))
    workorder_label = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='Radni nalog', verbose_name=_('Naziv radnog naloga'))

    # ── Prilagođena polja na dokumentima (JSON) ───────────────────────────────
    # Format: [{"name": "gradiliste", "label": "Gradilište", "type": "text", "required": false}]
    custom_invoice_fields = models.JSONField(
        default=list, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Prilagođena polja na fakturi'),
        help_text=_('JSON lista polja koja se dodaju na formu računa/ponude'),
    )

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Poslovni profil')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Poslovni profili')

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.company.name} — {self.get_profile_type_display()}'

    @property
    def enabled_modules(self):
        """Vraća listu stringova s nazivima aktivnih modula."""
        return [
            mod for mod in [
                'invoicing', 'pos', 'inventory', 'appointments',
                'projects', 'worktime', 'finance', 'partners',
                'documents', 'webshop', 'rental', 'fitness',
            ]
            if getattr(self, f'module_{mod}', False)
        ]


class ServiceTemplate(models.Model):
    """
    Preddefinirani cjenik / usluge za industrijsku vertikalu.
    Može se kopirati kao startni cjenik za novu tvrtku.
    """
    profile_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=BusinessProfile.ProfileType.choices,
        verbose_name=_('Tip profila'),
        db_index=True,
    )
    name         = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv usluge'))
    description  = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis'))
    unit         = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='kom', verbose_name=_('Jed. mjera'))
    default_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Zadana cijena (€)'))
    tax_rate     = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25, verbose_name=_('PDV %'))
    category     = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kategorija'))
    sort_order   = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0)
    is_active    = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Predložak usluge')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Predlošci usluga (cjenik)')
        ordering            = ['profile_type', 'sort_order', 'name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'[{self.profile_type}] {self.name} — {self.default_price} €'


# ── PODRUŽNICE / BRANCH SUSTAV ────────────────────────────────────────────────

class CompanyBranch(models.Model):
    """Pod-firma / poslovnica unutar matične tvrtke."""

    BRANCH_TYPES = [
        ('optika',      'Optika'),
        ('fitness',     'Fitness centar'),
        ('retail',      'Trgovina'),
        ('accounting',  'Knjigovodstvo'),
        ('restaurant',  'Restoran'),
        ('salon',       'Salon ljepote'),
        ('other',       'Ostalo'),
    ]

    parent_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='branches', verbose_name='Matična tvrtka',
    )
    name        = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name='Naziv podružnice')
    branch_code = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name='Šifra')
    branch_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=BRANCH_TYPES, default='other',
        verbose_name='Tip poslovnice',
    )
    address     = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True, verbose_name='Adresa')
    city        = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name='Grad')
    phone       = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name='Telefon')
    email       = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name='E-mail')

    # Module override — empty list = inherit parent company subscription
    active_modules = models.JSONField(
        default=list, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Aktivni moduli (override)',
        help_text='Prazno = nasljeđuje plan matične tvrtke',
    )

    is_active  = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name='Aktivna')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = 'Podružnica'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Podružnice'
        ordering            = ['name']
        unique_together     = [('parent_company', 'branch_code')]

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.name} ({self.get_branch_type_display()})'

    @property
    def allowed_modules(self):
        """Return set of allowed module names. Checks override first, then parent plan."""
        if self.active_modules:
            return set(self.active_modules)
        try:
            sub = self.parent_company.subscriptions.filter(status='active').first()
            if sub:
                return sub.allowed_modules
        except Exception:
            pass
        return set()

    @property
    def dashboard_url(self):
        mapping = {
            'optika':     '/branches/optika/',
            'fitness':    '/branches/fitness/',
            'retail':     '/branches/retail/',
            'accounting': '/branches/accounting/',
            'restaurant': '/branches/restaurant/',
            'salon':      '/branches/salon/',
        }
        return mapping.get(self.branch_type, '/branches/other/')

    @property
    def badge_color(self):
        mapping = {
            'optika':     '#60a5fa',
            'fitness':    '#4ade80',
            'retail':     '#f97316',
            'accounting': '#a78bfa',
            'restaurant': '#fb923c',
            'salon':      '#f472b6',
        }
        return mapping.get(self.branch_type, '#888')



class OnboardingProgress(models.Model):
    company              = models.OneToOneField(Company, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='onboarding')
    profile_completed    = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    first_partner_added  = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    first_invoice_created = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    bank_account_added   = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    first_item_added     = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    first_employee_added = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    pos_configured       = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    ai_intro_completed   = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    ai_intro_shown       = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    dismissed            = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    created_at           = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at           = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = 'Onboarding napredak'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Onboarding napredak'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Onboarding — {self.company}'

    @property
    def completed_steps(self):
        return sum([
            self.profile_completed,
            self.first_partner_added,
            self.first_invoice_created,
            self.bank_account_added,
            self.first_item_added,
            self.first_employee_added,
            self.ai_intro_completed,
        ])

    @property
    def total_steps(self):
        return 7

    @property
    def percent(self):
        return int(self.completed_steps / self.total_steps * 100)



class CompanyPartnership(models.Model):
    """
    Veza između partnera (resellera) i firme kojom upravlja.
    Partner plaća platformi, firma plaća partneru (ili je uključena u paket).
    """
    BILLING_TYPES = [
        ('included',    'Uključeno u paket'),
        ('discounted',  'Povlaštena cijena'),
        ('full',        'Puna cijena'),
        ('free',        'Besplatno'),
    ]

    ACCESS_LEVELS = [
        ('read',  'Samo čitanje'),
        ('full',  'Puni pristup'),
        ('admin', 'Admin pristup'),
    ]

    id               = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    partner_company  = models.ForeignKey(
        'Company', on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        related_name='managed_companies',
        verbose_name='Partner (reseller)'
    )
    managed_company  = models.ForeignKey(
        'Company', on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        related_name='managing_partners',
        verbose_name='Upravljana firma'
    )
    billing_type     = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=BILLING_TYPES, default='included')
    monthly_fee      = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    access_level     = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=ACCESS_LEVELS, default='full')
    valid_from       = models.DateField(auto_now_add=True)
    valid_until      = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)  # null = aktivan
    notes            = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at       = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    created_by       = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='created_partnerships'
    )

    class Meta:
        unique_together = [('partner_company', 'managed_company')]
        verbose_name = 'Partnerstvo'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Partnerstva'
        ordering = ['managed_company__name']

    def __str__(self):
        status = 'aktivan' if not self.valid_until else f'završen {self.valid_until}'
        return f"{self.partner_company.name} → {self.managed_company.name} ({status})"

    @property
    def is_active(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        return self.valid_until is None or self.valid_until >= timezone.now().date()


class CompanyModuleConfig(models.Model):
    """Per-company module visibility configuration."""
    company = models.OneToOneField('Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
                                    related_name='module_config')
    enabled_modules = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Aktivni moduli')
    sidebar_order = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True,
        verbose_name='Redoslijed u sidebaru')
    ai_recommended = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True,
        verbose_name='AI preporuceni moduli')
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Konfiguracija modula'

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.company.name}: {len(self.enabled_modules)} modula'

    def is_enabled(self, module_id):
        return module_id in self.enabled_modules

    def get_sidebar_modules(self):
        from companies.module_registry import MODULES, get_module
        result = []
        order = self.sidebar_order or self.enabled_modules
        for mid in order:
            if mid in self.enabled_modules:
                mod = get_module(mid)
                if mod:
                    result.append(mod)
        return result

from django.db import models
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _

from projects.models import Project


class Normative(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField(max_length=255, verbose_name=_('Naziv normativa'))
    unit = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='m2', verbose_name=_('Jedinica mjere'))
    base_material_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Cijena materijala'))
    estimated_hours_per_unit = models.FloatField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Sati rada po jedinici'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Normativ')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Normativi')

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

class Calculation(models.Model):
    project = models.OneToOneField(Project, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='calculation')
    margin_percentage = models.FloatField(default=20.0, verbose_name=_('Marža (%)'))
    vat_percentage = models.FloatField(default=25.0, verbose_name=_('PDV (%)'))
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    def calculate_totals(self):
        items = self.items.all()
        total_mat = sum(float(item.unit_price_material) * item.quantity for item in items)
        total_lab = sum(item.labor_hours_per_unit * float(item.labor_rate_applied) * item.quantity for item in items)
        net_total = total_mat + total_lab
        gross_total = net_total * (1 + (self.margin_percentage / 100))
        return {
            'material': round(total_mat, 2),
            'labor': round(total_lab, 2),
            'net_cost': round(net_total, 2),
            'offer_total_net': round(gross_total, 2),
            'offer_total_vat': round(gross_total * (1 + (self.vat_percentage / 100)), 2)
        }

class CalculationItem(models.Model):
    calculation = models.ForeignKey(Calculation, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='items')
    normative = models.ForeignKey(Normative, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    quantity = models.FloatField(default=1.0)
    unit_price_material = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    labor_hours_per_unit = models.FloatField(default=0)
    labor_rate_applied = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)


# ─── BOM / Bill of Materials ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

class BOM(models.Model):
    """Sastavnica materijala (normativ za InventoryItem)."""
    from django.contrib.auth.models import User as _User
    company         = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
    )
    article         = models.ForeignKey(
        'inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='bom_set', verbose_name=_('Artikl (gotov proizvod)'),
    )
    name            = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Naziv BOM-a'))
    yield_quantity  = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=3, default=1,
        verbose_name=_('Prinos (kom gotovog proizvoda)'),
    )
    is_active       = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivan'))
    notes           = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomene'))
    created_at      = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Sastavnica (BOM)')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Sastavnice (BOM)')
        ordering            = ['article__name']

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name or f'BOM — {self.article.name}'

    def total_material_cost(self):
        """Sum of (material.purchase_price * quantity) for all items."""
        from decimal import Decimal
        total = Decimal('0')
        for item in self.items.select_related('material').all():
            price = item.material.purchase_price if item.material else Decimal('0')
            waste_factor = Decimal('1') + item.waste_percent / Decimal('100')
            total += price * item.quantity * waste_factor
        return total.quantize(Decimal('0.01'))


class BOMItem(models.Model):
    """Jedna komponenta sastavnice."""
    bom             = models.ForeignKey(
        BOM, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='items', verbose_name=_('Sastavnica'),
    )
    material        = models.ForeignKey(
        'inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='used_in_boms', verbose_name=_('Materijal/Komponenta'),
    )
    quantity        = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=4, default=1,
        verbose_name=_('Količina'),
    )
    unit            = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='kom', verbose_name=_('JMJ'))
    waste_percent   = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Otpad (%)'),
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Stavka BOM-a')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke BOM-a')
        ordering            = ['id']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.material.name} × {self.quantity} {self.unit}'

    @property
    def net_quantity(self):
        """Quantity including waste."""
        from decimal import Decimal
        return self.quantity * (Decimal('1') + self.waste_percent / Decimal('100'))

=== ./marketplace/models.py ===
from decimal import Decimal
"""
marketplace/models.py - Grossmarkt Online tržnica

Povezuje OPG proizvođače s restoranima.
Jedan order može sadržavati proizvode od više OPG-ova.
"""
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class MarketplaceCategory(models.Model):
    """Kategorija proizvoda na tržnici (Povrće, Voće, Mliječni, Meso...)"""
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('Naziv'))
    slug = models.SlugField(unique=True)
    icon = models.CharField(max_length=10, default='🥬', verbose_name=_('Emoji'))
    parent = models.ForeignKey(
        'self', null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='children', verbose_name=_('Nadkategorija')
    )
    sort_order = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Kategorija tržnice')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Kategorije tržnice')
        ordering = ['sort_order', 'name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.icon} {self.name}"


class MarketplaceProduct(models.Model):
    """Proizvod na tržnici - vezan za OPG (seller_company) i njihov InventoryItem."""
    UNIT_CHOICES = [
        ('kg', 'Kilogram'),
        ('g', 'Gram'),
        ('l', 'Litra'),
        ('kom', 'Komad'),
        ('pakiranje', 'Pakiranje'),
        ('kutija', 'Kutija'),
        ('vreca', 'Vreća'),
    ]

    # Veza na OPG
    seller_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='marketplace_products',
        verbose_name=_('Prodavač (OPG)')
    )
    inventory_item = models.ForeignKey(
        'inventory.InventoryItem', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='marketplace_listings',
        verbose_name=_('Artikl iz skladišta')
    )

    # Osnovni podaci
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv proizvoda'))
    slug = models.SlugField(max_length=220, unique=True)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis'))
    category = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceCategory, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='products',
        verbose_name=_('Kategorija')
    )

    # Cijena i dostupnost
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Cijena'))
    unit = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=UNIT_CHOICES, default='kg', verbose_name=_('Jedinica'))
    min_order_qty = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=1, verbose_name=_('Min. narudžba'))
    available_qty = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Dostupna količina'))
    is_available = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Dostupno'))

    # Sezonalnost
    seasonal_from = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Sezona od'))
    seasonal_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Sezona do'))

    # Kvaliteta
    is_organic = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Ekološki'))
    is_eco_certified = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Eko certifikat'))
    origin_region = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Regija porijekla'))

    # Mediji
    image_main = models.ImageField(upload_to='marketplace/products/', blank=True, null=True, verbose_name=_('Glavna slika'))

    # Skladištenje
    shelf_life_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Rok trajanja (dana)'))
    storage_temp_min = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Min temp skladištenja °C'))
    storage_temp_max = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Max temp skladištenja °C'))
    requires_cold_chain = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Zahtijeva hladni lanac'))
    # Warehouse safety flags
    is_fragile = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_("Krhko (ne trpi tezinu)"))
    is_heavy = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_("Tesko (25+ kg)"))

    # Statistika
    is_featured = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Istaknuto'))
    sort_order = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    total_sold = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    avg_rating = models.DecimalField(max_digits=3, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    review_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)

    # Delivery pricing per product
    delivery_fee_per_unit = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_("Dostava po jedinici"),
        help_text=_("Cijena dostave po jedinici (kg/kom). 0 = besplatna."),
    )
    free_delivery_above = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_("Besplatna dostava iznad"),
        help_text=_("Besplatna dostava za narudzbe iznad ovog iznosa."),
    )
    pickup_available = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_("Osobno preuzimanje"))

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Proizvod na tržnici')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Proizvodi na tržnici')
        ordering = ['sort_order', '-created_at']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['seller_company', 'is_available']),
            models.Index(fields=['category', 'is_available']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} — {self.seller_company.name}"

    @property
    def is_in_season(self):
        if not self.seasonal_from and not self.seasonal_until:
            return True
        today = timezone.now().date()
        if self.seasonal_from and today < self.seasonal_from:
            return False
        if self.seasonal_until and today > self.seasonal_until:
            return False
        return True


class MarketplaceOrder(models.Model):
    """Narudžba restorana - može sadržavati proizvode od više OPG-ova."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Na čekanju')),
        ('confirmed', _('Potvrđena')),
        ('collecting', _('Prikupljanje')),
        ('in_transit', _('U dostavi')),
        ('delivered', _('Dostavljena')),
        ('completed', _('Završena')),
        ('cancelled', _('Otkazana')),
    ]

    order_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, editable=False)
    buyer_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='marketplace_orders',
        verbose_name=_('Kupac (restoran)')
    )
    buyer_user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Naručio')
    )

    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')

    # Financije
    subtotal = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    delivery_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=8, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    # Dostava
    delivery_date_requested = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Željeni datum dostave'))
    delivery_time_window = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Vremenski prozor'))
    delivery_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Adresa dostave'))
    delivery_notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomena za dostavu'))

    # Veze na delivery modul
    assigned_driver = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='assigned_deliveries',
        verbose_name=_('Vozač')
    )

    # Timestamps
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    confirmed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    delivered_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Recurring orders
    is_recurring = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_("Ponavljajuca narudzba"))
    recurring_interval = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, blank=True, default="",
        choices=[
            ("daily", _("Dnevno")),
            ("weekly", _("Tjedno")),
            ("biweekly", _("Svaka 2 tjedna")),
            ("monthly", _("Mjesecno")),
        ],
        verbose_name=_("Interval ponavljanja"),
    )
    recurring_source = models.ForeignKey(
        "self", on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name="recurring_copies",
        verbose_name=_("Izvorna ponavljajuca narudzba"),
    )
    next_recurring_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_("Sljedece ponavljanje"))

    # Veza na fakturu
    invoice = models.ForeignKey(
        'invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='marketplace_orders',
        verbose_name=_('Faktura')
    )

    # Smart Cart fields
    DELIVERY_METHOD_CHOICES = [
        ('standard', _('Standardna dostava')),
        ('express', _('Express dostava')),
        ('gepek_taxi', _('Gepek / Taxi — hitno')),
        ('pickup', _('Osobno preuzimanje')),
    ]
    delivery_method = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=DELIVERY_METHOD_CHOICES,
        default='standard', verbose_name=_('Nacin dostave')
    )
    requires_cold_chain = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Zahtijeva hladni lanac'))
    cutoff_acknowledged = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Cut-off potvrden'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Narudžba tržnice')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Narudžbe tržnice')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.order_number} — {self.buyer_company.name}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.order_number:
            year = timezone.now().year
            last = MarketplaceOrder.objects.filter(
                order_number__startswith=f"MKT-{year}-"
            ).count()
            self.order_number = f"MKT-{year}-{last + 1:05d}"
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def recalculate(self):
        self.subtotal = sum(i.total for i in self.items.all())
        self.total = self.subtotal + self.delivery_fee
        self.save(update_fields=['subtotal', 'total'])


class MarketplaceOrderItem(models.Model):
    """Stavka narudžbe - veže se na proizvod i na OPG."""
    SELLER_STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Čeka potvrdu')),
        ('confirmed', _('Potvrđeno')),
        ('preparing', _('U pripremi')),
        ('ready', _('Spremno')),
        ('collected', _('Prikupljeno')),
        ('delivered', _('Dostavljeno')),
        ('rejected', _('Odbijeno')),
    ]

    order = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceOrder, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='items', verbose_name=_('Narudžba')
    )
    product = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceProduct, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        verbose_name=_('Proizvod')
    )
    seller_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        verbose_name=_('Prodavač (OPG)')
    )

    quantity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Količina'))
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Jedinična cijena'))
    total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    seller_status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=SELLER_STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')

    # Traceability
    lot_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Broj šarže'))
    harvest_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Datum berbe'))
    expiry_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Rok trajanja'))

    seller_confirmed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    collected_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Warehouse: actual weighed quantity (dynamic pricing)
    actual_quantity = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=10, decimal_places=3, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Stvarna kolicina (vaganje)')
    )
    actual_total = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Stvarni iznos (nakon vaganja)')
    )
    packed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='packed_items',
        verbose_name=_('Pakirao')
    )
    packed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Pakirano'))

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Stavka narudžbe')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke narudžbe')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.product.name} × {self.quantity}"

    @property
    def final_quantity(self):
        return self.actual_quantity if self.actual_quantity is not None else self.quantity

    @property
    def final_total(self):
        return self.actual_total if self.actual_total is not None else self.total

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.total = self.quantity * self.unit_price
        if self.actual_quantity is not None:
            self.actual_total = self.actual_quantity * self.unit_price
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)


class SellerSubOrder(models.Model):
    """Sub-narudžba za jednog OPG-a iz veće narudžbe."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Čeka potvrdu')),
        ('confirmed', _('Potvrđeno')),
        ('ready', _('Spremno za preuzimanje')),
        ('collected', _('Preuzeto')),
        ('delivered', _('Dostavljeno')),
        ('rejected', _('Odbijeno')),
    ]

    marketplace_order = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceOrder, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='sub_orders', verbose_name=_('Glavna narudžba')
    )
    seller_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='seller_sub_orders',
        verbose_name=_('Prodavač (OPG)')
    )

    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')

    # Financije
    subtotal = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    commission_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=5.00, verbose_name=_('Provizija %'))
    commission_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    seller_payout = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Isplata prodavaču'))

    # Pickup
    pickup_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Adresa preuzimanja'))
    pickup_time_window = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True)
    collected_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    collected_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Preuzeo')
    )

    # Veza na otkupni blok
    purchase_document = models.ForeignKey(
        'invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='seller_suborders',
        verbose_name=_('Otkupni dokument')
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Sub-narudžba prodavača')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Sub-narudžbe prodavača')
        unique_together = ['marketplace_order', 'seller_company']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"SubOrder {self.seller_company.name} — {self.marketplace_order.order_number}"

    def recalculate(self):
        items = MarketplaceOrderItem.objects.filter(
            order=self.marketplace_order,
            seller_company=self.seller_company
        )
        self.subtotal = sum((i.total for i in items), Decimal("0"))
        self.commission_amount = self.subtotal * Decimal(str(self.commission_percent)) / Decimal("100")
        self.seller_payout = self.subtotal - self.commission_amount
        self.save(update_fields=['subtotal', 'commission_amount', 'seller_payout'])


# ── Discount / Rebate System ─────────────────────────────────────────

class PartnerDiscount(models.Model):
    """Tier-based discount between seller and buyer companies."""
    TIER_CHOICES = [
        ('standard', _('Standard (0%)')),
        ('bronze', _('Bronze (10%)')),
        ('silver', _('Silver (15%)')),
        ('gold', _('Gold (20%)')),
        ('platinum', _('Platinum (25%)')),
        ('custom', _('Custom')),
    ]

    seller_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='partner_discounts_given',
        verbose_name=_('Prodavac'),
    )
    buyer_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='partner_discounts_received',
        verbose_name=_('Kupac'),
    )
    tier = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TIER_CHOICES, default='standard',
                            verbose_name=_('Partnerski tier'))
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Rabat %'),
        help_text=_('Za tier!=custom, koristi se automatski postotak tiera.'),
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    valid_from = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    valid_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Partnerski rabat')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Partnerski rabati')
        unique_together = ['seller_company', 'buyer_company']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.seller_company.name} -> {self.buyer_company.name}: {self.tier}"


class CategoryDiscount(models.Model):
    """Discount per category, optionally targeted to specific buyer."""
    seller_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='category_discounts_given',
    )
    buyer_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='category_discounts_received',
        help_text=_('Prazno = vrijedi za sve kupce.'),
    )
    category = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceCategory, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        verbose_name=_('Kategorija'),
    )
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Rabat %'),
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    valid_from = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    valid_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Rabat po kategoriji')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Rabati po kategorijama')

        buyer = self.buyer_company.name if self.buyer_company else 'SVE'
        return f"{self.seller_company.name} -> {buyer}: {self.category.name} -{self.discount_percent}%"


class VolumeDiscount(models.Model):
    """Quantity-break discount. Stacks on top of partner/category discount."""
    seller_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='volume_discounts',
    )
    product = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceProduct, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='volume_discounts',
        help_text=_('Prazno = svi artikli.'),
    )
    category = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceCategory, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        null=True, blank=True,
        help_text=_('Prazno = sve kategorije.'),
    )
    min_quantity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2,
                                       verbose_name=_('Min. kolicina'))
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0,
        verbose_name=_('Rabat %'),
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Kolicinski rabat')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Kolicinski rabati')
        ordering = ['seller_company', 'min_quantity']

    def __str__(self):
        target = self.product.name if self.product else (self.category.name if self.category else 'SVE')
        return f"{self.seller_company.name}: {self.min_quantity}+ {target} -> -{self.discount_percent}%"


# ── Futures / Pre-Harvest Reservation System ──────────────────────────

class FuturesListing(models.Model):
    """Ponuda za buducu berbu/proizvodnju. OPG objavljuje sto ce imati dostupno."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', _('Nacrt')),
        ('open', _('Otvoreno za rezervacije')),
        ('fully_booked', _('Potpuno rezervirano')),
        ('harvesting', _('U berbi / pripremi')),
        ('delivering', _('Isporuka u tijeku')),
        ('completed', _('Zavrseno')),
        ('cancelled', _('Otkazano')),
    ]

    seller_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='futures_listings', verbose_name=_('Prodavac (OPG)'),
    )
    product = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceProduct, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='futures_listings',
        verbose_name=_('Povezani proizvod'),
    )
    category = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceCategory, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
    )

    name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv'))
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    unit = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='kg')
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Garantirana cijena'))
    total_capacity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Ukupni kapacitet'))
    reserved_qty = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Rezervirano'))
    min_order_qty = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=1, verbose_name=_('Min. narudzba'))

    available_from = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Dostupno od'))
    available_until = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Dostupno do'))
    booking_deadline = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Rok za rezervaciju'))

    deposit_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=20, verbose_name=_('Avans %'))
    origin_region = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Regija'))
    is_organic = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    requires_cold_chain = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='open')
    image = models.ImageField(upload_to='marketplace/futures/', blank=True, null=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Futures ponuda')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Futures ponude')
        ordering = ['available_from', '-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.seller_company.name}) — {self.available_from}"

    @property
    def available_qty(self):
        return self.total_capacity - self.reserved_qty

    @property
    def percent_reserved(self):
        if self.total_capacity == 0:
            return 0
        return int(self.reserved_qty / self.total_capacity * 100)

    @property
    def is_bookable(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        today = timezone.now().date()
        return (
            self.status == 'open'
            and today <= self.booking_deadline
            and self.available_qty > 0
        )


class FuturesReservation(models.Model):
    """Rezervacija buduce robe od strane kupca."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Ceka potvrdu')),
        ('confirmed', _('Potvrdeno')),
        ('deposit_paid', _('Avans placen')),
        ('ready', _('Roba spremna')),
        ('partially_delivered', _('Djelomicno isporuceno')),
        ('delivered', _('Isporuceno')),
        ('completed', _('Zavrseno')),
        ('cancelled', _('Otkazano')),
    ]
    DELIVERY_CHOICES = [
        ('delivery', _('Dostava')),
        ('pickup', _('Osobno preuzimanje')),
        ('bus', _('Bus linija')),
        ('courier', _('Kurir')),
    ]

    listing = models.ForeignKey(
        FuturesListing, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='reservations',
    )
    buyer_company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='futures_reservations',
    )
    buyer_user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
    )

    quantity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Kolicina'))
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Garantirana cijena'))
    total_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    deposit_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Avans'))
    deposit_paid = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Avans placen'))
    deposit_paid_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    delivery_preference = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=DELIVERY_CHOICES, default='delivery')
    delivery_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True)
    delivery_notes = models.TextField(blank=True)
    recurring_weekly = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Tjedna isporuka'))

    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')
    discount_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Rabat %'))

    confirmed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    delivered_qty = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Futures rezervacija')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Futures rezervacije')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.buyer_company.name}: {self.quantity} {self.listing.unit} {self.listing.name}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.total_price = self.quantity * self.unit_price * (1 - self.discount_percent / Decimal('100'))
        self.deposit_amount = self.total_price * self.listing.deposit_percent / Decimal('100')
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @property
    def remaining_qty(self):
        return self.quantity - self.delivered_qty

# ── Reklamacija / Complaint ─────────────────────────────────
class OrderComplaint(models.Model):
    """QR-based complaint — buyer scans QR on invoice, files a complaint."""
    REASON_CHOICES = [
        ('damaged', _('Osteceno')),
        ('wrong_item', _('Krivi artikl')),
        ('wrong_qty', _('Kriva kolicina')),
        ('expired', _('Istekao rok')),
        ('temp_breach', _('Prekid hladnog lanca')),
        ('quality', _('Losa kvaliteta')),
        ('other', _('Ostalo')),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('open', _('Otvorena')),
        ('acknowledged', _('Primljena')),
        ('replacement_sent', _('Zamjena poslana')),
        ('refunded', _('Povrat novca')),
        ('rejected', _('Odbijena')),
        ('closed', _('Zatvorena')),

    order = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceOrder, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='complaints', verbose_name=_('Narudzba')

        null=True, blank=True, related_name='complaints',
        verbose_name=_('Stavka')
    )
    reason = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=REASON_CHOICES, verbose_name=_('Razlog'))
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis problema'))
    photo = models.ImageField(upload_to='marketplace/complaints/', blank=True, null=True, verbose_name=_('Fotografija'))
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='open')
    resolution_note = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Biljeska o rjesenju'))

    # Auto-storno
    storno_invoice = models.ForeignKey(
        'invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='complaint_stornos',
        verbose_name=_('Storno racun')
    )
    replacement_order = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceOrder, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='replacement_for_complaints',
        verbose_name=_('Zamjenska narudzba')
    )

    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Prijavio')
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    resolved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Reklamacija')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Reklamacije')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Reklamacija #{self.pk} — {self.order.order_number} ({self.get_reason_display()})"


# ── WhatsApp Concierge: Cart Snapshot ──────────────────────
class CartSnapshot(models.Model):
    """
    Frozen cart state for WhatsApp/SMS share links.
    Security: token expires in 60min, cart_hash prevents race conditions.
    Scenario 2.1: Token expiration (403 after TTL).
    Scenario 2.2: cart_hash mismatch blocks stale payments.
    """
    token = models.CharField(max_length=64, unique=True, db_index=True)
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='cart_snapshots', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka kupca')
    )
    cart_data = models.JSONField(verbose_name=_('Snapshot kosrice'))
    cart_hash = models.CharField(max_length=64, verbose_name=_('SHA-256 hash stanja'))
    total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    # Security
    expires_at = models.DateTimeField(verbose_name=_('Istice'))
    is_used = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Iskoristen'))

    # Delivery
    delivery_address = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True)
    delivery_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='standard')
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)

    # Result
    order = models.ForeignKey(
        MarketplaceOrder, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name='from_snapshot',
        verbose_name=_('Kreirana narudzba')
    )

    # WA delivery status
    wa_message_id = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    wa_sent = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    wa_fallback_used = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)  # sms, email, none

    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Kreirao (dispeeer)')
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Cart Snapshot')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Cart Snapshots')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Snapshot {self.token[:8]}... -> {self.company.name}"

    @property
    def is_expired(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        return timezone.now() > self.expires_at

    @property
    def is_valid(self):
        return not self.is_expired and not self.is_used

    @staticmethod
    def generate_token():
        import secrets
        return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)

    @staticmethod
    def compute_hash(cart_data):
        import hashlib, json
        raw = json.dumps(cart_data, sort_keys=True, default=str)
        return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
