class GrossmarktDocument(models.Model):
    """Bazni model za procesne dokumente."""
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', _('Nacrt')),
        ('confirmed', _('Potvrđeno')),
        ('cancelled', _('Stornirano')),
    ]
    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey(Company, on_delete=models.CASCADE, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'))
    objects = CompanyManager()
    partner = models.ForeignKey(Partner, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Partner'))
    doc_number = models.CharField(max_length=255, db_index=True, verbose_name=_('Broj dokumenta'))
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft')
    date_issued = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Datum izdavanja'))
    date_confirmed = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Datum potvrde'))
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomena'))
    created_by = models.ForeignKey('auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='%(class)s_created', verbose_name=_('Kreirao'))
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        abstract = True
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def confirm(self):
        from django.utils import timezone as tz
        self.status = 'confirmed'
        self.date_confirmed = tz.now()
        self.save(update_fields=['status', 'date_confirmed', 'updated_at'])

    def cancel(self):
        self.status = 'cancelled'
        self.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

    @property
    def total(self):
        return sum(i.line_total for i in self.items.all())

    @property
    def total_vat(self):
        return sum(i.vat_amount for i in self.items.all())

    @property
    def total_with_vat(self):
        return self.total + self.total_vat


class GrossmarktDocItem(models.Model):
    """Bazna stavka za procesne dokumente."""
    product_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naziv'))
    quantity = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Količina'))
    unit = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='kg', verbose_name=_('Jed. mjere'))
    unit_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name=_('Jed. cijena'))
    vat_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=25, verbose_name=_('PDV %'))
    discount_pct = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Popust %'))

    class Meta:
        abstract = True

    @property
    def line_total(self):
        base = float(self.quantity * self.unit_price)
        if self.discount_pct:
            base -= base * float(self.discount_pct) / 100
        return round(base, 2)

    @property
    def vat_amount(self):
        return round(self.line_total * float(self.vat_rate) / 100, 2)

    @property
    def line_total_with_vat(self):
        return round(self.line_total + self.vat_amount, 2)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.product_name} x {self.quantity} {self.unit}"


class Otpremnica(GrossmarktDocument):
    delivery_address = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Adresa dostave'))
    vehicle_plate = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Reg. oznaka'))
    driver_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Vozač'))

    class Meta(GrossmarktDocument.Meta):
        verbose_name = _('Otpremnica')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Otpremnice')
    def __str__(self):
        return f"OTP-{self.doc_number}"

class OtpremnicaItem(GrossmarktDocItem):
    document = models.ForeignKey(Otpremnica, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='items')
    batch_number = models.CharField(
        max_length=50, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('LOT / Šarža'),
        help_text='Npr. LOT-20261015 — iz kojeg prešanja/berbe je roba',
    )
    expiry_batch = models.ForeignKey(
        'pos.ExpiryBatch', on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='otpremnica_items',
        verbose_name=_('Veza na LOT (ExpiryBatch)'),
    )
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Stavka otpremnice')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Stavke otpremnice')

# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# ArhivaEntry — Centralizirana arhiva svih dokumenata
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class ArhivaEntry(models.Model):
    """Centralni registar svih dokumenata u sustavu — denormalizirani metapodaci za brzu pretragu."""
    SOURCE_TYPES = [
        ('invoice_out',   _('Izlazni račun')),
        ('invoice_in',    _('Ulazni račun (smart import)')),
        ('eracun',        _('e-Račun (FINA/XML)')),
        ('document',      _('Dokument (ručni upload)')),
        ('fiscal_cert',   _('FINA certifikat')),
        ('otpremnica',    _('Otpremnica')),
        ('primka',        _('Primka')),
        ('otkupni_blok',  _('Otkupni blok')),
        ('cmr',           _('CMR')),
        ('pos_receipt',   _('POS račun')),
    ]

    company = models.ForeignKey(Company, on_delete=models.CASCADE, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'))
    objects = CompanyManager()

    source_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=SOURCE_TYPES, verbose_name=_('Tip izvora'))
    source_id = models.CharField(max_length=255, verbose_name=_('ID izvornog objekta'))
    archive_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, blank=True, default='', db_index=True, verbose_name=_('Evidencijski broj'))

    # Denormalizirani metapodaci za pretragu
    doc_number = models.CharField(max_length=255, db_index=True, verbose_name=_('Broj dokumenta'))
    partner_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Partner'))
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=14, decimal_places=2, default=0, verbose_name=_('Iznos'))
    currency = models.CharField(max_length=3, default='EUR', verbose_name=_('Valuta'))
    doc_date = models.DateField(verbose_name=_('Datum dokumenta'))

    # Fiskalni podaci (ako postoje)
    zki = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, verbose_name=_('ZKI'))
    jir = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, verbose_name=_('JIR'))

    # File reference
    file_path = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Putanja datoteke'))
    has_xml = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Ima XML'))
    has_pdf = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Ima PDF'))
    has_image = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Ima sliku'))

    # Audit
    archived_by = models.ForeignKey('auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Arhivirao'))
    archived_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name=_('Datum arhiviranja'))
    session_key = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, db_index=True, verbose_name=_('Ključ sesije'))

    # Integritet i zaštita
    integrity_hash = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, db_index=True, verbose_name=_('Integritet hash'))
    deletion_protected_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Zaštićeno do'))

    # AI Bookkeeper
    is_booked = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_("Proknjiženo"))
    ai_proposal = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name=_("AI prijedlog knjiženja"))

    # Barcode
    barcode = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, db_index=True,
                               verbose_name=_('Barcode'),
                               help_text='Interni barcode za fizičko mapiranje dokumenta')

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Arhivski zapis')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Arhivski zapisi')
        ordering = ['-archived_at']
        constraints = []  # Deep duplicate check handled in save()
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['company', 'source_type'], name='arhiva_co_src_idx'),
            models.Index(fields=['company', 'doc_date'], name='arhiva_co_date_idx'),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_source_type_display()} — {self.doc_number}"

    def compute_integrity_hash(self):
        """Deterministicki SHA-256 od 6 kljucnih polja."""
        import hashlib
        data = '|'.join([
            str(self.source_type or ''),
            str(self.source_id or ''),
            str(self.doc_number or ''),
            str(self.amount or 0),
            str(self.doc_date or ''),
            str(self.company_id or ''),
        ])
        return hashlib.sha256(data.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()

    def set_deletion_protection(self):
        """11 godina od doc_date — HR zakonski rok cuvanja."""
        from datetime import timedelta
        if self.doc_date:
            self.deletion_protected_until = self.doc_date + timedelta(days=11 * 365)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # Deep duplicate check — ALL criteria must match
        if not self.pk and self.doc_number:
            qs = ArhivaEntry.objects.unscoped().filter(
                company_id=self.company_id,
                doc_number=self.doc_number,
                source_type=self.source_type,
            )
            # Add amount check (within 0.01 tolerance)
            if self.amount:
                from decimal import Decimal
                tolerance = Decimal('0.01')
                qs = qs.filter(
                    amount__gte=self.amount - tolerance,
                    amount__lte=self.amount + tolerance,
                )
            # Add date check
            if self.doc_date:
                qs = qs.filter(doc_date=self.doc_date)
            # Add partner check (first 20 chars, case-insensitive)
            if self.partner_name:
                qs = qs.filter(partner_name__istartswith=self.partner_name[:20])
            if qs.exists():
                return  # Skip true duplicate — original already archived
        if not self.archive_number:
            self._assign_archive_number()
        if not self.integrity_hash:
            self.integrity_hash = self.compute_integrity_hash()
        if not self.deletion_protected_until:
            self.set_deletion_protection()
        if not self.barcode:
            self._generate_barcode()
        # Retry on archive_number collision — each attempt in its own SAVEPOINT
        # to avoid PostgreSQL broken transaction state inside @atomic blocks
        from django.db import IntegrityError, transaction
        for attempt in range(5):
            try:
                sid = transaction.savepoint()
                super().save(*args, **kwargs)
                transaction.savepoint_commit(sid)
                return
            except IntegrityError as e:
                transaction.savepoint_rollback(sid)
                err_str = str(e)
                if 'arhiva_unique_doc_per_company' in err_str or 'duplicate' in err_str.lower():
                    return  # Duplicate entry — skip silently
                elif 'archnum_uniq' in err_str or 'archive_number' in err_str:
                    self.archive_number = ''
                    self._assign_archive_number()
                else:
                    raise
        # Final attempt without catching
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def _generate_barcode(self):
        import random
        import string as _string
        from datetime import date
        prefix = (self.company.name[:4].upper().replace(' ', '') if self.company_id else 'LCNK')
        rand = ''.join(random.choices(_string.digits, k=6))
        self.barcode = f"LC-{date.today().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{prefix}-{rand}"

    def _assign_archive_number(self):
        from datetime import date as _d
        yr = _d.today().year
        pfx = 'ARH-%d/' % yr
        last_nums = []
        for an in ArhivaEntry.objects.filter(
            archive_number__startswith=pfx
        ).values_list('archive_number', flat=True):
            try:
                last_nums.append(int(an.split('/')[-1]))
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                pass
        seq = (max(last_nums) + 1) if last_nums else 1
        self.archive_number = '%s%05d' % (pfx, seq)

    @property
    def is_deletion_protected(self):
        from django.utils import timezone
        if not self.deletion_protected_until:
            return False
        return timezone.now().date() < self.deletion_protected_until

# DocumentBlob — Binary storage u PostgreSQL
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class DocumentBlob(models.Model):
    """Sprema datoteke direktno u bazu (bytea). Max 500KB po fajlu."""
    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey(Company, on_delete=models.CASCADE, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'))
    filename = models.CharField(max_length=255, verbose_name=_('Naziv datoteke'))
    content_type = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('MIME tip'))
    file_data = models.BinaryField(verbose_name=_('Podaci'))
    file_size = models.IntegerField(verbose_name=_('Velicina (bytes)'))
    file_hash = models.CharField(max_length=64, db_index=True, verbose_name=_('SHA-256'))
    source_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Tip izvora'))
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Document Blob')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Document Blobs')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.filename} ({self.file_size // 1024}KB)"

class StorageConfig(models.Model):
    """Konfiguracija storage backenda per-company."""
    BACKEND_CHOICES = [
        ('local', _('Lokalni disk')),
        ('cloud', _('Cloud (B2/S3)')),
        ('database', _('Baza podataka')),
    ]
    company = models.OneToOneField(Company, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='storage_config', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'))
    backend = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=BACKEND_CHOICES, default='local', verbose_name=_('Storage backend'))
    max_file_size_mb = models.IntegerField(default=10, verbose_name=_('Max velicina datoteke (MB)'))
    auto_compress_images = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Auto kompresija slika'))
    cloud_bucket = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Cloud bucket'))
    cloud_prefix = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Cloud prefix'))
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Storage Konfiguracija')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Storage Konfiguracije')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.company} — {self.get_backend_display()}"


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# ArhivaBookmark — Korisničke oznake na arhivskim zapisima
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

from django.conf import settings as _settings

class ArhivaBookmark(models.Model):
    user = models.ForeignKey(_settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    entry = models.ForeignKey('ArhivaEntry', on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='bookmarks')
    note = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = ('user', 'entry')
        verbose_name = _('Arhivska oznaka')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Arhivske oznake')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Bookmark: {self.user} → {self.entry}"


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════


# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# ConversionRecord — Dokaz o konverziji slike u XML
# Za regulatornu uskladenost (kazna 5000 EUR za izgubljeni racun)
# ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class ConversionRecord(models.Model):
    """
    Audit trail za konverziju: Slika ulaznog racuna -> strukturirani XML.
    Svaki zapis dokazuje da je XML vjerna reprodukcija originala.
    """
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Na cekanju')),
        ('extracted', _('Ekstrahirano')),
        ('xml_generated', _('XML generiran')),
        ('validated', _('Validirano')),
        ('failed', _('Neuspjelo')),
    ]
    CONFIDENCE_CHOICES = [
        ('high', _('Visoko (>90%)')),
        ('medium', _('Srednje (70-90%)')),
        ('low', _('Nisko (<70%)')),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey(Company, on_delete=models.CASCADE, verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'))
    arhiva_entry = models.ForeignKey('ArhivaEntry', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
                                      related_name='conversions', verbose_name=_('Arhivski zapis'))

    # Originalna datoteka
    original_filename = models.CharField(max_length=255, verbose_name=_('Originalni naziv'))
    original_size = models.IntegerField(verbose_name=_('Originalna velicina (bytes)'))
    original_hash = models.CharField(max_length=128, verbose_name=_('SHA-256 originala'))
    original_content_type = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name=_('MIME tip originala'))
    original_path = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Putanja originala'))

    # AI ekstrakcija
    ai_model = models.CharField(max_length=200, default='gemini-2.5-flash', verbose_name=_('AI model'),
                                  help_text='Override via settings.CONVERSION_AI_MODEL')
    ai_prompt_version = models.CharField(max_length=50, default='v1.0', verbose_name=_('Verzija prompta'))
    ai_confidence = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=CONFIDENCE_CHOICES, default='medium',
                                      verbose_name=_('Pouzdanost AI'))
    ai_confidence_score = models.FloatField(default=0.0, verbose_name=_('Tocnost (0-1)'))
    ai_response_tokens = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Tokeni odgovora'))
    ai_cost_eur = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=4, default=0, verbose_name=_('Cijena (EUR)'))

    # Ekstrahirani podaci (JSON snapshot)
    extracted_data = models.JSONField(default=dict, verbose_name=_('Ekstrahirani podaci'))

    # Generirani XML
    xml_content = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Generirani XML'))
    xml_hash = models.CharField(max_length=128, blank=True, verbose_name=_('SHA-256 XML-a'))
    xml_size = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Velicina XML (bytes)'))
    xml_path = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Putanja XML-a'))

    # Validacija
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending', verbose_name=_('Status'))
    validation_errors = models.JSONField(default=list, verbose_name=_('Greske validacije'))
    is_valid = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Validirano'))

    # Usporedba: dokaz vjernosti
    size_reduction_pct = models.FloatField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Smanjenje velicine (%)'))
    fields_extracted = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Broj ekstrahiranih polja'))
    items_extracted = models.IntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Broj stavki'))

    # Audit
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(_settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
                                    verbose_name=_('Kreirao'))
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    completed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = _('Konverzijski zapis')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Konverzijski zapisi')
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['company', 'status'], name='conv_co_status_idx'),
            models.Index(fields=['original_hash'], name='conv_orig_hash_idx'),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Konverzija: {self.original_filename} ({self.get_status_display()})"

    @property
    def savings_display(self):
        if self.original_size and self.xml_size:
            return f"{self.original_size // 1024}KB -> {self.xml_size // 1024}KB ({self.size_reduction_pct:.0f}%)"
        return "-"

=== ./helpdesk/models.py ===
import uuid
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.utils import timezone
from core.managers import CompanyManager

class Ticket(models.Model):
    CATEGORY_CHOICES = [
        ('delivery', _('Dostava')), ('quality', _('Kvaliteta robe')),
        ('invoice', _('Racun')), ('pos', _('POS/Blagajna')),
        ('technical', _('Tehnicki problem')), ('complaint', _('Reklamacija')),
        ('feature', _('Zahtjev')), ('other', _('Ostalo')),
    ]
    PRIORITY_CHOICES = [
        ('low', _('Nizak')), ('normal', _('Normalan')),
        ('high', _('Visok')), ('urgent', _('Hitno')), ('critical', _('Kriticno')),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('open', _('Otvoren')), ('in_progress', _('U obradi')),
        ('waiting_client', _('Ceka klijenta')), ('waiting_internal', _('Ceka interni tim')),
        ('resolved', _('Rijesen')), ('closed', _('Zatvoren')),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    objects = CompanyManager()
    ticket_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
    subject = models.CharField(max_length=300)
    description = models.TextField()
    category = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CATEGORY_CHOICES, default='other')
    priority = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=PRIORITY_CHOICES, default='normal')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='open')
    source = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=[
        ('portal', 'Portal'), ('email', 'Email'), ('phone', 'Telefon'),
        ('whatsapp', 'WhatsApp'), ('internal', 'Interni'),
    ], default='internal')
    client = models.ForeignKey('partners.Partner', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='tickets')
    contact_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default='')
    contact_email = models.CharField(max_length=254, blank=True, default='')
    contact_phone = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default='')
    related_invoice = models.ForeignKey('invoicing.Invoice', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='tickets')
    assigned_to = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='assigned_tickets')
    sla_deadline = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    first_response_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    resolved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    closed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    satisfaction_rating = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    satisfaction_comment = models.TextField(blank=True, default='')
    portal_token = models.UUIDField(default=uuid.uuid4, unique=True)
    tags = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True, default='')
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='tickets_created')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Tiket'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Tiketi'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"#{self.ticket_number} - {self.subject}"

    def generate_ticket_number(self):
        year = timezone.now().year
        last = Ticket.objects.filter(ticket_number__startswith=f'TK-{year}-').order_by('-ticket_number').first()
        seq = 1
        if last:
            try: seq = int(last.ticket_number.split('-')[-1]) + 1
            except Exception: pass
        return f'TK-{year}-{seq:04d}'

    @property
    def is_overdue(self):
        if self.sla_deadline and self.status not in ('resolved', 'closed'):
            return timezone.now() > self.sla_deadline
        return False

    def set_sla(self):
        hours_map = {'critical': 2, 'urgent': 4, 'high': 8, 'normal': 24, 'low': 72}
        hours = hours_map.get(self.priority, 24)
        self.sla_deadline = timezone.now() + timezone.timedelta(hours=hours)


class TicketMessage(models.Model):
    ticket = models.ForeignKey(Ticket, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='messages')
    sender = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    sender_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default='')
    is_internal = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_from_client = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    body = models.TextField()
    attachment = models.FileField(upload_to='helpdesk/attachments/%Y/%m/', null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['created_at']


class TicketTemplate(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    objects = CompanyManager()
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    category = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Ticket.CATEGORY_CHOICES, default='other')
    body = models.TextField()
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class VIPContact(models.Model):
    """WhatsApp VIP kontakti — automatski prioritet i routing za helpdesk tikete."""
    PRIORITY_CHOICES = Ticket.PRIORITY_CHOICES

    phone = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, help_text='Bez + i razmaka, npr. 385976091655')
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    note = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True, default='')
    priority = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=PRIORITY_CHOICES, default='critical')
    auto_assign_to = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        help_text='Automatski dodijeli tiket ovom korisniku'
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'VIP kontakt'
        verbose_name_plural = 'VIP kontakti'
        ordering = ['name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.name} (+{self.phone}) — {self.get_priority_display()}'

=== ./superadmin_ai/models.py ===
import uuid
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _


class AIModulePermission(models.Model):
    """
    Definira razinu AI pristupa po modulu, po firmi.
    Granularne dozvole: can_read, can_write, can_create, can_modify.
    access_level se automatski izračunava iz booleana za gate kompatibilnost.
    """
    ACCESS_DISABLED = 0
    ACCESS_READ_ONLY = 1
    ACCESS_ASSISTED = 2
    ACCESS_AUTONOMOUS = 3

    ACCESS_LEVELS = [
        (ACCESS_DISABLED, _('Isključeno')),
        (ACCESS_READ_ONLY, _('Samo čitanje')),
        (ACCESS_ASSISTED, _('Asistiran (odobrenje)')),
        (ACCESS_AUTONOMOUS, _('Autonoman')),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='ai_permissions', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
        null=True, blank=True,
        help_text=_('Prazno = globalno (sve tvrtke)')
    )
    module = models.CharField(
        max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Modul')
    )
    # Primarni naziv za prikaz u UI
    module_name = property(lambda self: self.module)

    # ── Granularne dozvole ──
    can_read = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Čitanje'))
    can_write = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Pisanje'))
    can_create = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Kreiranje'))
    can_modify = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Izmjene'))

    # ── Kompatibilnost s gate sustavom ──
    access_level = models.IntegerField(
        choices=ACCESS_LEVELS, default=ACCESS_DISABLED,
        verbose_name=_('Razina pristupa')
    )

    updated_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Ažurirao')
    )
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Napomena'))

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'ai_module_permission'
        unique_together = ('company', 'module')
        verbose_name = _('AI dozvola modula')
        verbose_name_plural = _('AI dozvole modula')
        ordering = ['module']

    def __str__(self):
        company_name = self.company.name if self.company else 'GLOBALNO'
        perms = []
        if self.can_read: perms.append('R')
        if self.can_write: perms.append('W')
        if self.can_create: perms.append('C')
        if self.can_modify: perms.append('M')
        return f"{company_name} → {self.module} [{'/'.join(perms) or 'OFF'}]"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        """Auto-sync: compute access_level iz booleana za gate kompatibilnost."""
        # Compute access_level
        if self.can_modify or self.can_create:
            if self.can_write:
                self.access_level = self.ACCESS_AUTONOMOUS
            else:
                self.access_level = self.ACCESS_ASSISTED
        elif self.can_write:
            self.access_level = self.ACCESS_ASSISTED
        elif self.can_read:
            self.access_level = self.ACCESS_READ_ONLY
        else:
            self.access_level = self.ACCESS_DISABLED
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    @classmethod
    def get_level(cls, company, module):
        """Vrati razinu pristupa za modul. Provjeri firmu, pa globalno, pa DISABLED."""
        try:
            return cls.objects.get(company=company, module=module).access_level
        except cls.DoesNotExist:
            pass
        try:
            return cls.objects.get(company=None, module=module).access_level
        except cls.DoesNotExist:
            return cls.ACCESS_DISABLED

    @classmethod
    def get_perm(cls, company, module):
        """Vrati permission objekt ili None."""
        try:
            return cls.objects.get(company=company, module=module)
        except cls.DoesNotExist:
            try:
                return cls.objects.get(company=None, module=module)
            except cls.DoesNotExist:
                return None


class PendingAIAction(models.Model):
    """
    Za ASSISTED razinu — AI kreira prijedlog, superadmin/company_admin odobrava.
    """
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('pending', _('Čeka odobrenje')),
        ('approved', _('Odobreno')),
        ('rejected', _('Odbijeno')),
        ('executed', _('Izvršeno')),
        ('expired', _('Isteklo')),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='pending_ai_actions', verbose_name=_('Tvrtka'),
        null=True, blank=True,
    )
    module = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Modul'))
    action_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Tip akcije'),
        help_text=_('npr. create, update, delete, send')
    )
    description = models.TextField(verbose_name=_('Opis akcije'))
    proposed_code = models.TextField(blank=True, default='', verbose_name=_('Predloženi kod'))
    payload = models.JSONField(
        default=dict, verbose_name=_('Podaci'),
        help_text=_('JSON s detaljima akcije za izvršenje')
    )
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending',
        verbose_name=_('Status')
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    reviewed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name='reviewed_ai_actions', verbose_name=_('Pregledao')
    )
    reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    review_note = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Komentar'))
    result = models.JSONField(
        default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Rezultat izvršenja')
    )

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'ai_pending_action'
        verbose_name = _('AI čekajuća akcija')
        verbose_name_plural = _('AI čekajuće akcije')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.status}] {self.module}.{self.action_type}"


class AIActionLog(models.Model):
    """Audit trail svakog AI poziva — tko, što, kada, rezultat."""
    OUTCOME_CHOICES = [
        ('allowed', _('Dopušteno')),
        ('blocked', _('Blokirano')),
        ('queued', _('U redu čekanja')),
        ('executed', _('Izvršeno')),
        ('failed', _('Neuspješno')),
    ]

    id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4, editable=False)
    timestamp = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    company = models.ForeignKey(
        'companies.Company', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Tvrtka')
    )
    module = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Modul'))
    action_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Tip'))
    access_level = models.IntegerField(
        choices=AIModulePermission.ACCESS_LEVELS, verbose_name=_('Razina')
    )
    outcome = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=OUTCOME_CHOICES, verbose_name=_('Ishod')
    )
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        verbose_name=_('Korisnik')
    )
    details = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Detalji'))
    ip_address = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'ai_action_log'
        verbose_name = _('AI log')
        verbose_name_plural = _('AI logovi')
        ordering = ['-timestamp']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.timestamp:%d.%m %H:%M} | {self.module}.{self.action_type} → {self.outcome}"

=== ./maintenance/models.py ===
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils.translation import gettext_lazy as _
from django.utils import timezone
from core.managers import CompanyManager

class Asset(models.Model):
    CATEGORY_CHOICES = [
        ('vehicle', _('Vozilo')), ('equipment', _('Oprema')), ('it', _('IT oprema')),
        ('pos_device', _('POS uredjaj')), ('cold_chain', _('Hladnjaca/Termobox')),
        ('furniture', _('Namjestaj')), ('tool', _('Alat')), ('other', _('Ostalo')),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('active', _('Aktivno')), ('maintenance', _('Na servisu')),
        ('retired', _('Otpisano')), ('sold', _('Prodano')),
    ]
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True)
    objects = CompanyManager()
    name = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    asset_code = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True)
    category = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=CATEGORY_CHOICES, default='equipment')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='active')
    make = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default='')
    model_name = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default='')
    serial_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default='')
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, default='')
    location = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default='')
    photo = models.ImageField(upload_to='assets/', null=True, blank=True)
    registration_plate = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, default='')
    vin = models.CharField(max_length=17, blank=True, default='')
    mileage = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    fuel_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, default='')
    purchase_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    purchase_price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    current_value = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    depreciation_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    warranty_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    insurance_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    insurance_policy = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default='')
    registration_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    leasing_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    assigned_to = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='assigned_assets')
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='assets_created')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    depreciation_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=[('linear', 'Linearna'), ('declining', 'Degresivna')], default='linear')
    useful_life_months = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=60)
    salvage_value = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    last_depreciation_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    accumulated_depreciation = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Imovina'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Imovina'
        ordering = ['name']
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.asset_code} - {self.name}"

    def generate_asset_code(self):
        prefix = self.category[:3].upper() if self.category else 'AST'
        last = Asset.objects.filter(asset_code__startswith=prefix).order_by('-asset_code').first()
        seq = 1
        if last:
            try: seq = int(last.asset_code.split('-')[-1]) + 1
            except Exception: pass
        return f'{prefix}-{seq:04d}'


class MaintenanceSchedule(models.Model):
    FREQUENCY_CHOICES = [
        ('weekly', _('Tjedno')), ('monthly', _('Mjesecno')),
        ('quarterly', _('Kvartalno')), ('semi_annual', _('Polugodisnje')),
        ('annual', _('Godisnje')), ('mileage', _('Po kilometrazi')),
        ('once', _('Jednokratno')),
    ]
    asset = models.ForeignKey(Asset, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='schedules')
    task_name = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, default='')
    frequency = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=FREQUENCY_CHOICES, default='monthly')
    next_due = models.DateField()
    mileage_interval = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    estimated_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    service_provider = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default='')
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    notify_days_before = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=7)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['next_due']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.task_name} -> {self.asset.name}"

    @property
    def is_overdue(self):
        return self.next_due < timezone.now().date()

    def advance_schedule(self):
        from datetime import timedelta
        freq_map = {
            'weekly': timedelta(weeks=1), 'monthly': timedelta(days=30),
            'quarterly': timedelta(days=91), 'semi_annual': timedelta(days=182),
            'annual': timedelta(days=365),
        }
        delta = freq_map.get(self.frequency)
        if delta:
            self.next_due = timezone.now().date() + delta
            self.save(update_fields=['next_due'])


class MaintenanceLog(models.Model):
    TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ('preventive', _('Preventivno')), ('corrective', _('Korektivno')),
        ('inspection', _('Inspekcija')), ('calibration', _('Kalibracija')),
        ('repair', _('Popravak')),
    ]
    asset = models.ForeignKey(Asset, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='logs')
    schedule = models.ForeignKey(MaintenanceSchedule, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    maintenance_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=TYPE_CHOICES, default='preventive')
    title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, default='')
    date_performed = models.DateField(default=timezone.now)
    labor_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    parts_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    total_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)
    mileage_at_service = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    service_provider = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, default='')
    invoice_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, default='')
    performed_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='maintenance_performed')
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='maintenance_logged')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-date_performed']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.total_cost = self.labor_cost + self.parts_cost
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)
        if self.mileage_at_service and self.mileage_at_service > self.asset.mileage:
            self.asset.mileage = self.mileage_at_service
            self.asset.save(update_fields=['mileage'])
        if self.schedule:
            self.schedule.advance_schedule()


class DepreciationLog(models.Model):
    company = models.ForeignKey('companies.Company', on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='depreciation_logs')
    asset = models.ForeignKey(Asset, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='depreciation_logs')
    year = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    journal_entry_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        unique_together = [['asset', 'year', 'month']]
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.asset.asset_code} {self.year}-{self.month:02d} {self.amount}"

class HitnaIntervencija(models.Model):
    """
    Zahtjev za hitnu intervenciju (Notfall) s javne stranice viskovic-group.de
    """
    VRSTA_CHOICES = [
        ('voda',      'Voda / Poplave'),
        ('elektrika', 'Elektrika'),
        ('grijanje',  'Grijanje / Klima'),
        ('fasada',    'Fasada / Krov'),
        ('ostalo',    'Ostalo'),
    ]
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('novo',       'Novi zahtjev'),
        ('kontaktiran','Kontaktiran'),
        ('u_radu',     'U radu'),
        ('zavrseno',   'Završeno'),
        ('otkazano',   'Otkazano'),
    ]

    # Podaci podnositelja
    ime         = models.CharField(max_length=120, verbose_name=_('Ime i prezime'))
    telefon     = models.CharField(max_length=50, verbose_name=_('Telefon'))
    email       = models.EmailField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('E-mail'))
    adresa      = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Adresa'))
    grad        = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Grad'))
    drzava      = models.CharField(max_length=10, default='DE', verbose_name=_('Država'))

    # Problem
    vrsta       = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=VRSTA_CHOICES, default='ostalo',
                                   verbose_name=_('Vrsta intervencije'))
    opis        = models.TextField(verbose_name=_('Opis problema'))
    hitnost     = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Hitan slučaj (odmah)'))

    # Interni status
    status      = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='novo',
                                   verbose_name=_('Status'))
    napomena    = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Interna napomena'))
    dodijeljeno = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        related_name='hitne_intervencije', verbose_name=_('Dodijeljeno')
    )

    # Metapodaci
    ip_adresa   = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)
    jezik       = models.CharField(max_length=5, default='de', verbose_name=_('Jezik'))
    created_at  = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at  = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Hitna intervencija')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Hitne intervencije')
        ordering            = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.ime} — {self.get_vrsta_display()} ({self.created_at:%d.%m.%Y %H:%M})'


# ── Video galerija ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class VideoGallery(models.Model):
    """
    Video galerija za viskovic-group.de.
    Podržava YouTube/Vimeo embed i direktni upload .mp4
    """
    KATEGORIJA_CHOICES = [
        ('bau',       'Visković BAU'),
        ('wildberg',  'Wildberg'),
        ('najam',     'VG Najam'),
        ('webshop',   'VG Shop'),
        ('projekt',   'Projekt reference'),
        ('tim',       'Tim / Ekipa'),
        ('ostalo',    'Ostalo'),
    ]

    naslov_hr   = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name=_('Naslov (HR)'))
    naslov_de   = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Naslov (DE)'))
    naslov_en   = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Naslov (EN)'))
    opis_hr     = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis (HR)'))
    opis_de     = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis (DE)'))
    opis_en     = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('Opis (EN)'))

    # Video izvor — embed URL ili upload
    embed_url   = models.URLField(blank=True, verbose_name=_('YouTube / Vimeo URL'),
                                  help_text=_('Npr. https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=...'))
    video_file  = models.FileField(upload_to='website/videos/', null=True, blank=True,
                                   verbose_name=_('Video datoteka (.mp4)'))
    thumbnail   = models.ImageField(upload_to='website/video_thumbs/', null=True, blank=True,
                                    verbose_name=_('Thumbnail'))

    kategorija  = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=KATEGORIJA_CHOICES, default='ostalo',
                                   verbose_name=_('Kategorija'))
    trajanje    = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name=_('Trajanje'),
                                   help_text=_('Npr. 2:34'))
    redoslijed  = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0, verbose_name=_('Redoslijed'))
    is_active   = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name=_('Aktivno'))
    is_featured = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name=_('Istaknuto'))
    created_at  = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = _('Video')
        verbose_name_plural = _('Video galerija')
        ordering            = ['redoslijed', '-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return self.naslov_hr or self.naslov_de or f'Video {self.id}'

    @property
    def embed_id(self):
        """Izvuče YouTube video ID iz URL-a"""
        import re
        if not self.embed_url:
            return None
        m = re.search(r'(?:v=|youtu\.be/)([\w-]{11})', self.embed_url)
        return m.group(1) if m else None

    @property
    def is_youtube(self):
        return 'youtube' in (self.embed_url or '') or 'youtu.be' in (self.embed_url or '')

    @property
    def is_vimeo(self):
        return 'vimeo' in (self.embed_url or '')

    @property
    def embed_src(self):
        """Vraća src za <iframe>"""
        if self.is_youtube and self.embed_id:
            return f'https://www.youtube.com/embed/{self.embed_id}'
        if self.is_vimeo:
            import re
            m = re.search(r'vimeo\.com/(\d+)', self.embed_url)
            if m:
                return f'https://player.vimeo.com/video/{m.group(1)}'
        return None

    phone_1_name = models.CharField(max_length=50, default="Dragan", verbose_name="Kontakt 1 Ime")
    phone_1_number = models.CharField(max_length=30, default="+4917684397173", verbose_name="Kontakt 1 Broj")
    phone_2_name = models.CharField(max_length=50, default="Nikša", verbose_name="Kontakt 2 Ime")
    phone_2_number = models.CharField(max_length=30, default="+4915234209056", verbose_name="Kontakt 2 Broj")
    email = models.EmailField(default="viskovic.bau@gmail.com", verbose_name="Glavni E-mail")
